We evaluate two context biasing methods based on Whisper against three speech LLMs across read and non-read speech, reporting biased, unbiased, and overall word error rate (WER). The context biasing methods cut biased WER by up to 88% relative while leaving other words largely unaffected. Speech LLMs excel on read speech but generalize less well to non-read speech, and prove sensitive to distractor count and prompt word order. We characterize the resulting trade-offs to guide method selection.

</details>


### [38] [Task-Conditional Flow Matching for Balanced Multilingual Text Embedding Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05785)
*Tirth Bhatt,Naren Kumar S,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务条件流匹配（TCFM）框架，用于多语言文本嵌入模型的适配。核心思想是不同任务需要不同的优化策略：翻译任务使用Flow Matching，而检索、分类和配对分类任务使用与其学习动态更匹配的目标函数。此外，TCFM结合教师引导的表示保留和三阶段课程学习实现稳定适配，在Indic Massive Text Embedding Benchmark上达到新的最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言文本嵌入模型通常对多样任务使用单一训练目标进行适配，但不同任务本质上需要不同的优化策略，导致适配效果不佳。

Method: TCFM框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 对翻译任务选择性应用Flow Matching；(2) 对检索、分类和配对分类任务使用与各自学习动态更匹配的优化目标；(3) 结合教师引导的表示保留与三阶段课程学习以确保稳定适配。

Result: 在Indic Massive Text Embedding Benchmark上，TCFM取得了新的最先进结果，在多种多语言任务中持续提升嵌入质量，并能泛化到不同的嵌入模型家族。

Conclusion: TCFM证明了任务条件化的训练策略在多语言嵌入适配中的有效性，并计划在论文接收后公开发布代码和数据集。

Abstract: Multilingual text embedding models are commonly adapted using a single training objective across diverse tasks, despite different tasks requiring fundamentally different optimization strategies. We introduce Task-Conditional Flow Matching (TCFM), a multilingual embedding adaptation framework that selectively applies Flow Matching to translation tasks while optimizing retrieval, classification, and pair-classification tasks with objectives better aligned to their learning dynamics. TCFM further combines teacher-guided representation preservation with a three-stage curriculum to enable stable adaptation. Evaluated on the Indic Massive Text Embedding Benchmark, TCFM establishes a new state-of-the-art, consistently improving embedding quality across a diverse set of multilingual tasks and generalizing across embedding model families. We will publicly release the codebase and datasets upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了On-Policy Distillation (OPD)及其改进变体On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)在多语言数学推理任务中的表现，涵盖英语、韩语和日语。实验表明OPD²在三个语种上均优于原始OPD，尤其在韩语和日语上提升显著，并缩小了英韩性能差距。此外，仅用英语数据的OPD虽能提升韩日语性能，但会导致输出向英语偏移，凸显了多语言数据对保持目标语言回复的重要性。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD作为强化学习的替代方案在LLM后训练中展现出潜力，但其在多语言环境下的有效性尚未得到充分探索。作者旨在填补这一研究空白，探究OPD及其变体在多语言数学推理场景中的表现。

Result: OPD²在所有测试语言上均一致优于原始OPD，在韩语和日语上的提升尤为显著，并且通常缩小了英语与韩语之间的性能差距。另外发现仅用英语数据的OPD也能提升韩日语性能，但会导致生成内容向英语偏移。

Conclusion: OPD²在多语言数学推理中是一种比OPD更有效的蒸馏方法，尤其有利于非英语语言。研究还表明，使用多语言数据对于保持目标语言的输出至关重要，仅依赖英语数据虽然能提升性能，但会牺牲语言保真度。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) is emerging as a promising alternative to reinforcement learning for LLM post-training, yet its effectiveness in multilingual settings remains underexplored. We study OPD and its advanced variant, On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$), for mathematical reasoning in English, Korean, and Japanese. OPD$^2$ improves OPD by using the probability gap between a post-trained teacher and its base model as the learning signal. Experiments with Qwen3 show that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms the original OPD, with particularly strong improvements in Korean and Japanese, and generally narrows the English-Korean performance gap. We further find that English-only OPD can also increase performance for Korean and Japanese, but often shifts the responses toward English, highlighting the importance of multilingual data to preserving target-language responses.

</details>


### [40] [Hierarchical Latent Prediction for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05806)
*Chang Shi,Tim Pearce,Manan Tomar,Siddhartha Sen,John Langford*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出层级潜在预测（HiLP），通过引入辅助的高层级抽象潜变量来减少潜在空间滚动中的误差累积效应，从而改进语言模型的长程推理和规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Next-Token Prediction（NTP）的teacher-forced训练范式对长程推理和规划并非最优；已有的Multi-Token Prediction（MTP）和NextLat等辅助目标要么视野有限，要么在多步滚动中遭受复合误差，难以有效解决长程推理问题。

Method: HiLP引入辅助的高层级抽象潜变量，通过分层预测的方式减少潜在空间多步展开时的误差累积，同时保持对多个未来token的预测能力。

Conclusion: HiLP通过层级化潜在预测有效缓解了误差累积问题，在长程推理、编码任务和推理效率方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: While standard Next-Token Prediction (NTP) lays the foundation of language model pre- training, its teacher-forced training paradigm may not be optimal for long-horizon reasoning and planning. Recent works such as Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) and Next-Latent prediction (NextLat) try to mitigate the problem through predicting multiple future tokens and self-supervised prediction in the latent space. However, those auxiliary objectives either have a limited horizon or suffer from compounding error from multi-step rollout. We introduce Hierarchical Latent Prediction (HiLP), which introduces an auxiliary higher-level abstract latent to help reduce the error accumulation effect in latent-space rollouts. Experiments show that HiLP can lead to longer-horizon coherent belief state representation and demonstrate the effectiveness of our method across coding and multi-step reasoning benchmarks, and offers more speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>

*Hong Jiang,Junnan Zhu,Jingwang Huang,Xiao Sun,Yuming Yang,Jiang Zhong,Ruirui Chen,Jingman Shi,Hao Wu,Nayu Liu,Xinyi Jiang,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出M³R-Bench，一个统一的、基于证据的多模态隐喻理解基准，包含1000个图文实例及人工验证标注，提供隐喻出现、目标-源映射、情感和分阶段解释的联合标注。同时提出M³R-Reasoner模型，结合课程式推理监督与任务感知强化学习，仅用8B参数即在多个指标上超越更大的闭源多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐喻理解基准主要依赖孤立子任务进行评估，缺乏基于证据的解释，难以判断模型是否真正建立了基于视觉和文本线索的映射关系。需要统一的、基于证据的基准来全面评估多模态隐喻理解能力。

Method: 1) 构建M³R-Bench基准：1000个图文实例，基于概念隐喻理论和非字面语言理解理论，提供隐喻出现、目标-源映射、情感和分阶段解释（证据识别→映射建立→情感推理）的联合标注。2) 提出M³R-Reasoner模型：结合课程式推理监督与任务感知强化学习，将模型推理与隐喻解读对齐。

Result: 现有模型在M³R-Bench上常忽视视觉证据、依赖表面文本线索、产生不准确的目标-源映射，暴露跨模态证据-映射不匹配。M³R-Reasoner仅用8B参数即在四项统一任务指标上超越更大的闭源MLLM，在视觉证据和情感论证分数上比GPT-5.5高28.45和30.11分，平均rubric分数比Claude-Sonnet-4.6高8.00分。

Conclusion: M³R-Bench为多模态隐喻理解提供了统一的、基于证据的评估框架，揭示了当前模型在跨模态证据-映射方面的不足；M³R-Reasoner证明了通过推理监督与强化学习结合，小参数模型也能在多模态隐喻理解中超越大型闭源模型。

Abstract: Metaphor enables the understanding of abstract concepts through cross-domain mappings while conveying affective attitudes. In multimodal scenarios, visual and textual information jointly construct Target--Source mappings, requiring both conceptual understanding and cross-modal reasoning. However, existing benchmarks mainly evaluate metaphor understanding through isolated subtasks and lack evidence-grounded explanations, making it difficult to assess whether models establish mappings grounded in visual and textual cues.To address these limitations, we introduce M$^3$R-Bench, a unified and evidence-grounded benchmark containing 1,000 image--text instances with human-verified annotations. Guided by Conceptual Metaphor Theory and theories of nonliteral language understanding, M$^3$R-Bench provides joint annotations for metaphor occurrence, Target--Source mapping, sentiment, and stage-wise explanations following ``evidence identification--mapping establishment--sentiment inference.''Evaluations on M$^3$R-Bench reveal that existing models often overlook visual evidence, rely on superficial textual cues, and produce inaccurate Target--Source mappings, exposing a cross-modal evidence--mapping mismatch. To address this mismatch, we propose M$^3$R-Reasoner, which combines curriculum-based reasoning supervision with task-aware reinforcement learning to align model reasoning with metaphor interpretation. Experiments show that, with only an 8B-parameter backbone, M$^3$R-Reasoner outperforms larger proprietary MLLMs across four unified-task metrics and improves Visual Evidence and Sentiment Justification scores over GPT-5.5 by 28.45 and 30.11 points, respectively, while surpassing Claude-Sonnet-4.6 by 8.00 points in mean rubric score. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/hongshi4/M3R-Bench.

</details>


### [42] [Decomposed Entailment for Factuality Checking and Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05823)
*Achir Oukelmoun,Nasredine Semmar,Gaël De Chalendar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HallDetect是一个轻量级、无需参考、黑盒的幻觉检测框架，通过将生成内容分解为原子声明，利用编码器蕴涵模型与多尺度源块对比验证，跨4个基准测试在三个上优于同等资源的基线方法，并提供逐条声明的审计追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的可靠性常因事实不一致（如幻觉）而受损，现有检测方法通常在资源消耗、可解释性或多任务泛化方面存在不足，亟需一个轻量且高效的幻觉检测方案。

Method: HallDetect采用分解式事实评估：将生成内容分解为原子声明，通过紧凑的基于编码器的蕴涵模型，利用多尺度源块库的对比公式逐条验证，并以非对称评分聚合（单条高置信矛盾声明即标记响应为幻觉）。所有基线共享4比特量化骨干和消费级硬件预算，实现公平对比。

Result: 在受控实验协议下，HallDetect在四个基准中的三个上优于同等资源的生成式和嵌入式基线，且在不同骨干家族间保持稳定性，同时能生成声明到文本片段的审计追踪以定位每个错误。

Conclusion: HallDetect验证了轻量级、参考自由的编码器方法在源接地生成任务的幻觉检测上具有竞争力，并兼顾了可解释性与硬件可及性，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) is often compromised by factual inconsistencies, including hallucinations---cases where generated content is not supported by the underlying source. We present HallDetect, a lightweight, reference-free, and black-box framework for hallucination detection that we evaluate not only on summarization but across a broader range of source-grounded generation settings. HallDetect builds on decomposition-based factuality evaluation: generated content is decomposed into atomic claims, each verified by a compact encoder-based entailment model through a contrastive formulation over a multi-scale library of source chunks, and aggregated with an asymmetric score in which a single confidently contradicted claim flags the response. Under a controlled protocol in which all methods share the same 4-bit quantized backbones and consumer-grade hardware budget, HallDetect outperforms comparably resourced generative and embedding-based baselines on three of four benchmarks while remaining stable across backbone families, and yields a claim-to-span audit trail that localizes each error.

</details>


### [43] [MoCA: Implicit Social Context Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05825)
*Wenhao Xu,Kaiwen Zhang,Hao Li,Maowei You,Yongzheng Ji,Siyuan Zuo,Jingxuan Yu,Sina A,Xinyao Tan,Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了隐式社会情境分析（MoCA）任务，通过情感、意图和立场三个维度系统建模隐式社会交流，并构建了包含3108个多模态实例的高质量基准。同时提出冲突驱动溯因推理（CoDAR）框架，通过建模观察表达与预期真实行为之间的认知冲突来推断隐藏心理状态，显著提升了多模态大模型的性能，但与人类推理仍存在较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类社交中的情感和意图常以高度隐式的方式传达，其潜在含义通过间接的、社会文化性的信号表达，而非直接陈述。这类隐式社会情境在现实互动中普遍存在，但缺乏正式、系统的研究框架。

Method: 1) 提出MoCA任务，从情感、意图、立场三个关键维度系统建模隐式社会情境；2) 构建包含3108个多模态实例的高质量基准，配有细粒度认知标注（谁对谁表达什么、如何表达、为何如此表达）；3) 提出CoDAR框架，将观察表达与预期真实行为之间的差异建模为认知冲突，从而推断隐藏心理状态。

Result: 实验表明最先进的多模态大模型在该任务上表现较差，因为它们依赖显式线索且对潜在社会情境的推理能力有限。CoDAR框架能显著提升模型性能，但与人类推理水平仍存在较大差距。

Conclusion: 隐式社会理解是一个根本性难题。CoDAR通过认知冲突驱动的溯因推理为隐式社会情境分析提供了有效路径，但当前模型与人类之间的巨大差距表明该领域仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: Human social communication, such as affection and intent, is often conveyed in highly implicit ways, where underlying meanings are expressed through indirect, socially and culturally grounded signals rather than explicit statements. Such implicit social contexts are pervasive in real-world interactions, yet there remains a lack of a formal and systematic framework for studying them. In this paper, we introduce Implicit Social Context Analysis (MoCA), a novel task that systematically models implicit social scenarios along three key dimensions: affection, intent, and stance. We construct a high-quality benchmark containing 3,108 multimodal instances collected from real-world sources, with fine-grained cognitive annotations revealing who expresses what toward whom, as well as how and why it is conveyed. Using the MoCA dataset, we show that state-of-the-art multimodal large language models struggle significantly with this task because of their reliance on explicit cues and limited ability to reason over latent social contexts. To address this challenge, we propose Conflict-Driven Abductive Reasoning (CoDAR), a novel framework that models the discrepancy between observed expressions and expected truthful behavior as cognitive conflict, thereby enabling the inference of hidden mental states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoDAR substantially improves model performance. Nevertheless, a large gap from human reasoning remains, highlighting the fundamental difficulty of implicit social understanding.

</details>


### [44] [Enhancing Social Intelligence in LLMs with Hierarchical Reasoning and Utterance-Level Goal Rewarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05832)
*Xiaofeng Wang,Kakam Chong,Shuai Xiao,DeXin Kong,Qingyuan Tian,Chen Ju,Xu Yan,Shuai Zhao,Fei Huang,Rui Wang,Shuguang Han,jufeng chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TSR框架和LHRL-VGR算法，将社交对话分解为高层策略规划和低层语言执行两个层次，通过基于方差的奖励路由机制平衡目标完成与策略遵循，在SOTOPIA基准上使Qwen2.5-7B智能体超越GPT-4o基线7.32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法对所有话语施加统一的目标导向奖励，忽略了每一轮对话目标的特异性，也未能考虑潜在策略的合理性。LLMs在需要长期目标协调和快速适应的动态社交互动中表现不佳。

Method: 受计划行为理论启发，提出TSR框架将社交对话分解为高层战略规划与低层语言执行两阶段。提出LHRL-VGR算法，一种线性化分层强化学习算法，基于目标达成分数的方差动态路由奖励，平衡目标完成与策略遵循。

Result: 在SOTOPIA基准上，使用Qwen2.5-7B微调的智能体在目标完成成功率上超越GPT-4o基线7.32%，在多智能体社交协商任务中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过分层策略规划与方差门控奖励机制，能够有效提升LLM在动态社交互动中的目标达成能力，验证了将策略规划与语言执行分离的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel in structured tasks but struggle with dynamic social interactions, where success requires long-term goal coordination and rapid adaptation. Current methods often apply uniform goal-based rewards to every utterance, overlooking the specificity of objectives at each dialogue turn and failing to account for the rationale of potential strategies. Inspired by the Theory of Planned Behavior, we propose the Think-Strategy-Response (TSR) framework, which decomposes social dialogue into two hierarchical stages: high-level strategic planning and low-level linguistic execution. To optimize TSR, we introduce Linearized Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning with Variance-Gated Rewards (LHRL-VGR), a novel algorithm that dynamically routes rewards - balancing goal completion and strategy adherence - based on the variance of goal achievement scores. Experiments on the SOTOPIA benchmark show that our approach fine-tunes a Qwen2.5-7B agent to surpass the GPT-4o baseline by 7.32% in goal completion success, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance in multi-agent social negotiation tasks.

</details>


### [45] [MameLoshnLM: Yiddish Language Model and Evaluation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05850)
*Uri Katz,Omer Goldman,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Reut Tsarfaty,Noah A. Smith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MameLoshnLM，这是第一个专门为意第绪语（Yiddish）构建的开源80亿参数语言模型。作者创建了Oytser高质量预训练语料库和Kashes多任务基准，通过在Llama 3.1 8B上继续预训练获得该模型。实验表明MameLoshnLM在基准任务上优于同规模开源模型，并更好地捕捉了意第绪语特有的词汇和形态模式，为低资源语言模型开发提供了实用模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意第绪语虽然拥有丰富的文本传统，但数字资源有限且缺乏可靠的评估基准，阻碍了其语言建模发展。现有的多语言语料库和基准往往包含大量噪声、机器翻译和错误分类的文本，无法有效代表该语言，因此需要专门的高质量资源和模型。

Method: 作者构建了两个核心资源：(1) Oytser——结合当代网络来源和文学材料的高质量意第绪语预训练语料库；(2) Kashes——涵盖翻译、语言分析、信息抽取和语言理解的多任务基准。基于这些资源，在Llama 3.1 8B上进行继续预训练得到MameLoshnLM。

Result: 在Kashes基准的所有任务中，MameLoshnLM超越了同规模的开源基线模型。分析显示其优势不仅是数量上的：相比通用多语言模型，MameLoshnLM更好地捕捉了意第绪语定义性的词汇和形态模式，揭示了嘈杂的网络级多语言数据对低资源语言的更广泛失败模式。

Conclusion: 该工作为意第绪语NLP奠定了基础，并为资源丰富但数字代表性不足的历史语言提供了语言模型开发的实用模板。研究结果强调了为低资源语言构建专门的高质量数据和基准的重要性，而不是依赖有噪声的多语言替代品。

Abstract: We present MameLoshnLM, the first open-source 8B-parameter language model built specifically for Yiddish. Despite Yiddish's rich textual tradition, its limited digital presence and the scarcity of reliable evaluation resources have constrained progress in Yiddish language modeling. Existing multilingual corpora and benchmarks are often poor proxies for the language, containing substantial amounts of noisy, machine-translated, and misclassified text. We address these gaps by introducing Oytser, a high-quality Yiddish pretraining corpus that combines contemporary web-native sources with literary materials, and Kashes, a multi-task benchmark spanning translation, linguistic analysis, information extraction, and language understanding. Using these resources, we continue pretraining Llama 3.1 8B to obtain MameLoshnLM. Across the tasks in the benchmark, MameLoshnLM outperforms open baselines of similar scale. Our analyses show that these gains are not only quantitative: relative to general-purpose multilingual models, MameLoshnLM better captures language-defining lexical and morphological patterns, pointing to a broader failure mode of noisy web-scale multilingual data for low-resource languages. Our results provide both a foundation for Yiddish NLP and a practical template for language model development in historically rich but digitally underrepresented languages.

</details>


### [46] [Mapping Similarity Spaces across Embedding Models with Synthetic Query Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05857)
*Marcin Rozmus,Peter van der Putten*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了检索增强生成（RAG）系统中不同嵌入模型之间相似度分数不可直接比较的问题，提出了Synthetic Query Probing方法，通过从文档生成查询来构建受控的查询-块对，无需人工标注即可大规模分析跨模型的相似度行为。实验表明，虽然模型在排序上大体一致，但绝对分数存在系统性偏差，通过线性、等渗和分位数映射可以部分对齐这些分数空间，其中等渗回归效果最佳，提高了阈值的可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖相似度分数检索相关内容，但不同嵌入模型因几何性质不同，其相似度分数无法直接比较。这导致模型迁移困难，且已调优的阈值无法跨模型复用，限制了系统的灵活性和可维护性。

Method: 提出Synthetic Query Probing框架：从文档中生成查询，构造受控的查询-块对，实现大规模、无参考的跨模型相似度行为分析。然后学习分数转换函数（线性、等渗、分位数映射），将不同嵌入模型的分数分布进行对齐，而非映射嵌入本身。

Result: 在SciFact和专有语料库上的实验表明：1) 不同嵌入模型在排序上大体一致，但绝对分数存在系统性失真；2) 学习的映射函数能部分对齐分数空间，改善阈值可移植性；3) 等渗回归在所有映射方法中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 跨模型校准是必要的，Synthetic Query Probing可作为分析嵌入可比性的可扩展框架，为RAG系统的模型迁移和阈值复用提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems rely on similarity scores to retrieve relevant content, yet scores are not directly comparable across embedding models due to differing geometric properties, complicating model migration and limiting threshold reuse. We study how similarity scores can be related by learning mappings between score distributions rather than embeddings. We introduce Synthetic Query Probing, generating queries from documents to create controlled query-chunk pairs, enabling large-scale, reference-free analysis of cross-model similarity behavior. We evaluate the approach on multiple embedding configurations and learn score conversion functions using linear, isotonic, and quantile mappings. Experiments on SciFact and a proprietary corpus show that while models largely agree on rankings, their absolute scores exhibit systematic distortions. Learned mappings partially align these spaces and improve threshold portability, with isotonic regression performing best. Our results highlight the need for cross-model calibration and position Synthetic Query Probing as a scalable framework for analyzing embedding comparability.

</details>


### [47] [MACRO: Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05872)
*Paweł Batorski,Abtin Pourhadi,Akylgali Aitaza,Przemysław Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MACRO是一个在无需修改模型权重的情况下，通过学习任务特定的马尔可夫路由策略来优化LLM层执行路径的框架。它在多个基准上平均提升5.0%准确率，相比最优动态路由方法Dr. LLM提升7.2%，同时将路由搜索时间减少9.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM逐层顺序执行，而动态层路由（如层重复、跳过等）可以提升性能。然而现有路由方法存在三个主要问题：需要更新模型权重、每个测试实例需运行昂贵的搜索循环、或在推理时需要真实标签。作者希望提出一种无需这些限制的轻量级路由方案。

Method: MACRO将层路由建模为上下文相关的马尔可夫策略，其状态条件包含：层索引、计算预算阶段、方向位移和算子上下文。该策略支持三种操作：跳过、重复和残差隐藏状态加法。路由分布通过训练数据的反馈进行更新，并使用top-k Viterbi算法解码出高概率的候选程序（即路由路径）。整个过程不修改底层LLM参数。

Result: 在多个开源LLM及多样化的推理和知识基准上，MACRO相对未路由基线平均提升5.0%准确率，小模型上增益最大。相比最先进的动态路由方法Dr. LLM，MACRO提升了7.2%，同时将路由搜索时间从14.8小时降至1.6小时（9.4倍加速）。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MACRO证明了通过马尔可夫策略学习任务特定层路由能有效提升LLM性能，同时避免了权重更新和昂贵的逐实例搜索，提供了一种高效、可扩展的动态路由替代方案。

Abstract: Standard Large Language Models (LLMs) execute layers sequentially. Dynamic layer routing, i.e. search for a different execution path through layers involving layer repetitions, skips and other moves, can improve performance. Existing routing approaches often require updating model weights, running expensive search loops per test instance, or demand ground-truth labels during inference. In this work, we propose Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers (MACRO), a framework that learns task-specific routes over LLM architectures without modifying underlying parameters. MACRO models layer routing as a context-dependent Markov policy conditioned on layer indices, computation budget phases, directional displacements, and operator context, supporting skip, repeat, and residual hidden-state addition operations. The Markov route distribution is updated via feedback on training data and decoded using a top-k Viterbi algorithm to isolate high-probability candidate programs. We evaluate MACRO across diverse reasoning and knowledge benchmarks on multiple open-weight LLMs. MACRO achieves a +5.0% average accuracy improvement over the unrouted baselines, with largest gains on small models. We outperform the best dynamic routing approach Dr. LLM by +7.2%, while reducing route-search time 9.4x (from 14.8 to 1.6 hours). Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Batorskq/MACRO.

</details>


### [48] [Causal Episodic Memory for Feedback-Driven Agent Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05906)
*Khang Nhat Hoang Vo,Tam Minh Chu,Anh Trac Duc Dinh,Thuyen Vinh Ha Bui,Tho Quan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MERIT，一种无需训练（training-free）的Text-to-SQL修复智能体，通过维护在线双极性记忆（oracle验证的成功修正 + 观察到的失败方向）来跨episode复用修复经验，在Spider上从66.34%提升到69.79%，在BIRD上从47.35%提升到48.44%的执行准确率。配对分析显示Spider上的增益显著而BIRD上较弱，且MERIT与无类型动态检索相比没有稳定优势，但Reflexion式记忆在BIRD上达到51.24%却推理成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在修复失败时经常丢弃成功修正，导致后续episode需要重新发现类似解决方案。本文动机是研究在不进行参数更新的情况下，能否将已完成的修复成果（finalized repair outcomes）用于改善后续Text-to-SQL episodes，从而避免重复劳动并提高修复效率。

Method: MERIT包含三个核心组件：1) 在线双极性记忆库，存储oracle验证的成功修正和观察到的失败方向；2) 确定性分类器分配粗粒度失败类型；3) 混合词法-稠密检索器（hybrid lexical-dense retriever）在类型条件下检索记忆，供冻结模型生成每次修订。只有earlier finalized episodes的记忆才有资格被检索。

Result: 使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和相同的初始预测与修复预算，MERIT在Spider上从66.34%提升到69.79%，在BIRD上从47.35%提升到48.44%。配对分析表明Spider上的增益有清晰证据，BIRD上证据较弱。与无类型动态检索相比，MERIT在两个基准上均无可靠分离。消融实验显示负记忆贡献有限，类型条件和词法-稠密排序的价值因数据集而异，schema内局部经验提供最一致的收益。

Conclusion: 研究厘清了因果跨查询记忆何时能改善修复（在Spider上有效），以及何时更广泛的记忆表示更可取（BIRD上Reflexion式记忆效果更好但成本高）。MERIT的增益主要来自schema局部经验，这为后续设计提供了方向性指导。

Abstract: LLM agents that repair failures often discard successful corrections, forcing later episodes to rediscover similar solutions. We study whether finalized repair outcomes can improve subsequent Text-to-SQL episodes without parameter updates. We introduce MERIT, a training-free agent that maintains an online dual-polarity memory of oracle-verified corrections and observed unsuccessful directions. Under oracle-assisted benchmark feedback, only memories from earlier finalized episodes are eligible for retrieval. A deterministic classifier assigns a coarse failure type, which conditions a hybrid lexical-dense retriever before the frozen model generates each revision. Using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with identical initial predictions and repair budgets, MERIT improves execution accuracy over stateless iterative repair from \(66.34\%\) to \(69.79\%\) on Spider and from \(47.35\%\) to \(48.44\%\) on BIRD. Paired analyses provide clear evidence for the Spider gain but weaker evidence on BIRD. MERIT is not reliably separated from untyped dynamic retrieval on either benchmark, while Reflexion-style memory reaches \(51.24\%\) on BIRD at substantially higher inference cost. Ablations show that negative memory contributes modestly, the value of type conditioning and lexical--dense ranking is dataset dependent, and schema-local experience provides the most consistent benefit. These results clarify when causal cross-query memory improves repair and when broader memory representations remain preferable.

</details>


### [49] [Clinical Communication Processing with Models Trained on LLM-Generated Synthetic Data: A Structured Survey and Novel Application Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05993)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇结构化叙事综述,探讨利用大语言模型将结构化临床数据(如病历、诊断标签、症状列表、护理计划)转化为合成临床通信文本(如短信、口头交接),以指导下游医疗NLP模型的训练。文章提出了按源表示、通信形式和参与者、生成方法、下游任务组织的分析框架,并辅以13个新颖案例研究,证明合成通信可以引导构建临床NLP系统,尤其在缺乏真实标注数据的通信渠道和低资源语言场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量临床价值蕴含在沟通交流中(患者症状描述、医患对话、救护车交接、护士换班),而非结构化记录。这类语言的含义依赖于说话者角色、意图、因果、不确定性、省略和渠道噪声,因此医疗NLP必须按"被传达的方式"而非"被编码的方式"来解释信息。然而,真实交流的语料稀缺——隐私性、碎片化和标注成本高,构成了核心瓶颈。

Method: 本文采用结构化叙事综述方法,从四个维度组织文献:(1)源表示(临床数据的原始形式);(2)通信形式和参与者(文本vs语音、说话者角色);(3)生成方法(如何将源转换为合成通信);(4)下游任务(合成通信用于训练何种NLP系统)。此外,作者补充了13个新颖的案例研究,覆盖EMS预到达报告、战场无线电伤员文档、护士交接、患者门户分诊、低资源出院通信等场景。

Result: 案例研究发现:合成通信可以引导构建临床NLP系统;微调的编码器模型在竞争性上优于评估的零样本基线;故意降级的通信(模拟真实渠道噪声)对提升鲁棒性有价值。主要局限在于:大多数研究在保留的合成通信上进行评估,而"在合成数据上训练、在真实数据上测试"的证据仍然有限。

Conclusion: 合成临床通信正逐渐成为一种实用的研究资源,但要将其确立为可复用的临床基础设施,仍需真实数据迁移验证、安全性和外部验证方面的努力。这为医疗NLP在缺乏真实标注数据的通信场景提供了可行的bootstrap路径。

Abstract: Much clinical value is conveyed not through structured records but through communication: exchanges in which patients describe symptoms, clinicians reason and give instructions, ambulances hand over to emergency departments, and nurses pass on a shift. Such language differs from tabular data because meaning depends on speaker role, intent, causality, uncertainty, omission, and channel noise. Healthcare natural language processing must therefore interpret information as conveyed rather than coded. This requires well-annotated corpora, which are scarce because authentic exchanges are private, fragmented, and costly to annotate. Large language models offer a way forward by transforming clinical sources, such as records, diagnostic labels, symptom lists, or care plans, into written and transcribed communication for downstream models. We present a structured narrative survey organized by source representation, communication form and participants, generation method, and downstream task, complemented by thirteen novel case studies. These build clinical NLP systems for communication channels and languages without labeled real-world data, including EMS pre-arrival reports, field-radio casualty documentation, nurse handoffs, patient-portal triage, and low-resource discharge communication. They show that synthetic communication can bootstrap such systems. Findings include the competitiveness of fine-tuned encoder models over evaluated zero-shot baselines and the value of deliberately degraded communication for robustness. The main limitation is that most studies evaluate on held-out synthetic communication, while train-on-synthetic, test-on-authentic evidence remains limited. We conclude that syn-thetic clinical communication is becoming a practical research resource; establishing it as reusable clinical infrastructure will require authentic-data transfer, safety and external validation.

</details>


### [50] [EpiBench: Can LLMs Understand Epitopes for Antibody Drug Discovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06022)
*Zirui Wang,Jiaqi Wang,Qinghan Wang,Yuzhi Xu,Gang Du,Tingjun Hou,Odin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EpiBench，一个基于序列、封闭式且可自动评分的基准，用于评估大语言模型（LLMs）在抗体表位推理方面的能力。该基准包含1609个基于结构接触、功能B细胞实验和深度突变扫描逃逸数据构建的样本，覆盖五个关联任务，并系统评估了九个通用LLM，发现现有模型在抗体特异性序列理解、长上下文残基定位和生物学推理方面仍存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表位决定抗体与抗原的结合位置，对药物开发至关重要。尽管LLM展现出强大的生物医学推理能力，但尚无明确评估证明其能否直接从抗原和抗体序列中推断表位信息。现有表位资源局限于孤立预测任务或依赖专门的结构设定，而通用蛋白质基准未覆盖抗体开发全流程中表位中心的决策。

Method: 构建EpiBench基准：1) 收集1609个基于结构抗体-抗原接触、功能B细胞实验和深度突变扫描逃逸测量的样本；2) 设计五个关联任务（可靶向区域发现、抗体条件表位识别、表位分箱、功能表位评估、抗体逃逸评估）；3) 采用受控采样减少捷径评估伪影；4) 评估九个通用LLM，并结合任务专用基线、抗原长度分层、显式推理对比和失败模式检查进行行为分析。

Result: 当前LLM仅捕获部分表位相关信号，但在抗体特异性序列接地、长上下文残基定位和生物学接地推理方面存在明显不足，尚未达到可靠辅助抗体发现所需的水平。

Conclusion: EpiBench为衡量和提升序列感知的生物医学LLM提供了诊断性测试平台，有助于推动LLM辅助抗体发现向更可靠的方向发展。

Abstract: Epitopes determine where antibodies bind antigens and shape downstream therapeutic properties such as functional blockade and escape resistance, making epitope understanding central to antibody drug discovery. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong biomedical reasoning ability, it remains unclear whether they can infer epitope information directly from antigen and antibody sequences. Existing epitope resources typically focus on isolated prediction tasks or rely on specialized structural settings, while general protein benchmarks do not evaluate epitope-centered decisions across the antibody development workflow. To address this gap, we introduce EpiBench, a closed-book, sequence-based, and automatically scorable benchmark for evaluating epitope reasoning in LLMs. EpiBench contains 1,609 curated samples grounded in structural antibody--antigen contacts, curated functional B-cell assays, and deep mutational scanning escape measurements. It covers five connected tasks: targetable region discovery, antibody-conditioned epitope identification, epitope binning, functional epitope assessment, and antibody escape assessment, with controlled sampling to reduce shortcut-based evaluation artifacts. We evaluate nine general-purpose LLMs and analyze their behavior through task-specific baselines, antigen length stratification, explicit-reasoning comparison, and failure-mode inspection. The results show that current LLMs capture partial epitope-related signals but remain limited in antibody-specific sequence grounding, long-context residue localization, and biologically grounded reasoning. Therefore, EpiBench provides a diagnostic testbed for measuring and improving sequence-aware biomedical LLMs toward reliable LLM-assisted antibody discovery.

</details>


### [51] [FormBharo: Designing and Evaluating a Voice Agent for Conversational Form Filling in Rural India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06027)
*Aman Dalmia,Sanskriti Midha,Jigar Doshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FormBharo，一个面向印度低识字率人群的语音代理系统，通过电话自动填写社会福利表格。系统结合LLM与确定性规则校验，并在ARMMAN组织试点用于孕产妇健康项目登记。作者开源了FormVoiceAgentBench基准测试集，发现组件性能无法预测端到端表现，最终采用基于Pareto的加权标量化方法在准确性、成本和延迟之间做出权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度几乎所有社会福利都始于表格填写，但最需要这些福利的人群往往不识字，需要通过对话完成登记。目前这项工作依赖一线卫生工作者逐一登记受益人，严重浪费有限的医疗资源，亟需自动化方案。

Method: 构建FormBharo语音代理，将LLM与确定性规则校验及流程控制相结合，在严格的延迟和成本预算下通过电话对话填写结构化表格。同时开源FormVoiceAgentBench基准（含3,760个多轮对话测试和960个模拟通话），并使用Pareto加权标量化选择部署配置。

Result: 使用错误率高的真实语音转写文本时，表格完成率下降高达约41个百分点。规则控制能恢复许多轮次级别的提取错误，使小型廉价模型在表格完成率上匹配或超越前沿模型。组件性能无法预测端到端表现：GPT-5.5在参考转写上的轮次提取准确率领先（99.8%）但在表格完成率上排名更低。没有单一模型在准确性、成本和延迟三个维度同时最优。

Conclusion: 由于错误在流水线中既会传播也会相互抵消，最优模型选择只能通过端到端评估来发现。在准确性、成本和延迟三者间存在权衡，需要采用多目标优化方法（Pareto加权标量化）来选出可部署的配置。

Abstract: In India, almost every social benefit starts with a form, yet the people who need these benefits most are often unable to read or write. Reaching them requires a spoken conversation. Today that work falls to frontline health workers who enroll beneficiaries one at a time, a poor use of stretched capacity. We built FormBharo ("fill the form" in Hindi), a voice agent that fills a structured form over a phone call under tight latency and cost budgets by pairing Large Language Models (LLMs) with deterministic, rule-based validation and flow control. It is being piloted with ARMMAN, an NGO running large-scale maternal and child mobile-health programs in India, to enroll low-income, Hindi-speaking mothers in antenatal and postnatal care. To our knowledge, it is the first voice agent piloted to fill an enrollment form for this population. We openly release FormVoiceAgentBench, a benchmark pairing human-recorded Hindi audio with 3,760 multi-turn conversation tests across 960 simulated calls, to evaluate our agent's components (transcription, extraction, reply generation) and end-to-end form completion under real acoustic variations. Form completion drops by up to ~41 points when LLMs receive error-prone real-speech transcripts instead of reference ones. The rule-based controls recover many turn-level extraction errors, helping smaller, cheaper models match or surpass frontier models on form completion. Component performance does not predict end-to-end performance: GPT-5.5 leads turn-level extraction accuracy on reference transcripts (99.8%) but ranks lower on form completion. Since errors both propagate and cancel across the pipeline, the optimal model choice of models emerges only through end-to-end evaluation. Finally, no single model is best across accuracy, cost, and latency at once, so we use a Pareto-based weighted-sum scalarization to select a deployable configuration balancing the three.

</details>


### [52] [Training-Free Token-Level Steering for LLM Personalized Co-Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06069)
*Wenhao Mao,Chengbin Hou,Weixiao Wang,Jialiang Zhu,Min Liu,Yibin Hao,Hairong Lv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SteerWrite是一个无需训练的个性化协同写作框架，通过特定设计适配小规模数据集，在不进行梯度更新的情况下将基础模型适配到专业领域，并在多个数据集、指标和模型上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在个性化任务中缺乏专业领域知识。传统微调方案计算成本高且难以应对快速数据更新，检索增强生成无法提供细粒度的token级控制，而现有界面仍以聊天为主，协同写作范式尚未被充分利用。

Method: SteerWrite采用无需训练的框架设计，不依赖梯度更新即可将基础模型适配到专业领域，并针对小数据集场景进行了专门的设计优化。

Result: 在多个数据集、指标和模型上达到最先进的性能，显著减少了人工编辑工作量。

Conclusion: SteerWrite验证了无需训练的协同写作方法在个性化任务上的有效性，为克服微调的计算瓶颈和检索增强的粒度限制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) show great promise for personalization, they often lack specialized domain knowledge. Conventional solutions like fine-tuning struggle with high computational costs and rapid data updates, while Retrieval-Augmented Generation fails to provide fine-grained, token-level steering. Furthermore, chat-based interfaces remain dominant, whereas productive co-writing paradigms have not yet been well exploited beyond the coding domain. To this end, we introduce SteerWrite, a training-free framework designed for personalized co-writing. Our method effectively adapts the base model to specialized domains without gradient updates, with specific designs tailored to small datasets. Experiments demonstrate that SteerWrite achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse datasets, metrics, and models, significantly reducing human editing effort.

</details>


### [53] [Beyond Sequence Order: Syntax-Informed Positional Embeddings for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06111)
*Haris Riaz,Hyungji Kim,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SiPE（语法信息位置嵌入），在预训练阶段从依存句法解析中学习一个轻量级句法先验，并将其注入绝对、相对、旋转三种主流位置嵌入家族中。在SyntaxGym基准上提升最高10.3%，同时困惑度降低9.0%；在GLUE上提升最高8.2%，且只需单一解析条件化，建立了句法监督与推理成本之间新的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer的位置嵌入主要编码token距离与顺序，但对句法结构几乎不敏感。虽然已有将句法信息注入语言模型的工作，但大多在推理时需要边缘化多个解析树或运行时丢弃句法信息，导致性能下降或推理成本过高。作者希望在不改变自注意力及整体架构的前提下，找到语法先验注入的最优位置与方式。

Method: SiPE在预训练阶段从依存句法解析中学习轻量级句法先验，将其注入三种主流位置嵌入家族（绝对、相对、旋转），同时适用于编码器与解码器。作者系统隔离了先验应注入的位置与方式，发现：自回归解码器使用相对PE时，先验与注意力分数中的相对位置项进行乘法耦合效果最佳；编码器则将先验直接加到输入嵌入中效果最好。

Result: 在SyntaxGym基准上最高提升10.3%，同时困惑度降低9.0%（现有大多数句法注入方法反而会降低此指标）；在GLUE基准上最高提升8.2%。与需要推理时边际化多个解析或丢弃句法的现有方法不同，SiPE仅基于单一解析，建立了句法监督与推理成本之间的新帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: SiPE通过轻量级句法先验的精确注入，在不改动自注意力机制的情况下，同时提升了句法泛化、语言建模与下游语言理解能力，且推理时仅需单一解析，兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Positional embeddings (PE) in Transformers encode token distance and order but are largely agnostic to \textit{syntactic structure}. We introduce \textbf{S}yntax-\textbf{i}nformed \textbf{P}ositional \textbf{E}mbeddings (\textbf{SiPE}), which learns a lightweight syntactic prior from dependency parses during pretraining and injects it across all three dominant PE families (absolute, relative, rotary), for both encoders and decoders, leaving self-attention and the rest of the architecture untouched. We isolate \emph{where} and \emph{how} the prior should enter the model, and find it depends on the architecture: for autoregressive decoders that use relative PE, the prior is strongest when coupled multiplicatively with the relative-position term of the attention score, outperforming injection into the input embeddings, into self-attention, or into the positional and attention terms jointly---while for encoders it is best added directly to the input embeddings, composing with each encoder's native positional mechanism. We find that models pre-trained with SiPE improve on the SyntaxGym benchmark by up to $10.3\%$ while simultaneously reducing perplexity by $9.0\%$ over a base model with no syntactic supervision---a metric nearly every existing syntax-injection method instead degrades. Crucially, these gains extend beyond syntactic generalization: SiPE also improves real-world language understanding, raising scores on the GLUE benchmark by up to $8.2\%$ over a model trained without it. Unlike existing syntactic language models that marginalize over many parses at inference or discard syntax at runtime, SiPE conditions on a single parse, establishing a new Pareto frontier between syntactic supervision and inference cost.

</details>


### [54] [Routing Is Least Learnable Where It Is Most Valuable: Bounds on Representation Routing for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06171)
*Jiaming Wei,Zekun Wu,Adriano Koshiyama,Maria Perez-Ortiz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了Web智能体在不同观察模式（文本、像素或两者结合）下的表现，测量了六种观察模式在八个网站-模型组合上的效果。研究发现各种模式具有互补性，但路由选择的收益很大程度上被运行噪声所稀释。只有成本约束是稳健的：将无模式能解决的任务发送到最便宜的模式可节省9.5-30.6%成本。测试的五个路由策略均无法稳健地胜过固定选择一种良好模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web智能体的观察模式选择通常是固定的，但不同任务可能需要不同的观察模式。作者想探究按任务动态选择观察模式（路由）能带来多大收益，以及这种收益是否是真实的。

Method: 在VisualWebArena和WebArena上测量六种观察模式在八个站点-模型组合上的表现，测试oracle路由的上限，评估运行噪声的影响，然后测试五种路由策略（模式选择、强模式花费决策、基于任务文本的零成本规则、置信度级联、池化成本层级）。

Result: 各观察模式具有互补性，但oracle收益被12-14%的运行噪声大幅膨胀。成本约束是唯一稳健的发现：8/8个单元中节省9.5-30.6%成本且成功率不变。五种路由策略无一能稳健胜过固定良好模式。路由监督受限于智能体成功率，但标签供应与路由机会高度相关（相关系数0.95）。

Conclusion: 当前路由的障碍不在于路由本身，而在于当前智能体的能力限制。更强壮的智能体会产生更多标注数据，从而可能使路由策略变得有效。未来随着智能体能力提升，路由的潜力有望被释放。

Abstract: Web agents observe a browser through text, pixels, or both, and the choice is usually fixed once for all tasks. We measure six observation modes across eight site-model combinations (cells) on VisualWebArena and WebArena and ask what choosing per task would buy. The modes are complementary: each solves tasks the others miss, they fail in structurally different ways, and the best choice reverses between task sets. The obvious prize, an oracle that picks a winning mode for every task, looks large but is inflated by run-to-run noise: rerunning the same mode on the same tasks changes 12-14% of outcomes, so a second run of a mode already in hand gains about as much as adding a new one. What survives is a cost bound: sending only the tasks no mode solves to the cheapest mode cuts cost by 9.5-30.6% in 8 of 8 cells at unchanged success. We then test five routing policies (picking the mode, deciding when to spend on the strong mode, a zero-cost rule read off the task text, a confidence cascade, and pooled cost tiers), and none robustly beats simply fixing one well-chosen mode; the one exception is a fragile result in our sparsest cell. The central obstruction is that routing supervision is produced at the agent's success rate: the weaker the agent, the fewer labels a router gets, exactly where routing would be most valuable. This limit belongs to today's agents rather than to routing itself. Label supply and routing opportunity rise together (correlation 0.95 across cells), so a stronger agent can overturn the result, and we report the rerun noise bands and the full measurement protocol.

</details>


### [55] [NeSy-RAG: Neuro-Symbolic RAG for Explainable Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06292)
*Jonas Gann,Michael Gertz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NeSy-RAG，一个模块化神经符号RAG框架，通过将检索文本合成为可溯源的Prolog模块，实现可验证、可归因的推理，并引入符号知识缺口检测机制自动识别缺失的用户上下文，在ShARC基准上达到61.1%准确率，显著优于同模型RAG基线（42.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统存在两大问题：一是推理过程不透明，中间推理步骤难以验证和归因到具体证据；二是缺乏对用户特定上下文缺失的系统性检测，常导致不完整或错误的输出。

Method: NeSy-RAG包括三个核心组件：(1) 从检索文本块合成可归因的Prolog模块，为每个块生成编码布尔断言的语义谓词；(2) 使用联合自然语言-代码嵌入检索和组合谓词，构成Prolog查询；(3) 引入符号知识缺口检测机制，识别影响查询结果但缺失的用户事实，并自动触发后续交互。

Result: 在ShARC基准上，无需领域特定训练，NeSy-RAG达到61.1%的准确率，相比同模型RAG基线的42.8%提升了约18个百分点。

Conclusion: 执行Prolog查询可获得确定性答案及透明的执行轨迹，每个推理步骤都能链接到其来源文档，有效提升了RAG推理的可信度和可归因性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves question answering by grounding large language models (LLMs) in external knowledge such as text corpora. However, its reasoning process remains largely opaque: intermediate reasoning steps are difficult to verify and cannot be reliably attributed to specific evidence. Moreover, missing user-specific context is rarely detected systematically, often leading to incomplete or incorrect output.
  We propose NeSy-RAG, a modular neuro-symbolic RAG framework that synthesizes attributable Prolog modules from retrieved text chunks. For each chunk, the system generates semantically meaningful predicates that encode Boolean claims, which may depend on user facts. Using joint natural language-code embeddings, predicates are retrieved and composed into Prolog queries. To address incomplete user context, we introduce a symbolic knowledge-gap detection mechanism that identifies missing user facts whose truth values affect the query outcome and automatically triggers follow-up interactions.
  Executing the resulting Prolog queries yields deterministic answers together with transparent execution traces that link each reasoning step to its originating source. On the ShARC benchmark, without domain-specific training, NeSy-RAG achieves 61.1% accuracy, outperforming a same-model RAG baseline that achieves 42.8% accuracy.

</details>


### [56] [Benchmarking and Enhancing LLMs for Rule-Intensive Review of National Standard Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06312)
*Tao Wang,Qihao Yang,Rongjiao Liang,Lianghong Lin,Haitao Wang,Xinyu Cao,Tianyong Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GB/T-Bench，这是首个面向国家标准文档结构化审查的基准测试，包含层级化审查分类体系、可控反例生成机制和诊断导向的评估协议，并提出了多智能体框架GB/T-Reviewer，实验结果揭示了人机差距并验证了结构化技能协调的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在规则密集型文档审查方面的能力尚未得到充分评估。现有基准聚焦于领域知识和问答，忽视了专业文档的内在质量审查。国家标准的审查依赖人类专家，成本高且难以规模化，因此需要建立一个系统化的基准来评估和提升LLM在此类任务上的能力。

Method: 构建了GB/T-Bench，包含：(1) GB/T审查分类体系——覆盖文档结构、范围对齐、规范性模态、术语一致性和规范性引用的层级化模式，含25种可诊断错误类型；(2) 可控反例生成机制——结合确定性规则与约束LLM重写，将488份文档转化为7,306个可追踪的审查错误实例；(3) 诊断导向评估协议——要求错误位置、审查维度和错误类型的精确匹配，并辅以文档级覆盖率指标；(4) GB/T-Reviewer多智能体框架——将审查知识转化为专业技能，协调全局检查、定向诊断、规则扫描和结果验证。

Result: 对14个主流LLM的实验显示，最强模型仅达到0.3280的CMCS分数，而人类专家为0.6640，存在显著的人机差距。GB/T-Reviewer将最佳CMCS提升至0.5094，验证了结构化技能协调对规则密集型文档审查的价值。

Conclusion: 该工作为标准化及其他高风险文档领域中的可信AI铺平了道路，证明了将审查知识结构化为专门技能并协调多智能体协作，能显著提升LLM在规则密集型专业文档审查中的表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly support complex professional tasks, yet their capabilities in rule-intensive document review remain insufficiently evaluated. National standard documents, such as China GB/T standards, offer a representative testbed: they are lengthy, highly structured, and governed by explicit rules for scope, terminology, normative wording, and cross-section consistency. Existing benchmarks focus on domain knowledge and question answering, largely overlooking intrinsic quality review for professional documents. Such reviews rely heavily on human experts, making them costly and difficult to scale. To bridge this gap, we introduce GB/T-Bench, the first benchmark for the structured review of national standard documents. Its GB/T Review Taxonomy is a hierarchical schema covering document structure, scope alignment, normative modality, terminology consistency, and normative references, with 25 diagnosable error types. A controllable counterexample generation mechanism combines deterministic rules and constrained LLM rewriting to process 488 documents into 7,306 traceable review error instances for evaluation. We also develop a diagnosis-oriented evaluation protocol requiring exact matches on error location, review dimension, and error type, plus document-level coverage metrics. We further propose GB/T-Reviewer, a multi-agent framework that converts review knowledge into specialized skills and coordinates global inspection, targeted diagnosis, rule scanning, and result verification. Experiments with 14 mainstream LLMs reveal a substantial human-LLM gap: the strongest model achieves only 0.3280 CMCS versus 0.6640 for experts. GB/T-Reviewer raises the best CMCS to 0.5094, showing the value of structured skill coordination for rule-intensive document review. This work paves the way for trustworthy AI in standardization and other high-stakes document domains.

</details>


### [57] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Benchmarks for Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06329)
*Noam Koren,Roy Bar-Haim,Abigail Goldsteen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无参考框架，利用LLM评判器评估面向任务的对话智能体基准测试的质量（一致性、复杂性和策略覆盖率），并通过与人工标注对比及对LLM生成和扰动基准的验证，证明该框架能有效区分基准质量水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的任务导向对话智能体评估依赖基准测试，但基准本身的质量很少被评估。低质量的基准可能包含不一致的任务、过于简化的场景或有限的策略覆盖，导致评估结果不可靠，因此需要一种系统性评估基准质量的方法。

Method: 提出一个参考自由（reference-free）框架，使用LLM评判器从三个维度评估基准质量：(1) 一致性（consistency）、(2) 复杂性（complexity）、(3) 策略覆盖率（policy coverage），同时提供可操作的薄弱点诊断。

Result: 该框架与独立人工标注具有一致性；在不同领域和评判模型上，所提指标能一致区分不同质量的基准（包括不同能力LLM生成的基准和受控质量退化扰动的基准）；框架也适用于人工策划的基准测试。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估合成和人工策划的对话智能体基准测试提供了一种实用方法，有助于提升基准质量和评估可靠性。

Abstract: Task-oriented conversational agents are evaluated using curated or automatically generated benchmarks, yet benchmark quality is rarely assessed. Poor benchmarks may contain inconsistent tasks, simplistic scenarios, or limited policy coverage, leading to unreliable evaluations. We introduce a reference-free framework that uses LLM judges to assess benchmark consistency, complexity, and policy coverage, while providing actionable diagnostics of weaknesses. We validate the framework by demonstrating agreement with independent human annotations and by evaluating benchmarks generated by LLMs of varying capabilities, as well as benchmarks subjected to controlled quality-degrading perturbations. Across domains and judge models, the proposed metrics consistently distinguish between benchmark quality levels. We further demonstrate the framework's applicability to manually curated benchmarks. Our framework offers a practical approach for evaluating synthetic and manually curated conversational-agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [58] [RP-OPSD: Reasoning-Pivot-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation for Multilingual Reasoning Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06347)
*Xinye Wang,Junxiao Liu,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RP-OPSD（Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation），通过在策略自蒸馏中识别并引导"推理枢轴"（reasoning pivots）——即推进或改变推理过程的关键决策token——来提升多语言推理迁移能力。利用教师模型有无英文参考解时的分布偏移作为操作代理，引导特权蒸馏和参考锚定，在17种语言的数学推理基准上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言推理迁移对扩展LLM在低资源语言上的推理能力至关重要。现有OPSD及其变体虽提供密集token级监督，但其目标函数并未显式优先考虑对跨语言迁移最关键的推理信号。作者认为目标语言推理包含表层文本生成和推理枢轴（推进/引导推理过程的关键决策），现有方法未对此进行区分。

Method: 1) 提出推理枢轴概念，区分表层文本token与推理控制token；2) 利用教师模型在有无英文参考解条件下视图间的分布偏移作为操作代理，识别推理枢轴；3) 基于此引导特权蒸馏（privileged distillation）和参考锚定（reference anchoring），形成RP-OPSD框架。

Result: 在覆盖17种语言、多个难度级别的数学推理基准上，RP-OPSD优于强多语言推理基线和OPSD变体。进一步分析表明，该方法将特权蒸馏集中在推理控制和问题条件状态更新token上，同时降低对主要支持表层实现的token的权重。

Conclusion: 通过明确区分并优先训练推理枢轴token，可以显著提升多语言推理迁移的性能。这一发现揭示了OPSD类方法中推理信号优先级的重要性，为多语言推理蒸馏提供了新方向，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multilingual reasoning transfer is crucial for extending reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) beyond high-resource languages. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) and its variants have emerged as a promising paradigm, providing dense token-level supervision on student-generated rollouts, yet their objectives do not explicitly prioritize reasoning signals most critical to cross-lingual transfer. We characterize that target-language reasoning comprises the generation of both surface text and reasoning pivots, which are decisions that advance or redirect the reasoning process and shape subsequent inference. This motivates concentrating privileged distillation around such pivots. We therefore propose RP-OPSD, Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, using the distributional shift between matched teacher views with and without an English reference solution as an operational proxy to guide privileged distillation and reference anchoring. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks covering 17 languages and multiple difficulty levels show that our method outperforms strong multilingual reasoning baselines and OPSD variants. Further analysis reveals that RP-OPSD concentrates privileged distillation on reasoning-control and problem-condistioned state-update tokens, while downweighting it for tokens that mainly support surface realization. Our code is available at https://github.com/NJUNLP/RP-OPSD.

</details>


### [59] [The Bitter Lesson of Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06370)
*Ishan Patel,Sahil Sen,Elias Lumer,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了程序化工具调用（PTC，即以Python代码形式调用工具）与原生JSON工具调用在BFCL v4基准上14个语言模型上的性能差异。结果显示PTC在11/14个模型上达到或超过JSON基线，GPT-5.6系列提升10.6%，并行场景下13/14模型占优，且在上下文衰减条件下表现更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用使LLM超越训练数据成为智能体，而对代码能力强的模型，程序化工具调用（用脚本替代刚性JSON调用）能自然实现链接和并行化。但此前缺乏在成熟基准上、跨多代模型、真实任务条件下的系统性评估。

Method: 在BFCL v4基准上，对14个语言模型进行经验性对比实验：将工具暴露为类型化的Python存根（stub），模型通过代码调用工具，执行与结果在单轮智能体对话中处理。对比对象为原生JSON工具调用基线，并额外测试了并行扇出（parallel fan-out）和上下文衰减（context rot）场景。

Result: 1) PTC在11/14个模型上匹配或超越JSON工具调用基线；2) GPT-5.6系列相较JSON基线提升10.6%；3) 在并行扇出条件下，PTC在13/14个模型上匹配或优于基线；4) 在上下文衰减条件下，PTC保持稳定，而基线平均下降2.3%。

Conclusion: 程序化工具调用是JSON工具调用的可行且稳健的替代方案，其性能随模型发布代际的能力提升而同步增长，证明该范式具有实用价值和扩展潜力。

Abstract: Tool use transforms LLMs into agents that act beyond their training data, and for code-capable models, programmatic tool calling extends this further by replacing rigid JSON calls with scripts that chain and parallelize naturally. However, a systematic evaluation of tools as code on an established benchmark across current and prior model generations under real-world task conditions has not been conducted. In this work, we empirically compare programmatic tool calling (PTC) to native JSON tool calling across 14 language models on BFCL v4. In the programmatic tool calling paradigm, tools are exposed as typed Python stubs that the model invokes through code, with execution and results handled in a single agent turn. Programmatic tool calling matches or exceeds native JSON tool calling in 11 of 14 models on BFCL v4, with the GPT-5.6 family achieving a 10.6% improvement over the JSON tool calling baseline. Further, it matches or outperforms baseline in 13 of 14 models under parallel fan-out, and holds stable under context rot conditions where baseline degrades 2.3% on average. Our results demonstrate that programmatic tool calling is a viable and robust alternative to JSON tool calling, with performance tracking model capability across release generations.

</details>


### [60] [Learning When to Trust via Selective Context Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06377)
*Xian Sun,Wei Chow,Yingshuo Wang,Junhao Liu,Wei Gao,Qing Wu,Lingdong Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将语言模型对外部信号的响应问题重新定义为"选择性信任"问题，并引入MIST基准（四种匹配条件下的人工标注推理数据集）和SC2W指标（衡量误导信号将正确答案翻转为错误答案的频率），以及SCOPE方法（基于DPO目标、均衡覆盖四种条件的偏好对优化），在保持准确性不受损的同时显著降低模型的SC2W分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地依赖外部信号来回答问题，但单一的误导信号就可能将正确答案变为错误。传统的"训练模型抵抗误导信号"方法存在缺陷：完全忽略上下文的模型虽然看似健壮，但在上下文值得信任时却毫无用处。因此需要将问题重新定义为选择性信任，而非单纯的抵抗能力。

Method: 1) 引入MIST基准：每个推理项目在四种匹配条件下呈现（干净、误导、正确上下文、无关上下文）；2) 提出SC2W配对指标：统计误导信号将干净正确答案翻转为错误的频率；3) 提出SCOPE方法：挖掘"干净正确/误导错误"的失败样本，在四种条件均衡配对的偏好对上优化标准DPO目标，而非仅针对误导项目。

Result: 在广泛的基准测试中观察到误导信号的易感性是普遍存在的。SCOPE方法在流行的开源模型上显著降低了SC2W分数，同时当添加的上下文是干净、正确或无关时，保持了模型的准确性。

Conclusion: 作者主张模型应依据"选择性信任"来评判，而非仅仅依据抵抗力。真正的健壮性在于能够在可信的上下文中正确利用信息，同时抵御误导性信息的干扰。

Abstract: Language models increasingly condition their answers on external signals, and a single misleading one can turn a correct answer wrong. The obvious remedy, training models to resist such signals, hides a failure mode: a model that ignores all context looks robust yet is useless when the context is worth trusting. We recast the problem as selective trust and introduce MIST, a human-annotated benchmark that renders each reasoning item under four matched conditions (clean, misleading, correct-context, and irrelevant-context), together with SC2W, a paired metric counting how often a misleading signal flips a clean-correct answer to wrong. Across a comprehensive benchmark study, we observe that such a susceptibility is universal. We then propose SCOPE, which mines clean-correct/misleading-wrong failures and optimizes a standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) objective over matched preference pairs balanced equally across all four conditions, rather than over misleading items alone. Our approach substantially reduces SC2W on popular open-sourced models while preserving accuracy when the added context is clean, correct, or irrelevant. With this work, we argue that models should be judged on selective trust, not on resistance alone.

</details>

Abstract: This paper develops a unified mathematical theory of implied, local, and learned volatility surfaces. Total variance $w_t(k,τ)=τσ_t^2(k,τ)$ is an infinite-dimensional state constrained by positivity, calendar monotonicity, and the butterfly differential inequality. We establish the topology and tangent geometry of this arbitrage set and prove that a nondegenerate Gaussian shock at an active constraint exits with probability tending to one half. Exact invariance therefore requires tangency, reflection, or confinement to an arbitrage-free manifold. We separate this static invariance problem from dynamic no-arbitrage, derive the Musiela maturity-transport identity, and identify the additional fixed-contract martingale restriction. We formulate Hilbert-space dynamics, prove an exact modal reduction with closed-form truncation error, derive Karhunen--Loève factors, identify the portfolio derivative as a vega field, and obtain the covariance-optimal hedge $α^\ast=(H^\ast C H)^{-1}H^\ast Cν$. The local-volatility chart completes the geometry: Dupire local variance is the ratio $a=\partial_τw/g[w]$ of the calendar and butterfly constraint functionals. Neural operators provide arbitrage-free universal approximation through simplex and cone heads. Normalizing-flow maps then add tractable conditional densities: we derive exact change-of-variables formulae for exponential local-variance flows and invertible stick-breaking price-simplex flows, while stating the quasi-invariance conditions required in genuine function space. Finally, fading-signature fields encode surface history and yield autonomous finite-dimensional controlled dynamics. The result is one framework for representation, dynamics, arbitrage, dimension reduction, likelihood-based learning, simulation, and hedging, together with a falsifiable empirical protocol.

Abstract: This article studies a dynamic corporate risk management problem by considering the decision-making of risk-averse managers who exert costly effort and select project risk. We study how a Value-at-Risk (VaR) constraint affects managerial decisions and the distribution of firm value when the manager's objective is non-concave with a fixed salary and options. By the concavification technique, we analyze the optimal terminal firm value on the concave envelope of the objective function. Applying the quantile formulation and the martingale approach, we can derive explicit solutions for optimal effort, terminal firm value, and project choice. The optimal terminal firm value can be divided into nine cases by carefully discussing the choices of VaR floor and tail probability. Compared with the benchmark case, we find that a VaR manager will smooth terminal firm value across states, reducing it in good states while supporting it in adverse states. Moreover, a VaR requirement generally improves downside protection and reduces bankruptcy probability when the VaR floor is low or moderate. However, when the VaR floor is sufficiently high, it can increase bankruptcy probability and induce gambling-for-recovery behavior in adverse states. Our sensitivity analysis indicates that greater managerial effort uniformly improves firm value. Moreover, more incentive options make managers more responsible, leading to a smoother terminal firm value across states. In contrast, a high fixed salary makes the manager less responsible and ultimately causes a more dispersed firm value.

Abstract: Neural and numerical policy solvers can produce feasible controls even when the optimal rule and its binding constraints are unavailable. A primal-dual bracket certifies value loss, but it does not locate the optimal policy or explain which constraints genuinely bind. We show that, on the same declared simulation grid, one bracket can support both conclusions. For polyhedral controls, an exact conditional budget identity rewrites the residual as a pathwise nonnegative terminal Fenchel defect plus date-by-constraint complementary-slackness terms. A canonical Doob compensation removes the budget martingale that obscures small residuals. Bellman-primitive curvature conditions then yield an occupancy-weighted policy region with the sharp O(sqrt(G)) radius, while a paired constraint relaxation lower-bounds the optimal multiplier and certifies a binding face. A finite-sample resolution theorem quantifies the path budget needed to certify a target policy tolerance or face. Locked one-asset and two-asset audits cover every external policy error and make no false face declaration. An exact-wrapper stress test remains tight through 50 assets, while a separate state-dependent pilot identifies learned-dual tightness as the high-dimensional bottleneck. Reference solutions enter only after certification.

Abstract: Optimizing for both low Age of Information (AoI) and high throughput is critical for remote sensing applications that rely on multichannel wireless networks. However, jointly optimizing these two metrics is an analytically challenging problem, particularly in systems with heterogeneous and unreliable channels. To address this challenge, we propose TSDM, a Two-Stage Deficit Matching scheduling framework. TSDM is based on a second-order approach that characterizes the performance of each data flow by its mean and temporal variance. In the first stage, TSDM translates the high-level utility maximization objective into a concrete set of target mean and temporal variance statistics for transmissions over each node-channel pair. In the second stage, a low-complexity Weighted Matching Deficit (WMD) rule performs real-time channel assignment. We theoretically prove that TSDM achieves the desired mean and temporal variance for each flow. Furthermore, we conduct extensive simulations on two open joint throughput-AoI optimization problems. In both cases, TSDM significantly outperforms existing scheduling policies.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化控制系统面临隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击时，如何在攻击者与防御者的博弈中合理分配安全资源（监测节点）是一个关键挑战。现有方法在处理大规模网络时计算复杂度过高，需要一种可扩展且实用的安全分配策略。

Method: (i) 将最坏情况网络性能损失问题转化为可求解的半定规划(SDP)问题并求其上界；(ii) 在充分条件下建立SDP问题与有向图Katz中心性度量的关系，将问题转化为与网络规模无关的优化问题；(iii) 基于Katz中心性度量提出启发式搜索算法来选取次优监测节点，无需求解优化问题。

Result: 在Erdos-Renyi随机图上进行了不同网络规模的广泛仿真验证，结果表明所提出的方法能够有效估计最坏情况攻击下的网络性能损失，并合理选择监测节点以抵御所有可容许的攻击场景。

Conclusion: 该研究为大规模网络化控制系统的安全防护提供了实用性见解，通过Katz中心性度量降低了安全资源分配的计算复杂度，使得在大型网络中无需求解优化问题即可获得有效的防护策略。

Abstract: This paper deals with security allocation challenges for networked control systems represented by positive-weighted digraphs under stealthy false data injection attacks. These systems consist of interconnected subsystems, referred to as nodes in the underlying digraph, where an adversary aims to maximize network performance loss by stealthily attacking specific nodes. Meanwhile, a defender monitors several nodes to impose stealthiness constraints on the adversary's actions, thereby minimizing the network performance loss. We analyze the worst-case network performance loss of these stealthy attacks and make the following contributions: we (i) show that the worst-case network performance loss is upper-bounded by a tractable semi-definite programming (SDP) problem; (ii) establish the relationship between the SDP problem and the Katz centrality measure of the underlying digraph under a sufficient condition, resulting in a network-size-independent optimization problem; and (iii) provide a heuristic search based on the Katz centrality measure of the underlying digraph for selecting sub-optimal monitor nodes against all admissible attack scenarios without solving optimization problems. These results offer practical insights for safeguarding large-scale networked control systems against stealthy false data injection attacks. The obtained results are validated via extensive simulations on Erdos-Renyi random graphs with different network sizes.

</details>


### [67] [A Passivity-Based Analysis of First-Order Momentum-Based Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05492)
*Sepehr Moalemi,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散时间无源性的分析方法，用于分析一阶动量方法。通过环路变换，将动量方法表示为与输出严格无源（OSP）系统负反馈连接的无源控制器，并利用弱无源性定理推导出移位梯度渐近消失的超参数条件，在额外假设下建立了迭代收敛到全局最小值的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶动量方法（如梯度下降动量法）在优化中被广泛使用，但其收敛性分析通常依赖传统泛函分析或Lyapunov方法，缺乏统一且直观的系统论视角。作者希望利用无源性理论这一控制领域的强有力工具，为动量方法提供一种新的离散时间分析框架，从而更清晰地揭示超参数与收敛性之间的内在联系。

Method: 本文方法包括：（1）针对梯度满足下界0和上界L的扇形有界函数类，通过环路变换将动量方法重构为反馈互联结构；（2）在该结构中，动量方法被表示为无源控制器，与输出严格无源（OSP）系统形成负反馈连接；（3）应用弱无源性定理推导出移位梯度渐近消失的显式超参数条件；（4）额外假设存在唯一驻点且远离该点处梯度不会任意小，从而建立迭代收敛到全局最小值的结论。

Result: 推导出了保证移位梯度渐近趋于零的显式超参数条件；在唯一驻点和梯度远离驻点处非任意小的附加假设下，证明了迭代序列收敛到全局最小值点。

Conclusion: 无源性理论为动量方法的收敛性分析提供了一个统一的系统论框架，能够简洁地刻画动量方法的稳定性和收敛特性，并为超参数选择提供了理论指导。

By leveraging path-following techniques from real-time optimal control, the approach achieves tractable computation with guaranteed error bounds.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建分钟级检测流水线：1) 基于平滑状态速度（期权Delta速度用于指数期权，价格速度用于股票）；2) 时间严格分区；3) 结合SHAP归因解释每个警报；4) 测试集严格留出，阈值事先固定；5) 用隐马尔可夫模型推断市场制度作为调节条件；6) 通过泵-逆转形状评分实现跨市场迁移。

Result: 在印度BANKNIFTY指数期权测试集上，普通自编码器恢复10/10监管确认的操纵日。HMM市场制度调节导致以召回换精度的负面结果，精度仍约25%。动态特征迁移至美国小盘股：形状评分在ARQQ上AUC 0.91、ACY上AUC 0.81，且ARQQ案例中评分峰值落在投诉文档记录的分钟窗口内。SHAP归因显示未确认警报与确认日归因余弦相似度达0.99。

Conclusion: 跨市场和工具类型可迁移的是动态特征本身（泵-崩盘速度模式），而非速度幅值。精度天花板与不完整的执法标签一致，而非检测器失效。监管机构可依据该动态签名而非静态水平来识别操纵。

Abstract: Intraday market manipulation is hard to detect because its footprint is brief, buried in millions of quotes, and statistically similar to ordinary volatility. Detectors reach high recall only by flagging so many other days that measured precision collapses, producing alerts no regulator can act on. We show that this manipulation leaves a distinctive dynamic signature: a pump-and-crash pattern visible in the velocity of market state, rather than its level.
  We build a minute-level detection pipeline, strictly partitioned in time, based on smoothed state velocity: option-Delta velocity for index options and price velocity for equities. We explain every alert with SHAP attribution. We hold the test period strictly out-of-sample and fix all thresholds before evaluation. On the locked Indian BANKNIFTY index-options test, the plain autoencoder recovers 10 of 10 regulator-identified manipulation days.
  Conditioning detection on market regimes inferred by a hidden Markov model yields an instructive negative result. The regimes are descriptively distinct, but using them trades recall for precision. Under the closed-world assumption that unlabeled days are normal, precision remains near 25%.
  The same dynamic appears in thinly traded U.S. equities (SEC v. Patel). The shape of the signature survives the transfer; its velocity magnitude does not. A pump-reversal shape score ranks the complaint's alleged manipulation days with AUC 0.91 (ARQQ) and 0.81 (ACY). On the ARQQ worked example, the score peaks inside the complaint's documented minute window.
  Finally, exact SHAP attribution over every alert shows that unconfirmed alerts share the regulator-identified days' attribution profile (cosine similarity 0.99). The precision ceiling is consistent with incomplete enforcement labels rather than detector failure. What transfers across markets and instrument types is the dynamic signature itself.

</details>

Result: 提出两项政策建议：(1) 为避免挤占其他风险分担形式造成低效，系统运营商应允许通过其他方式签约的资源选择退出强制性容量机制，其对容量要求的贡献应从行政定义的需求曲线中扣除；(2) 若监管者希望推广单一合同形式，应考虑用塑形远期能源合同替代现有类似期权的容量机制。

Conclusion: 文章讨论了自由化电力系统在同时促进可靠性和竞争性结果之间面临的张力，并就资源充足性机制设计提供了具体政策建议。

Abstract: Liberalized electricity markets often include resource adequacy mechanisms that require consumers to contract with generation resources well in advance of real-time operations. While administratively defined mechanisms have most commonly taken the form of a capacity obligation, efficient markets would feature a broad array of arrangements adapted to the risk profiles and appetites of market participants. This article considers how the financial hedge embedded in alternative resource adequacy contract designs can induce different responses from risk-averse investors, with consequences for the resource mix and market structure. We construct a stochastic equilibrium model describing a competitive market with incomplete risk trading and compute investment equilibria under different contracting regimes. Two policy recommendations result. First, to avoid creating inefficiency by crowding out other forms of risk sharing, system operators should allow resources contracted through other means to opt out of mandatory capacity mechanisms, with their contribution to those requirements subtracted from administratively defined demand curves. Second, if they wish to promote a single contractual form, regulators should consider replacing existing option-like capacity mechanisms with a shaped forward contract for energy. Beyond these recommendations, we discuss the tension that liberalized systems face in seeking to promote both reliability and competitive outcomes.

</details>

### [73] [Quality Diversity for Reliable Data Driven Time-Use Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05230)
*Aneta Neumann,Ty Stanford,Dorothea Dumuid,Frank Neumann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个不确定性量化的质量多样性（QD）框架，用于日常时间使用分配推荐。该框架基于成分数据分析从超过1000名儿童的大型队列数据中构建目标函数，将预测不确定性直接嵌入QD优化过程中，在最大化健康收益的同时平衡模型置信度，从而生成更可靠的时间使用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每日24小时时间预算的分配与身体健康、心理健康和认知健康密切相关。现有预测模型虽然能估计时间使用组成与健康结果（如BMI、生活满意度、认知）之间的关系，但大多数优化方法仅关注最大化预期收益，忽视了数据驱动预测中固有的不确定性。忽略健康相关决策中的不确定性会导致不现实的时间使用建议。

Method: 1) 使用成分数据分析（compositional data analysis）从大型儿童队列数据集（n>1000）中推导目标函数，捕捉日常活动组成与多种健康指标之间的关系。2) 将预测不确定性纳入质量多样性（QD）优化过程中，平衡预期健康收益与模型置信度。3) 通过基于变量和基于目标的两种行为表征方式探索解空间，揭示不确定性下多样化高质量的时间使用组成及健康结果间的明确关系。

Result: 该框架能够探索解空间以揭示多样化高质量的时间使用组成，并发现不确定性下健康结果之间的明确关系。通过将不确定性直接嵌入优化，时间使用建议向低不确定性区域偏移，同时保留高质量结构。

Conclusion: 该框架将时间使用建议引向更低不确定性的区域，同时保持高质量的结构，为行为健康领域提供更可靠的决策支持。这是首次将不确定性量化引入质量多样性优化用于时间使用推荐的尝试，推动了行为健康决策中可靠性方面的进展。

Abstract: The daily allocation of the finite 24-hour time budget is strongly associated with physical, mental, and cognitive health. While predictive models can estimate the relationship between time-use compositions and health outcomes such as body mass index, life satisfaction, and cognition, most optimization approaches focus only on maximizing expected benefit and do not consider the uncertainty inherent in data-driven prediction. Ignoring uncertainty in health-related decisions can lead to unrealistic time-use recommendations. To address this gap, we introduce an uncertainty quantification Quality Diversity (QD) framework for a more reliable time-use recommendation. Objective functions are derived using compositional data analysis using a large child cohort dataset n > 1000, to capture the relationship between daily activity compositions and multiple health indicators. We develop a new approach that incorporates predictive uncertainty into QD processes and produces more reliable recommendations that balance the expected health benefits with the confidence of the model. We explore the solution space through variable-based and objective-based behavioral representations, revealing diverse high-quality time-use composition and explicit relationships between health outcomes under uncertainty. By embedding uncertainty directly into optimization, our framework shifts the time-use recommendations toward regions of lower uncertainty while preserving high-quality structures for more reliable decision-making in behavioral health.

</details>


### [74] [A Unified Causal Inference Framework for the Desirability of Outcome Ranking Paradigm in Benefit-Risk Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05244)
*Yuan Feng,Shiyu Shu,Yixin Fang,Ionut Bebu,Toshimitsu Hamasaki,Scott Evans,Guoqing Diao*

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个统一的协变量调整因果推断框架，用于估算DOOR（结果排名期望度）概率以进行获益-风险评估。该框架将DOOR概率表示为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的双线性泛函，通过序贯风险集危险函数估计条件有序分布，并推导了DOOR概率的有效影响函数（EIF）。模拟研究表明TMLE-SL点估计性能最佳，CVTMLE-SL在推断性能上整体最强，并在抗菌耐药性领导组的耐药菌网络数据上进行了应用演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机试验和观察性研究中，获益-风险评估需要综合考虑疗效和安全性结局。DOOR概率是非参数方法下评估治疗策略整体获益风险的一种有用工具，但现有方法对协变量调整的处理不足，缺乏统一的因果推断框架来有效地估计DOOR概率并构造可靠推断。

Method: 将DOOR概率表示为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的双线性泛函；通过序贯风险集危险函数估计条件有序结局分布；推导DOOR概率的有效影响函数（EIF）；点估计比较G-computation、IPW、AIPW和TMLE；推断采用AIPW-SL和TMLE-SL（含/不含交叉拟合），在重叠程度、治疗效应异质性、治疗分配等不同设置下评估。

Result: 点估计模拟中，TMLE-SL表现最强且最一致，AIPW-SL次之。推断评估中，CVTMLE-SL在DOOR尺度偏差、底层有序分布恢复、标准误精度和置信区间覆盖率方面整体表现最佳。

Conclusion: 所提出的协变量调整因果推断框架为DOOR概率估计提供了有效且稳健的工具，CVTMLE-SL是推荐的最优估计与推断策略，适用于随机试验和观察性研究中的获益-风险评估，并通过抗菌耐药性领导组数据进行了实际应用验证。

Abstract: We developed a unified covariate-adjusted causal inference framework for estimating the desirability of outcome ranking (DOOR) probability for benefit-risk evaluation in randomized trials and observational studies. The framework expresses the DOOR probability as a bilinear functional of the marginal ordinal outcome distributions under the two treatment strategies, estimates conditional ordinal distributions through sequential risk-set hazards, and derives the efficient influence function (EIF) of the DOOR probability. The point-estimation simulations compared G-computation, normalized inverse probability weighting (IPW), augmented IPW (AIPW), and targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE), with nuisance functions estimated using generalized linear models or Super Learner (SL). TMLE-SL showed the strongest and most consistent point-estimation performance, with AIPW-SL ranking second. EIF-based inference was then evaluated for AIPW-SL and TMLE-SL, with and without cross-fitting, across settings varying in overlap, treatment-effect heterogeneity, and treatment allocation. CVTMLE-SL showed the strongest overall performance across DOOR-scale bias, recovery of the underlying ordinal distributions, standard-error accuracy, and confidence-interval coverage. We illustrate the methodology using data from the multidrug-resistant organism network of the Antibacterial Resistance Leadership Group.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验采样法（ESM）数据存在三大挑战：标准统计方法无法扩展至高维场景；机器学习方法因缺失数据（尤其随机缺失）导致选择偏差而结果有偏；现有方法难以灵活建模数据的均值与相关结构。

Method: 开发了一种新的神经网络架构，将混合效应模型的固定效应与随机效应对应推广到深度学习框架中，实现半参数化灵活建模。估计方法结合了变分自编码器（VAE）的改编版与贝叶斯数据增强算法，可适应任意分布的纵向结局、高维扩展以及在随机缺失数据下提供有效推断。

Result: 将该模型应用于GrowIt!研究和多种模拟实验中，结果显示模型具有应用潜力，但由于模型不稳定性，性能仍欠佳（次优表现）。

Conclusion: 深度广义混合模型为ESM数据的高维与非正态纵向分析提供了新思路，但模型稳定性仍需改进，是未来研究方向。

Abstract: The experience sampling method (ESM) is a longitudinal research design where participants report their thoughts, emotional states and behaviours multiple times a day. Our work is motivated by such data collected by the GrowIt! app, which was released to investigate daily emotions among adolescents during the COVID-19 pandemic. Current procedures to analyse ESM data face various challenges. While standard statistical techniques may not scale well to a high-dimensional setting, machine learning procedures can give biased results due to selection bias introduced by missingness. In our motivating dataset, adolescents dropped out due to previous strong feelings of negative emotions. Hence, the implied missing data are of the missing-at-random type that standard machine learning procedures cannot accommodate. We develop a novel neural network architecture that generalises mixed effects models to deep learning to overcome these challenges. It allows semi-parametric and flexible modelling of data's mean and correlation structure through fixed and random effects. For estimation, we use an adaptation of variational auto-encoders and a Bayesian data augmentation algorithm. Through this approach, the model can accommodate longitudinal outcomes following generic distributions, scale well to high-dimensional settings and provide valid inference when data are missing-at-random. We applied the Deep Generalised Mixed Model to the GrowIt! study and various simulations. The results show potential for the Deep Generalised Mixed Model, yet suboptimal performance due to model instability.

</details>

*Taiane Schaedler Prass,Alisson Silva Neimaier,Guilherme Pumi*
