TL;DR: 本文提出了三种将概率回归树（PRTree）扩展以直接处理缺失预测变量的策略，无需事先插补，并保持概率守恒和边际兼容性等核心性质。实验表明缺失值处理策略对预测性能影响最大，在高缺失率数据集上优于CART。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归树处理缺失值通常需要事先插补或使用代理分裂，而PRTree作为平滑且一致的回归树替代方案，尚未被扩展以直接处理缺失预测变量。作者希望在不依赖事先插补的前提下，将PRTree的概率框架推广到任意缺失模式下。

Method: 提出三种处理缺失值的策略：(1) 均匀概率方法（uniform-probability）；(2) 部分观测方法（partial-observation）；(3) 降维平滑方法（dimension-reduced smoothing）。这些修改被设计为保持原始方法的概率守恒和边际兼容性等基本概率性质。

Result: 在多个具有不同缺失程度的真实数据集上，所提方法与经典回归树CART进行了对比。结果显示：缺失值填充（fill）策略是主导建模组件，其影响往往大于平滑分布或代理选择准则；在缺失比例较高的数据集中，所提方法常优于CART，同时保持树模型的可解释性和灵活性。

Conclusion: PRTree框架可以被有效扩展以直接处理缺失数据，缺失观测的处理方式对概率树构建的效果有显著影响。填充策略的选择至关重要，所提方法在缺失严重时比CART更具优势。

Abstract: Probabilistic Regression Trees (PRTrees) are a smooth and consistent alternative to classical regression trees, producing continuous predictions through probabilistic split assignments. This paper extends the PRTree framework to accommodate missing predictor values directly during tree construction, eliminating the need for prior imputation. Three strategies are proposed, each exploiting the available information differently: a uniform-probability approach, a partial-observation approach, and a dimension-reduced smoothing approach. These modifications are defined to preserve the fundamental probabilistic properties of the original methodology, including probability conservation and marginal compatibility, under arbitrary patterns of missing covariate values. The proposed methods are evaluated on several real-world datasets exhibiting different levels of missingness and are compared with classical regression trees. The results show that the effectiveness of probabilistic tree construction depends strongly on the treatment of missing observations. Across the considered datasets, the fill strategy emerged as the dominant modeling component, often exerting a larger influence on predictive performance than either the smoothing distribution or the proxy-selection criterion. In datasets where a substantial proportion of observations contained missing predictor values, the proposed methods frequently outperformed CART, while maintaining the interpretability and flexibility of tree-based models.

</details>


### [77] [Beyond Marginal Validity: Finite-Sample Guarantees for Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06206)
*Anton Conrad,Rustam Isaev,Denis Belomestny,Eric Moulines,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对随机局部化共形预测（RLCP）方法，提供了首个有限样本下的统一局部保证：在Hölder正则性、密度和核假设下，证明了条件覆盖率缺口和相对于oracle的长度误差的高概率界，并分解为局部化偏差与校准项，阐明了带宽偏差-方差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测提供边际覆盖保证，但边际有效性可能掩盖严重的协变量特定偏差，而精确的无分布条件覆盖在有限样本下不可实现。RLCP通过在测试点附近校准来缓解这一差距，但现有理论缺乏对实现局部化集合的有限样本保证，无法同时控制条件有效性和oracle效率。

Method: 在Hölder正则条件下，对固定分数（score）证明关于条件覆盖率缺口和长度误差（相对于oracle）的高概率界，这些界对实现的局部化邻域一致成立；进一步分析数据分割学习分数（如共形分位回归中目标枢轴分数）的情形，将统一局部保证分解为固定分数校准和统一分数估计误差。

Result: 推导出的界可分解为O(h^β)的局部化偏差项和随校准规模递减的校准项，从而清晰刻画带宽偏差-方差权衡及RLCP何时追踪oracle；当学习分数趋近枢轴分数时，改进的学习可以锐化局部化保证。

Conclusion: 本文填补了RLCP有限样本局部保证的理论空白，为条件覆盖率与oracle效率提供了统一的高概率界，并揭示了学习分数质量对局部化保证的提升效应。

Abstract: Conformal prediction endows arbitrary black-box predictors with finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage, yet marginal validity can hide severe covariate-specific miscalibration, while exact distribution-free conditional coverage is finite-sample unattainable. Randomly localized conformal prediction (RLCP) mitigates this gap by calibrating near the test point while preserving marginal coverage. Existing theory, however, lacks finite-sample guarantees for the realized localized set that jointly control conditional validity and oracle efficiency. We provide such guarantees. For any fixed score, under Hölder regularity of the conditional score CDF and standard density and kernel assumptions, we prove high-probability bounds, uniform over a realized localization neighbourhood, for the conditional-coverage gap and the length error relative to the oracle. The bounds decompose into an $O(h^β)$ localization bias and a calibration term decreasing with calibration size, clarifying the bandwidth bias-variance tradeoff and when RLCP tracks the oracle. We also analyze data-split learned scores: when the score targets a pivotal score, as in conformalized quantile regression, uniform local guarantees decompose into fixed-score calibration and uniform score-estimation errors, showing that improved learning sharpens localized guarantees.

</details>


### [78] [Minimax Optimal Early-Stopped Gradient Descent for Gaussian Mixture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06250)
*Alex Buna,Shirley Xiaoqi Liu,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化分类中，早停（early stopping）如何克服梯度下降隐式偏差导致的统计次优性。在带标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型中，适当停时的梯度下降能达到极小极大最优的0-1风险率，且比线性插值器指数级地节省样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化分类中训练数据可线性可分但实际分布不可分时，逻辑损失上的梯度下降在范数上发散、方向上收敛到最大间隔分类器，其隐式偏差可能是统计次优的。作者希望证明早停可以克服这种次优性。

Method: (1) 在高斯混合模型+标签翻转噪声下分析早停的梯度下降迭代；(2) 结合早停迭代的紧上界与任意分类器上的匹配统计下界；(3) 提出新的校准结果，将过剩逻辑风险转换为过剩0-1风险，处理标签翻转噪声导致的模型误设，并移除标准界的平方根速率；(4) 建立线性插值器的下界以对比样本复杂度。

Result: 早停的梯度下降在快速连续衰减的协方差谱（包括多项式和指数谱衰减）下实现极小极大最优的过剩0-1风险；新校准结果消除了平方根速率；线性插值器需要指数级更多样本才能达到相同过剩风险。

Conclusion: 早停在过参数化分类中可以有效克服最大间隔插值分类器的统计次优性，为实践中早停策略的有效性提供了理论依据，并通过实验验证了最优率的正确性。

Abstract: In overparameterised classification, training data can be linearly separable even when the underlying distribution is not. In this setting, gradient descent (GD) on the logistic loss diverges in norm while converging in direction to a max-margin interpolating classifier, whose implicit bias can be statistically suboptimal. In this work, we show that early stopping can overcome this suboptimality: in a Gaussian mixture model with label-flipping noise, GD stopped at an appropriate oracle time achieves minimax-optimal excess zero-one risk for covariance spectra with fast and continuous decay, including polynomial and exponential spectral decays. Our analysis combines a sharp upper bound for the early-stopped iterate with a matching statistical lower bound over arbitrary classifiers, yielding optimal rates that are validated by experiments. A central technical contribution is a new calibration result that converts excess logistic risk into excess zero-one risk; it handles the model misspecification induced by the label-flipping noise, and removes the square-root rate in standard bounds. We also establish a lower bound for linear interpolators, showing that interpolation can require exponentially more samples than early stopping to achieve the same excess risk.

</details>


### [79] [Stochastic Dynamics on Persistence Diagram Space via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06276)
*Farzana Nasrin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于持久性图（PD）空间上随机动力学的强化学习框架。通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作，定义在有限PD空间（可变基数）上的受控马尔可夫过程，并证明了该马尔可夫链的不可约性、非周期性和几何遍历性，保证PD空间上唯一平稳概率律的存在。通过设计涵盖分布匹配、任务特定拓扑统计和结构保持压缩的目标函数，指导动力学朝向科学相关的拓扑目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PD统计分析大多将图视为静态对象，缺乏对PD空间上概率建模和随机演化的框架。作者希望填补这一空白，为PD提供可演化的、概率性的建模工具。

Method: 1) 将PD演化建模为受控马尔可夫过程，通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作（增删改）驱动；2) 从理论上证明马尔可夫链满足不可约性、非周期性和几何遍历性，从而存在唯一平稳分布；3) 设计多目标奖励函数，平衡分布匹配、任务相关拓扑统计和图保真度/复杂度缩减；4) 在合成数据与神经影像PD上进行实验验证。

Result: 在合成数据和神经影像PD上的实验表明，该框架能够在降低图复杂度的同时保留主导拓扑结构，验证了其用于自适应拓扑简化和概率建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为PD空间上的随机动力学提供了第一个强化学习框架，兼具理论保证（遍历性、平稳分布存在性）与实用价值（拓扑简化、分布匹配），为动态拓扑数据分析开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams (PDs) provide stable and interpretable summaries of multiscale topological structure. While substantial progress has been made in the statistical analysis of PDs, existing literature often treats diagrams as static objects and provide limited frameworks for probabilistic modeling and stochastic evolution on PD space. We introduce a reinforcement learning framework for stochastic dynamics on PD space, where diagrams evolve through topology aware local edit operations. The dynamics define controlled Markov processes on spaces of finite PDs with variable cardinality. We establish conditions under which the induced Markov chains are irreducible, aperiodic, and geometrically ergodic, implying the existence of unique stationary probability laws on PD space. To guide the dynamics toward scientifically relevant topological targets, we formulate objectives that encompass distribution matching, task specific topological statistics, and structure-preserving compression. The resulting rewards balance task specific distributional targets, diagram fidelity, and complexity reduction, and yield a framework for adaptive topological simplification and probabilistic modeling. Experiments on synthetic and neuroimaging PDs demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve dominant topological structure while reducing diagram complexity.

</details>


### [80] [Optimal Rates for Learning with Monotone Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06337)
*Anay Mehrotra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了单调对手（monotone adversary）模型下的学习问题：对手在干净样本中插入少量正确标记的额外样本，但插入依赖于原始样本，破坏了可交换性。作者证明对于VC维d≥2的类别，最小最大期望误差为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))，即额外对数因子是本质性的；而在d=1时仍为Θ(1/n)。相同的下界也适用于Littlestone维，表明干净的在线到批量学习速率无法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Larsen等人引入单调对手模型，发现经验风险最小化达到O((d/n)log(n/d))，而所有已知的最优学习器都可能被推离PAC最优的Θ(d/n)速率。核心悬而未决的问题是：这个额外的对数因子是特定算法的局限，还是缺乏可交换性的内在代价？本文旨在回答这一根本性问题。

Method: 作者通过构造一个显式的类别和先验分布来建立下界，其中两个目标假设在非零质量点上不同，但产生相同样本，从而使任何学习器都无法区分。上界则通过改进留一法（leave-one-out）论证，设计了一个简单的非适当学习器来达到一维情形的Θ(1/n)速率，推广了一包含图（one-inclusion graph）的分析框架。

Result: 在最坏情况下，VC维d=1时最小最大期望误差为Θ(1/n)，d≥2时为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))。相同结论对Littlestone维d_L成立，说明在线到批量的干净速率O(d_L/n)也不可达到。所有下界来自统一构造：显式类别和先验，其中两个仅在非零质量点不同的目标假设产生相同样本。

Conclusion: 添加正确标记的样本可以使学习变得更难（多出对数因子），这一现象是本质性的而非算法伪影，即使对于具有有限错误界的在线学习类别也是如此。这揭示了缺乏可交换性对学习复杂度产生的根本性影响，并回答了Larsen等人提出的开放问题。

Abstract: A monotone adversary observes an i.i.d. labeled sample and appends a finite number of further examples of its choice, every one of them labeled correctly by the target hypothesis. The learner sees a uniform shuffle of the combined sample and is scored on the original distribution. Every example is correctly labeled, but the insertions depend on the clean sample, so the combined sample is not exchangeable. Larsen, Pabbaraju, and Shetty, who introduced this model, showed that empirical risk minimization attains expected error $O((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for classes of VC dimension $d$, and that every known optimal learner can be pushed away from the $Θ(d/n)$ rate, optimal for PAC learning. They asked whether the extra logarithm is an artifact of those particular algorithms or an inherent consequence of the lack of exchangeability.
  We show that this additional cost is inherent beyond VC dimension one. In the worst case over classes of VC dimension $d$ and over known finite insertion budgets, the minimax expected error is $Θ(1/n)$ at $d=1$ and $Θ((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for $d\geq 2$. The same rates hold with Littlestone dimension $d_{\mathrm L}$ in place of $d$, so the clean online-to-batch rate $O(d_{\mathrm L}/n)$ is unattainable as well. Thus, somewhat counterintuitively, adding correctly labeled examples can make learning harder by a logarithmic factor, even for classes that admit finite mistake bounds in online learning. The dimension-one upper bound is achieved by a simple improper learner whose analysis adapts the leave-one-out argument underlying the one-inclusion graph. All of our lower bounds are elementary and come from a single construction: an explicit class and prior on which two target hypothesis, which differ a point of nonnegligible mass, produce the same sample.

</details>


### [81] [Scalable estimation of VARMA models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06340)
*Daniel Paulin,Victor Elvira*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套VARMA模型估计框架，通过偏自相关重参数化保证平稳性和可逆性，利用Parseval（傅里叶）恒等式将损失函数转化为仅依赖定长充分统计量的形式，使每次优化迭代的计算成本与序列长度T无关。该框架提供两种点估计器（正则化最小二乘和协方差边缘化的最大后验估计），均能以近参数速率恢复真实过程的无限自回归表示。框架同样适用于季节动态、外生回归变量（VARMAX）和滚动窗口重拟合。实验表明，在d=10到40维度下，估计器接近Oracle预测误差（经典条件MLE在此范围内会失效），并在零售需求、气象和空气质量数据上匹配或超越VAR、贝叶斯VAR、分量式ARMA和稀疏VARMA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VARMA模型具有用少量参数捕捉纯自回归需要大量滞后才能匹配的动态特性，但长期以来被认为在中等维度以上不实用，主要障碍包括：似然函数非凸、参数化仅在等价类下可辨识、每次评估需要遍历整个序列。传统纯VAR模型虽然计算方便，但需要大量滞后才能匹配VARMA的动态表达能力，这促使研究者寻找能消除VARMA计算障碍的估计框架。

Method: 该框架结合三个关键组件：(1) 偏自相关重参数化，从构造上保证平稳性和可逆性；(2) 对重参数化系数施加高斯先验，对角项和非对角项分别设置不同的尺度；(3) 通过Parseval（傅里叶）恒等式，将损失函数转化为只依赖固定大小充分统计量的形式，以截断长度的近线性成本求值。由此得到两种点估计器：正则化最小二乘拟合和协方差边缘化最大后验估计器。该机制可推广至季节动态、外生回归（VARMAX）及滚动窗口重拟合场景。

Result: 理论结果：在固定维度下，两种估计器均以近参数速率收敛到真实过程的无限自回归表示，截断不会引入渐近偏差。实证结果：在d=10到d=40的维度范围内，估计器保持接近Oracle预测误差（而经典条件MLE返回不可逆拟合导致预测发散）；在零售需求、气象和空气质量数据集上匹配或超越VAR、贝叶斯VAR、分量式ARMA和稀疏VARMA基线。

Conclusion: 该框架成功消除了VARMA估计的计算障碍，使每次迭代成本与序列长度无关，将似然型VARMA估计带到了实际从业者以往只能依赖VAR模型的规模。这有望推动VARMA模型在实际高维时序预测任务中的广泛应用。

Abstract: Vector autoregressive moving-average (VARMA) models have long been considered impractical beyond moderate dimensions: the likelihood is non-convex, the parametrization is identified only up to equivalence, and every evaluation costs a pass over the entire series. Yet their moving-average term captures with a few parameters what a pure autoregression matches only with many lags. We introduce an estimation framework that removes this computational barrier: each optimization iteration is independent of the series length $T$. The framework combines a partial-autocorrelation reparametrization that guarantees stationarity and invertibility by construction, Gaussian priors on the reparametrized coefficients with separate scales for diagonal and off-diagonal entries, and losses that depend on the data only through fixed-size sufficient statistics, evaluated by a Parseval (Fourier) identity at near-linear cost in the truncation length. This yields two point estimators: a regularized least-squares fit and a covariance-marginalized maximum-a-posteriori estimator. We prove that both recover the infinite-autoregressive representation of the true process at a near-parametric rate in fixed dimension, so the truncation introduces no asymptotic bias. The same machinery extends, at the same leading cost, to seasonal dynamics, exogenous regressors (VARMAX), and rolling-window refits. Empirically, the estimators stay close to the oracle forecast error from $d=10$ to $d=40$ (where classical conditional MLE returns non-invertible fits whose forecasts diverge) and match or beat VAR, Bayesian-VAR, component-wise ARMA, and sparse-VARMA baselines on retail-demand, meteorological, and air-quality data. This brings likelihood-based VARMA estimation, at a per-iteration cost independent of the series length, to the problem sizes where practitioners have so far relied on VAR models.

</details>

### [82] [Cross-Sectional Heterogeneity in LSTM Networks for Financial Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05755)
*Julius Döbelt*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一种扩展LSTM架构，通过集成宏观金融协变量和可学习的行业嵌入（sector embeddings）来预测S&P 500成分股的日度收益方向。实证表明该模型在风险与收益指标上优于基础LSTM、随机森林和买入持有基准，并利用潜在空间可视化和贡献度量提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融资产收益预测因低信噪比和半强式市场有效性而极具挑战。虽然LSTM等深度学习模型能捕捉时间依赖，但标准架构难以处理资产收益的横截面异质性（即不同行业/股票之间的差异性），导致预测精度受限且缺乏可解释性。

Method: 提出一种新颖的LSTM架构扩展：(1) 集成宏观金融协变量以捕捉更广泛的经济信号；(2) 引入可学习的行业嵌入（sector embeddings）以纳入行业层面的横截面异质性；(3) 基于日度方向性预测构建多空组合交易策略；(4) 使用潜在空间可视化分析模型如何区分行业；(5) 提出新的贡献度量通过检查LSTM权重来量化行业信息的影响。

Result: 实证结果显示，带行业嵌入的LSTM模型在关键风险和收益指标上优于所有基准（基础LSTM、随机森林、买入持有策略）。预测信号主要由短期反转因子和行业动量因子驱动。潜在空间可视化展示了模型对行业的内部表征区分能力。

Conclusion: 行业嵌入的引入使LSTM模型能够显式地整合横截面异质性，适应市场内不同行业的动态变化，同时通过潜在空间可视化和贡献度量缓解了深度学习黑箱问题，提升了模型的可解释性。该框架为深度学习方法在金融横截面预测中的应用提供了一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Predicting financial asset returns remains one of the most difficult challenges in empirical finance, driven by the low signal-to-noise ratio and the semi-strong form of market efficiency. While deep learning models, especially LSTM networks, have shown promise in capturing temporal dependencies, standard architectures often struggle to account for the cross-sectional heterogeneity of asset returns. This paper proposes a novel architectural extension to the basic LSTM model designed to improve both predictive accuracy and model interpretability. The framework integrates macro-financial covariates to capture broader economic signals and learnable sector embeddings to encompass heterogeneity by sector. The trading strategy involves constructing a long-short portfolio based on daily directional forecasts for each S&P 500 constituent, targeting stocks expected to under- or outperform the cross-sectional median return of the S&P 500. Model Performance is evaluated against three competitive benchmarks: a basic LSTM, a Random Forest model and a traditional market buy-and-hold strategy. The empirical results demonstrate that the LSTM with sector embeddings outperforms all benchmarks across key risk and return metrics. By utilizing sector embeddings, the model explicitly incorporates cross-sectional heterogeneity, allowing it to adapt to varying industry dynamics within the market. To address the black-box nature of deep learning, I use latent space visualizations to analyse how the model differentiates between sectors, providing insights into the internal representation of the sectors in the LSTM. The impact of the sector information can be quantified using a novel contribution metric by inspecting the weights of the LSTM. The predictive signal is driven by a short-term reversal factor and an industry momentum factor.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MS-MLB，一个可复现的开源基准测试，用于从全血RNA表达数据中进行多发性硬化症（MS）与健康对照的分类研究。该基准使用GSE17048公共数据集，采用防泄漏的流水线（嵌套交叉验证、独立holdout集、bootstrap置信区间等）评估多种机器学习算法，最终梯度提升模型在holdout集上取得MS Research Score 93.83、AUC-ROC 0.989的成绩。这是首个聚焦于该任务且内置外部模型提交流程的开源基准，但仅供研究比较，未经临床验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性硬化症的诊断依赖临床评估、影像学和实验室证据，过程复杂且缺乏简便的生物标志物。血液RNA表达数据可能包含疾病相关的免疫信号，但缺乏一个统一、可复现的基准来公平评估机器学习模型在该任务上的表现，现有研究各自为政，难以比较。

Method: 利用公共GSE17048队列构建MS与健康对照的二分类任务；设计共享且防泄漏的评估流水线，包括嵌套交叉验证、独立分层holdout测试集、bootstrap置信区间、ROC/PR分析、校准度量以及探索性的MS Research Score；框架内还内置了外部模型提交的文档化路径。

Result: 梯度提升在holdout集上排名第一：MS Research Score为93.83，AUC-ROC为0.989，灵敏度0.950，特异性0.778，F1分数0.927，Brier分数0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB是首个针对GSE17048全血RNA数据中MS与健康对照分类的开源基准，提供了可复现、防泄漏的评估框架和外部模型提交流程。该基准旨在促进研究层面的方法比较，尚未经过临床验证，不能替代临床诊断。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [84] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFTER框架，通过挖掘冻结预测模型的残差中的可解释特征来驱动轻量级事后修正器，大幅提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测模型常以结构化、可重复的方式犯错，而通过微调修复这些错误成本高昂。因此需要一种轻量级的、基于特征挖掘的事后修正方法来提升模型表现。

Method: CRAFTER保持骨干网络冻结，通过两种互补的生成器挖掘其残差：一种是对原始输入通道的组合搜索，另一种是LLM提出命名特征组合、二元标志和简短可执行代码。所有候选特征通过单一的验证集门槛被接受或拒绝，最后由验证集选择的修正器应用被接受的特征或保持预测不变。这种与来源无关的管道使先前的特征工程系统能在相同条件下评估。

Result: 在六个公开数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上，CRAFTER在每种特征预算下都超越了所有专用特征工程系统，将修正器单独改进的效果大约翻倍，并将最弱骨干网络的误差降低高达27%。这些改进在不同LLM后端下都很稳健，且对微调后的骨干网络依然有效。

Conclusion: CRAFTER通过建模"模型失败过程"而非"数据生成过程"来发现修正性特征，是一种有效的、源无关的事后修正框架，可作为评估特征来源贡献的工具。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [85] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于编程LLM流程的概率性语言，使开发者能够量化和传播整个应用流程中的不确定性，并通过实验研究和定理证明智能体的案例研究验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建基于LLM的可靠应用仍面临重大挑战：LLM输出缺乏准确性和置信度度量，特别是在多步调用LLM和其他工具的流程中，不确定性会累积放大，导致开发者和最终用户难以信任结果。

Method: 提出一种概率性语言用于编程基于LLM的流程，使开发者能够量化和传播整个流程中的不确定性，并允许在不添加额外代码（除流程逻辑外）的情况下实验不同的推理缩放技术。

Result: 通过实验研究展示了该能力，并开展了一个构建Rocq定理证明器智能体的案例研究。

Conclusion: 该概率性语言为解决LLM应用中的不确定性传播问题提供了一种新范式，使开发者能够更有效地构建可信赖的LLM应用。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [86] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGTime模型，通过将感知与描述解耦来解决多模态时间序列与语言对齐训练中的自监督陷阱。确定性计算从真实多变量时间序列中提取统计量，LLM仅负责将预计算结果语言化，避免模型从自身局限中学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练方法存在两个核心问题：(1) 自监督陷阱——让LLM读序列写描述，标签质量受限于模型本身的感知能力，数据永远无法教会模型超出标注者已知的内容；(2) 单变量局限——大多数数据集只用单变量，但重要的模式（跨通道相关性、领先滞后结构、共现异常）需要多变量才能体现，而这恰是LLM感知最弱的领域。由此产生三难困境：现有方法在可靠性、真实感、可扩展性三者中无法兼得。

Method: 核心思想是解耦感知与描述。用确定性代码从真实开源多变量时间序列中计算统计量集合，再由LLM将这些预计算事实进行语言化表达。感知（LLM不擅长的部分）由计算完成，表达（LLM擅长的部分）由LLM完成。由此构建4B参数的CGTime模型。

Result: 在多元时间序列理解任务上，CGTime超越远大于自身的通用模型：多元事实评分0.283，优于GPT-4o-mini的0.173和GPT-5.4-nano的0.203，且通过Holm校正配对显著性检验。生成的描述中包含更准确的可验证数值事实，覆盖更广泛的统计属性范围。

Conclusion: 通过计算与语言的分离，可以打破多模态时间序列训练的感知上限瓶颈，以较小的模型规模在专业任务上超越大规模通用模型，同时保证生成内容的可验证性和事实准确性。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [87] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiSR框架，通过将3D感知与推理显式分离（利用现成感知模型重建结构化3D证据，再用LoRA微调LLM进行推理），在不进行大规模3D VQA训练的情况下，在空间推理基准上取得有竞争力的性能，同时具备更好的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的端到端训练方式需要大规模训练才能同时获得隐式的3D感知和推理能力。作者观察到现代感知模型擅长连续3D几何估计，而LLM擅长组合和符号推理，因此希望利用两者的互补优势，避免大规模训练的成本。

Method: 提出可分离空间推理器（DiSR）：1）使用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化的3D证据；2）使用LoRA技术微调LLM，使其仅基于显式的几何证据进行推理。无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂的工具使用策略。

Result: 在流行的空间推理基准上取得了有竞争力的性能，同时提供了更好的可解释性、模块化和计算效率，证明显式分离感知与推理是可扩展且有效的替代范式。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知和推理是端到端建模的一种可扩展且有效的替代方案，为空间智能提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [88] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因子化生成模型中常见的错误假设：将潜在风格变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到高斯先验并不能保证其与类别信息独立。作者推导出因子化采样需要满足四个条件中的精确分解，并通过实验证明消除边缘分布不匹配是必要但不充分的。大量实验表明即使在全局MMD接近零的情况下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率从风格潜在变量中恢复类别标签，证明了基于边缘分布的度量无法验证类别独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型通常通过将风格潜在变量的边缘分布正则化为固定高斯先验来声称风格表示与类别信息独立，但这在数学上是不成立的——边缘分布匹配对类条件分布没有任何约束，允许风格潜变量仍然高度预测类别标签。这种错误解释广泛存在于现有文献中，需要被纠正。

Method: 作者推导出因子化采样所需的四个条件的精确分解，证明边缘分布匹配只是其中之一。通过案例研究模型和四个代表性潜在基线进行实证分析，使用全局MMD评估边缘分布匹配度，同时用线性探针衡量类别泄露。还测试了六种扰动（模型容量、课程学习、先验几何、监督等）下的鲁棒性，并评估四种缓解策略及两种后处理方案（条件先验和经验风格库）。

Result: 五个模型在近零全局MMD下仍允许线性探针以74%-100%准确率恢复类别（10%为随机水平）。模型达到99.15%聚类准确率但外部评估的类条件生成仅16%成功率。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%但未改变类内依赖。后验条件先验在MNIST上达到0.97类条件生成分数，但在CIFAR-10上仅0.41；经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格潜在变量边缘分布的散度计算无法认证与类别标签的独立性，仅报告边缘统计量不能验证因子化生成模型中通常声称的性质。该发现对因子化生成模型的评估和设计具有重要方法论意义。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [89] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架和PRISM-Eval基准，研究多模态模型对"规则理解"（rubric comprehension）的能力，即模型作为执行者而非生成者，按照带优先级的规则集对图像进行验证并输出整体判断。仅用10K合成样本即可将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，且增益可迁移至其他MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的多模态指令往往捆绑多个重要性不等的要求，但大多数多模态训练数据仍将指令跟随简化为回答单个独立问题。这一差距导致模型缺乏对多规则、优先级感知的指令执行能力。

Method: 提出PRISM，一个四阶段数据合成框架：(1) persona-task pairs；(2) prefix-guided rule sets；(3) quality-filtered rubrics；(4) structured verification traces。同时引入PRISM-Eval基准，包含Loose和Strict两种度量，使用确定性匹配对固定标签，无需推理时评判模型。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准的平均表现。增益可迁移至四种额外的开源MLLM，覆盖稠密和MoE架构。

Conclusion: 结构化规则监督是通往多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令跟随的可扩展路径，表明"规则理解"这一设置值得更广泛的关注与探索。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT是一个参数高效的过拟合感知多任务微调框架，通过将调度器状态从全模型检查点压缩为可合并的适配器状态，冻结预训练骨干并仅训练注入的适配器，在五个骨干网络上均取得比msft更高的平均准确率（61.36% vs 59.85%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务SFT将异构数据混合视为单一优化问题，但不同任务在不同时间达到最佳泛化。现有msft方法通过任务级展开、排除和回滚解决这一问题，但其原始方案将调度器状态物化为全模型检查点，导致阶段转换的存储、恢复和部署成本高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练骨干，仅训练注入的适配器，在任务级峰值时回滚适配器检查点并在剩余活跃混合上继续训练。在层级别，每个适配器采用AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层应用于低秩权重因子而非样本表征，使更新保持输入线性、秩有界，且可精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留骨干比较协议下，AuroSFT平均准确率达61.36%，超过msft参考行的59.85%，并在全部五个骨干网络上获得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT通过参数高效的适配器状态替代全模型检查点，在降低调度器状态存储与部署成本的同时提升了多任务SFT的性能，验证了可合并适配器状态在过拟合感知多任务微调中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroOFT 在量化正交微调（qoft）基础上引入零初始化的门控低秩非线性残差分支，在保持量化兼容性的同时增强了模型的输入依赖非线性校正能力，相比 qoft 提升 Macro-6 达 1.30-2.70%，并大幅减少可训练参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qoft 通过学习结构化的激活旋转实现低比特语言模型的参数高效微调，但它的任务特定更新局限于线性正交变换，缺乏输入依赖的非线性矫正能力，限制了模型的表达力和微调性能。

Method: AuroOFT 保留 qoft 作为稳定的量化兼容主干分支，同时为每个适配的线性层附加一个零起始（zero-start）门控低秩非线性残差分支。该分支将激活映射到 RMS 归一化的紧凑隐空间，采用自适应非线性基函数配合有界或与 token 相关的门控。上投影矩阵初始化为零，使得 AuroOFT 在初始化时功能上与 qoft 完全一致，正交性仅作为分支级稳定性属性保留。

Result: 在匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT 在 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 设置上相比 qoft 提升 Macro-6 达 1.30-2.70%，相比 QLoRA 提升 6.52-10.62%，且在代表性规模上比 QLoRA 节省 32.3-44.7% 的可训练参数。

Conclusion: AuroOFT 通过零初始化门控低秩非线性残差分支有效弥补了 qoft 线性约束的不足，在保持量化稳定性的同时显著提升了下游任务性能并降低了参数量开销，为低比特语言模型的高效微调提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [92] [FlowAdam: Implicit Regularization via Geometry-Aware Soft Momentum Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06652)
*Devender Singh,Tarun Sheel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowAdam，一种混合优化器，通过ODE连续梯度流积分增强Adam。当EMA统计量检测到优化困难（如参数强耦合）时，FlowAdam切换为截断的ODE积分，并引入软动量注入机制在模式转换时融合ODE速度与Adam动量，防止训练崩溃。实验表明在低秩矩阵/张量恢复、协同过滤等耦合优化任务上错误率降低6-22%，同时保持良态任务（CIFAR-10）上等效于Adam的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩方法使用对角逐坐标预条件，依赖坐标系统，在处理稠密或旋转参数耦合（如矩阵分解、张量分解、图神经网络）时因独立对待每个参数而表现不佳。

Method: 1) 混合优化器架构：将Adam与连续梯度流的ODE积分相结合；2) 自适应切换机制：通过EMA统计量检测优化景观困难度，据此决定是否切换至截断的ODE积分；3) 软动量注入（Soft Momentum Injection）：在模式转换期间将ODE速度与Adam动量混合，避免朴素混合方法导致的训练崩溃。

Result: 在低秩矩阵/张量恢复任务上held-out误差降低10-22%，在Jester真实协同过滤任务上降低6%，超过调优后的Lion和AdaBelief；在良态任务CIFAR-10上与Adam性能匹配。消融实验表明软注入至关重要——硬替换将准确率从100%降至82.5%。

Conclusion: FlowAdam通过软动量注入巧妙解决了混合优化器在模式转换时的训练崩溃问题，其性能提升源自参数耦合交互而非偏差估计，为处理密集旋转耦合问题提供了一种有效、鲁棒的优化方案。

Abstract: Adaptive moment methods such as Adam use a diagonal, coordinate-wise preconditioner based on exponential moving averages of squared gradients. This diagonal scaling is coordinate-system dependent and can struggle with dense or rotated parameter couplings, including those in matrix factorization, tensor decomposition, and graph neural networks, because it treats each parameter independently. We introduce FlowAdam, a hybrid optimizer that augments Adam with continuous gradient-flow integration via an ordinary differential equation (ODE). When EMA-based statistics detect landscape difficulty, FlowAdam switches to clipped ODE integration. Our central contribution is Soft Momentum Injection, which blends ODE velocity with Adam's momentum during mode transitions. This prevents the training collapse observed with naive hybrid approaches. Across coupled optimization benchmarks, the ODE integration provides implicit regularization, reducing held-out error by 10-22% on low-rank matrix/tensor recovery and 6% on Jester (real-world collaborative filtering), also surpassing tuned Lion and AdaBelief, while matching Adam on well-conditioned workloads (CIFAR-10). MovieLens-100K confirms benefits arise specifically from coupled parameter interactions rather than bias estimation. Ablation studies show that soft injection is essential, as hard replacement reduces accuracy from 100% to 82.5%.

</details>


### [93] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款面向散户投资者的个性化税务感知投资组合管理应用，包含FastAPI后端与Web仪表盘，采用三阶段强化学习系统（自监督跨资产编码器、MoE分配策略与LoRA适配器）将自然语言投资目标路由到六个投资任务之一并生成经纪商实时集成推荐。系统已通过Alpaca纸面交易的端到端集成测试，并附14天前向回测作为部署前验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者无法获得机构客户享有的个性化税务感知投资组合管理：现有robo-advisor仅用静态规则分配，而机构级系统有账户门槛和技术栈壁垒，存在明显的服务缺口。

Method: 采用三阶段强化学习系统：（1）自监督跨资产编码器；（2）带学习意图路由器的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）分配策略；（3）轻量级LoRA适配器，从个人经纪行为个性化推荐而无需重训共享模型。同时构建了FastAPI后端和web仪表盘，支持自然语言目标路由至六个投资任务。

Result: 系统功能完整并通过Alpaca纸面交易API的端到端集成测试，包括多用户认证、预览-确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计操作链。14天前向回测（含自助置信区间）作为初步部署前验证。同时报告了三个工程经验：静默失效的集成路径、挂起的第三方API调用、以及端到端实证验证优于信任checkpoint元数据。

Conclusion: 该系统是尚未部署的早期应用，但已有清晰的完整部署路径。所报告的实践工程教训被认为可推广到其他基于外部实时数据源的应用强化学习系统。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [94] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了梯度下降在因子化模型上隐式偏向低秩解，而Adam不具备这一特性，根本原因在于优化器是否具有规范等变性（gauge-equivariance）。作者通过结构定理和传递定理系统刻画了这一机制，并在矩阵感知、Muon优化器和Transformer实验中验证了基选择对优化器选择的决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降在因子化模型W=UV^T上隐式偏好低秩解，但Adam从相同的小初始化出发却不具备此特性。理解这一差异对于设计更好的优化器以及理解深度学习中隐式偏置的本质至关重要。用户希望探究不同更新规则在低秩恢复能力上的差异及其根本原因。

Method: 作者将差异追溯到损失的规范对称性，即损失在(U,V)→(UQ,VQ)变换下的不变性。关键洞察是：梯度流的低秩机制仅对规范等变的优化器可用，这是低秩恢复的必要但非充分条件。作者提出一个结构定理，刻画了无记忆等变规则恰好等于Gram确定（Gram-determined）的左预条件算子；一个传递定理将梯度流的路径性质传递到公共标量流。随后对九种更新规则在欠定矩阵感知任务上按恢复误差排序，并通过从逐坐标到共享标量的单参数预条件族证明各向异性是导致偏差丧失的原因。提出了"谱调度"来解释关于Muon的两种矛盾报告。最后在Transformer和超光谱数据集上进行验证实验。

Result: 梯度下降、动量、共享标量Adam、Muon和Shampoo满足规范等变性；而Adam、RMSProp及其他逐坐标方法不满足。等变优化器在Transformer中保持两种规范等价初始化的浮点精度一致，而Adam在第一步就区分了它们，最终每头不变量W_Q^T W_K的相对Frobenius距离相差56%。在两组超光谱数据集上，匹配训练损失时，梯度下降在最低采样密度下将留出误差降低43-44%，且在更低的有效秩下表现更好。

Conclusion: 基的选择（basis choice）不是调参细节，而是决定优化器选择何种插值函数（interpolant）的关键决策。各向异性是Adam等逐坐标方法丧失低秩偏差的根源，规范等变性是低秩恢复的必要条件。这一框架统一解释了多种优化器在隐式偏置上的差异，并为设计具有可控隐式偏置的优化器提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


### [95] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN）用于野火后泥石流预测，发现TabPFN在性能上最优（威胁评分为0.637），SHAP分析表明短时降雨强度和风暴累积是主要预测因子，并通过TabPFN生成的合成数据增强提升了多数模型的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火后泥石流预测对灾害防治至关重要，但现有挑战包括：泥石流与非泥石流事件在特征空间重叠、模型可解释性需求、训练数据有限。需要系统评估可靠的机器学习模型来应对这些挑战。

Method: 基于美国西部流域尺度的野火后泥石流观测数据，比较15种模型（含TabPFN），采用重复分层交叉验证评估预测性能；使用SHAP分析特征重要性；利用TabPFN生成合成样本进行数据增强以缓解样本稀缺问题。

Result: TabPFN在未增强数据上取得最高性能（威胁评分0.637），紧随其后的是最佳树模型；SHAP显示短时降雨强度和风暴累积量排名最高，燃烧严重度和地形特征贡献较小；合成数据增强提升了除CNN外所有模型的性能，深度学习模型平均威胁评分提升最大（+0.041）。

Conclusion: 通过结合严格的模型基准测试、可解释的特征分析和合成数据增强，本文提供了一个全面提升野火后泥石流预测能力的综合框架。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models. By combining rigorous model benchmarking, interpretable feature analysis, and synthetic data augmentation, this work provides a comprehensive framework for improving post-wildfire debris-flow prediction.

</details>


### [96] [Rectifying Geometric Misalignment: Online Source-Free Adaptation for Class-Imbalanced EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05315)
*Shiwen Chu,Shanglin Li,Motoaki Kawanabe,Reinmar Kobler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSPDIM，一种基于黎曼流形的无源在线无监督域自适应框架，通过流形约束的偏置参数和信息最大化优化，解决脑机接口中标签偏移导致的几何错位问题，在多个运动想象数据集上显著优于标准黎曼基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电信号驱动的脑机接口（BCI）在不同被试和会话间泛化时，常需要无监督域自适应（UDA）。现有的黎曼对齐方法（如RCT）虽能处理协变量偏移，但隐含假设类别先验平衡。在实际在线BCI场景中，标签分布动态变化（标签偏移），导致标准对齐技术无法正确对齐目标数据分布，因此需要一种能应对标签偏移的在线自适应方法。

Method: OSPDIM在切空间映射中引入流形约束的偏置参数，通过信息最大化（information maximization）优化该参数，以纠正由于不平衡数据流引起的几何偏移。与依赖全局批次统计的离线方法不同，OSPDIM能够在线实时估计和纠正几何偏置，实现无源（source-free）的在线UDA。

Result: 在2D SPD矩阵上的仿真实验直观展示了OSPDIM能有效纠正标准中心化无法解决的错位问题。在多个运动想象数据集上的大量实验表明，OSPDIM在类别严重不平衡的具有挑战性的在线自适应场景中，显著优于标准黎曼基线方法。

Conclusion: OSPDIM为实际可即插即用的BCI系统提供了稳健的解决方案，能够有效处理在线场景下的标签偏移问题，为脑机接口的跨被试/跨会话泛化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) based Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) often require unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) to generalize across subjects and sessions. While Riemannian alignment methods like the Riemannian Centering Transformation (RCT) are effective for handling covariate shifts, they implicitly assume balanced class priors. However, in realistic online BCI scenarios, the label distributions vary dynamically (label shift), causing standard alignment techniques to geometrically misalign the target data distributions. In this work, we propose OSPDIM (Online SPD manifold information maximization), a source-free online UDA framework designed to address label shifts on the Riemannian manifold. OSPDIM introduces a manifold-constrained bias parameter into the tangent space mapping, which is optimized via information maximization to correct the geometric skew caused by imbalanced data streams. Unlike offline methods relying on global batch statistics, OSPDIM estimates and corrects geometric bias on-the-fly. Simulations on 2D SPD matrices visually demonstrate that OSPDIM successfully rectifies the misalignment where standard centering fails. Extensive experiments on multiple motor imagery datasets show that OSPDIM significantly outperforms standard Riemannian baselines, particularly in challenging online adaptation scenarios with severe class imbalance, offering a robust solution for practical, plug-and-play BCI systems.

</details>


### [97] [Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotopes for Identifiable and Refinable Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05454)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合概率Zonotope（HProbZ）输出头，将预测中的三种不确定性来源（离散模式选择、有界系统漂移、不可约随机噪声）分别建模为二元、有界和随机生成器，并通过对卷积获得闭式似然。共享有界生成器使未来预测代数耦合，单步观测即可在前向传播中精化所有剩余步的预测分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率预测头要么输出高斯混合，要么输出单一保形区域，无法区分真实预测任务中不同的不确定性来源（离散模式选择、模式内有界漂移、不可约随机噪声），这些来源具有本质不同的性质和处理方式。

Method: 引入HProbZ输出头：将三种不确定性分别表示为zonotope的二元、有界和随机生成器，通过对卷积实现闭式似然；共享有界生成器使各预测步代数耦合；提供解析的每模式风险和多模态分布无关保形集。

Result: 证明了三种生成器可从似然中可辨识（直至排列），且HProbZ密度与任何有限高斯混合在表示上不同。在代表性预测基准上的经验分析显示相对同编码器混合基线设计有效，同时提供混合或凸保形预测器不具备的结构性质。

Conclusion: HProbZ同时提供了多模态概率预测、跨步不确定性耦合和解析保形集，兼具表示丰富性与计算效率，填补了高斯混合与凸保形方法之间的空白。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [98] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型推理中KV缓存的内存瓶颈，提出QEvict三级缓存管理方案，用可恢复的量化驱逐取代二元驱逐策略，在固定内存预算下保留更广泛的历史上下文，并提升长上下文理解、检索和推理任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力分数的token驱逐策略存在一个关键缺陷：驱逐是不可逆的二元决策，一旦token被丢弃就无法恢复。但在实际解码过程中，token和窗口的重要性会随着生成的查询演变而发生动态变化，导致被驱逐的状态可能后来获得大量注意力。作者通过引入Future Missed Mass和Global LIR两个诊断指标来表征这一现象。

Method: 提出QEvict三级KV缓存管理方案：(1) 高置信度窗口保持全精度；(2) 中间窗口存储在量化的可恢复层；(3) 仅删除最低置信度窗口。解码过程中，累积注意力分数持续更新窗口重要性，当量化窗口重新变得重要时，则进行反量化并提升为全精度。

Result: 在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准测试中，QEvict相较代表性的驱逐和量化基线方法持续改进，减少了错过注意力并提升了信息保留能力。

Conclusion: 通过在驱逐策略中引入可恢复性，QEvict在固定内存预算下能够更好地平衡历史上下文保留与关键区域精确度，验证了驱逐决策不应是不可逆的。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache. A dominant line of work reduces this footprint by evicting tokens that appear unimportant under attention-derived scores. However, such policies make an implicit irreversible decision: once a token is evicted, it cannot become useful again. We show that this assumption is brittle during decoding. Token and window importance drift as generated queries evolve, causing standard eviction policies to permanently discard states that later receive substantial attention under the full-cache model. To characterize this behaviour, we introduce Future Missed Mass and Global LIR, two diagnostics that measure future attention assigned to discarded states and the reactivation of historically inactive regions. We propose QEvict, a three-tier KV-cache management scheme that replaces binary retain-or-delete eviction with recoverable eviction. QEvict maintains high-confidence windows in full precision, stores intermediate windows in a quantized recoverable tier, and deletes only the lowest-confidence windows. During decoding, cumulative attention scores update window importance and when a quantized window becomes important again, it is dequantized and promoted to the full-precision. Under a fixed memory budget, this design preserves broader historical context while retaining exact full precision for the most important regions. Across long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks, QEvict consistently improves over representative eviction and quantization baselines, reducing missed attention and improving information retention

</details>


### [99] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并模拟了DG-FedReuse机制，一种联邦学习中的模拟器级机制，允许选中的客户端在随机头梯度差异代理低于轮次相关阈值时，贡献年龄衰减的缓存更新。在90轮预算下，与匹配的Top-K FedAvg相比，DG-FedReuse实现了83.36-85.42%的上行更新数据量节省（相对76.88%），种子对齐的准确率差异在-5.29到-0.14个百分点之间。然而，当考虑对称的密集模型下行链路时，主要节省降至41.68-42.71%，相对Top-K FedAvg的增量收益仅为3.24-4.27个百分点，表明通信结论严重依赖于计算边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习反复经历本地优化和模型更新传输，通信开销成为瓶颈。现有方法缺乏对"复用"（重用缓存更新）与Top-K稀疏化相结合的系统性研究，且通信收益结论常依赖于被忽略的通信记账边界，需要提出一个可在模拟器层面验证的客户端更新复用机制，并诚实评估其效益与局限。

Method: 提出DG-FedReuse机制：(1) 允许选中的客户端在随机头梯度差异代理低于轮次相关阈值时贡献年龄衰减的缓存更新；(2) 设置硬性缓存年龄上限和最少新鲜客户端配额来约束复用；(3) 新鲜更新采用自适应每张量Top-K数值字段表示。实验覆盖6个图像分类数据集、50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异构性（α=0.5）和3个随机种子。

Result: 在90轮预算下，DG-FedReuse实现83.36-85.42%的建模上行更新数据字段节省（对比Top-K FedAvg的76.88%）；种子对齐准确率差异为-5.29至-0.14个百分点。测试控制的检查点下最优测试准确率相对匹配FedAvg为-2.38至+0.45个百分点。当纳入对称密集模型下行链路时，主要节省降至41.68-42.71%，对Top-K FedAvg的增量增益为3.24-4.27个百分点。

Conclusion: DG-FedReuse在模拟器中显示了通信节省的有效性，但收益高度依赖通信记账边界（上行仅算与上下行都算的结果差异巨大）。作者诚实声明：该研究描述的是所实现模拟器中的复用规则，不建立无偏泛化、端到端带宽减少、运行时/能量节省、更快收敛或其优于现有陈旧更新和惰性聚合方法的结论。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [100] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出次梯度驯服非调整Langevin算法（SG-TULA），用于从势能同时非光滑、超线性梯度增长且非凸的目标分布中采样。该方法直接作用于次梯度，无需计算昂贵的平滑过程，并通过驯服技术处理超线性区域，产生稳定的显式方案。作者推导了Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界，所有常数都以维度和逆温度显式追踪，改进了现有基于次梯度的Langevin算法的速率。文章还提供了相关优化问题的超额风险估计，并在GPT-2系列LLM的正则化预训练势能上验证了假设，其提升坐标变体在预训练中与微调后的AdamW和Muon竞争性相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样算法的理论保证通常假设势能光滑或梯度有界增长，而实际应用中（如LLM预训练）的目标分布往往同时具有非光滑性、超线性梯度增长和非凸性。需要一种直接基于次梯度且无需昂贵平滑过程的稳定采样算法，并提供严格的非渐近收敛保证。

Method: 引入SG-TULA算法：基于Langevin扩散的离散化，直接对次梯度进行操作，采用驯服（taming）技术构造稳定的显式数值方案来处理超线性梯度增长区域。在Wasserstein-2距离下建立非渐近收敛界，显式追踪所有常数（维度与逆温度依赖），并给出优化问题的超额风险估计。

Result: 推导了改进的收敛速率（优于现有基于次梯度的Langevin算法），并提供了超额风险估计。在GPT-2系列LLM的正则化预训练势能上验证了假设（给出显式常数），提升坐标变体（boosted coordinate-wise SG-TULA）在预训练任务上表现与微调后的AdamW和Muon相当，后者目前缺乏可比的非渐近保证。

Conclusion: SG-TULA为同时具有非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸特性的采样问题提供了一种理论上可保证且计算上实用的方案，其理论速率优于现有方法，且在LLM预训练等实际场景中展现出竞争力。这为在复杂非光滑非凸环境中运行Langevin类算法提供了新的理论支撑和实用工具。

Abstract: We study the problem of sampling from target distributions whose potentials are simultaneously non-smooth, subject to superlinear gradient growth, and non-convex. We introduce the Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA), a discretisation of the Langevin diffusion that operates directly on subgradients, without relying on computationally demanding smoothing procedures. To handle the superlinear regime, taming techniques are employed to produce a stable, explicit scheme. We derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds in Wasserstein-2 distance, with all constants tracked explicitly in terms of dimension and inverse temperature, improving upon the currently known rates for subgradient-based Langevin algorithms. We further provide excess risk estimates for the associated optimisation problem. We verify the assumptions, with explicit constants, for the regularized pretraining potential of a LLM in the GPT-2 lineage and the boosted coordinate-wise variant of SG-TULA pretrains the former competitively against finetuned AdamW and Muon, for which no comparable non-asymptotic guarantees are presently available.

</details>


### [101] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量子结构化世界模型（QSWMs），一种受量子理论启发的预测性世界建模框架，使用结构化潜状态、潜转移算子和测量启发的解码映射。研究量子启发的数学结构（如复值表示和类密度矩阵潜变量）是否能提供有用的归纳偏置，并在初等元胞自动机上评估其表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型大多使用经典向量、概率分布、循环隐藏状态或Transformer激活来表示状态，本文探索量子理论启发的数学结构是否能为世界建模提供更有用的归纳偏置。

Method: 提出QSWM框架，包含三个基础性质（经典包含性、预测充分性、结构紧凑性），并实例化两种变体：复值QSWM和类密度矩阵QSWM，在初等元胞自动机上进行评估。

Result: 复值QSWM展现出有前景的局部预测潜力，但长时程展开存在局限；密度矩阵变体表现不如预期。

Conclusion: 量子启发的结构（特别是复值表示）在局部预测上有一定优势，但长时程预测和密度矩阵类变体仍需改进，量子归纳偏置的价值有待进一步验证。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [102] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Surv-IPTB，一种基于注意力机制的框架，用于在生存分析中估计个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）。该方法将IPTB估计重构为二元分类问题，利用治疗组与对照组间的成对患者比较，并通过不精确概率表示（区间值概率）来处理右删失数据。注意力机制实现灵活的数据驱动聚合，实验表明在复杂非线性场景中优于T-learner和S-learner等元学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生存分析中，个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）的准确估计对个性化医疗决策至关重要，但现有方法在处理右删失数据和非线性复杂特征空间时表现不佳，需要一种更稳健且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 将IPTB估计重构为二元分类问题，通过跨治疗组与对照组的成对患者比较来实现；利用不精确概率表示（区间值概率）原则性处理右删失观测；采用注意力机制搭配可学习的查询-键变换，实现灵活的成对比较聚合，同时学习删失案例的软类概率。

Result: 在包含螺旋形、钟形和圆形特征空间的合成非线性数据集上，该方法在不同删失率和治疗效应强度下均保持稳健性能，一致优于配备随机生存森林、Cox比例风险和Beran估计器的T-learner和S-learner基线，尤其在常规方法严重退化的非线性场景中优势显著。

Conclusion: 该注意力框架被验证为具有可扩展性和统计原理性的个性化治疗获益评估方案，代码已公开。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [103] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过凸学习方法从非线性动力学系统观测中提取紧凑的线性状态空间表示（LDS）的可证明管线。该管线分为两步：首先使用Observation Spectral Filtering（OSF）学习一个隐式的谱预测器，然后通过谱到LDS的蒸馏将其转换为显式的循环线性动力系统。理论保证表明平均预测误差可分解为指数小的蒸馏项和由最佳观测器的Luenberger复杂度控制的OSF学习项，且与潜在维度无关。实验在LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接求解非线性系统辨识问题通常是非凸的，难以保证收敛到全局最优。作者希望绕过这一困难，探索是否可以通过凸学习管线来学习非线性动力学系统的紧凑线性状态空间表示，从而获得可证明的保证。

Method: 两阶段管线：（1）OSF（Observation Spectral Filtering）：一种凸方法，从观测中学习隐式谱预测器，与系统的最优线性观测器竞争；（2）谱到LDS蒸馏：将隐式预测器转换为显式的循环线性动力系统。理论分析将蒸馏LDS的平均预测误差分解为蒸馏项（指数小）和OSF学习项（受Luenberger复杂度控制）。

Result: 理论结果是无维度依赖的：只取决于观测器复杂度而非表示非线性系统所需的潜在维度。实验表明，在LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆任务上，该train-then-distill管线生成的紧凑LDS预测器匹配或优于直接训练的基线。

Conclusion: 该工作首次提供了端到端可证明的方法，通过凸学习加可证明蒸馏，从非线性动力学中提取最佳事后（best-in-hindsight）的LDS表示，为非线性系统辨识问题提供了一条新的理论可保证的路径。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [104] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需群体标注即可识别受虚假相关性影响样本的方法。核心发现在于：模型收敛后，与虚假相关性一致的样本被共享规则分类，而其余样本则通过输入特定的脆弱配置拟合。对收敛模型的输入施加固定扰动，后者预测翻转的频率远高于前者。该方法仅需每个训练样本两次前向传播，无需群体标注，也无需选择早停轮次。在Waterbirds上，用检测到的样本重新平衡训练将最差群体准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，接近使用真实群体标签的85.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验风险最小化训练的模型在含有虚假相关性的数据上表现高平均准确率，但在相关性不成立的子群体上失败。现有识别受影响样本的方法依赖早期训练信号，需要选择干预轮次这一超参数，且通常需要用带群体标注的验证集来调参，限制了实际应用。

Method: 利用模型收敛后的脆弱性差异信号：对收敛模型的输入施加固定扰动，观察预测翻转频率。与虚假相关性一致的样本因被共享规则分类而更鲁棒，其余样本因依赖输入特异性的脆弱配置而更易被扰动翻转。基于此信号识别受影响样本，并用其重新平衡训练数据。

Result: 在Waterbirds数据集上，该方法将最差群体准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，而使用真实群体标签的上限为85.8%。方法不需要群体标注、不需要早停轮次选择，仅需每个样本两次前向传播。

Conclusion: 模型收敛后的预测脆弱性可以作为一种有效且实用的信号来识别受虚假相关性影响的样本，避免了现有方法对早期训练信号和群体标注验证数据的依赖，为提升最差群体鲁棒性提供了更简单可行的方案。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [105] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了信息流网络（IFNs），将生成流网络框架扩展到不完全信息博弈中，证明原先生成流网络在不完全信息博弈中的约束不成立，并提出严格泛化AFlowNets的IFlowNets方法，在三个标准博弈环境中表现与OSMCCFR和RL方法相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有许多算法将强化学习（RL）与反事实遗憾（CFR）方法结合以利用计算速度和性能上的权衡，但对不完全信息博弈中生成式采样框架的探索较少。作者希望填补这一研究空白。

Method: 首先将对抗流网络（AFlowNets）这一生成流网络框架扩展到不完全信息博弈，提出信息流网络（IFNs）。作者证明了原先在完全信息博弈中建立的生成流网络约束在不完全信息博弈中无法获得有效的密度（对应玩家策略）和有效的训练目标。随后提出IFlowNets作为泛化推广，解决了该问题并严格泛化了AFlowNets。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境的初步实验中，IFlowNets在性能和速度上与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret（OSMCCFR）以及标准RL方法相比表现相当或更优。

Conclusion: IFlowNets成功将生成流网络应用于不完全信息博弈，并通过理论证明和实验验证说明其有效性、严格泛化了AFlowNets，在不完全信息博弈的生成式采样领域迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [106] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"自由"（freedom）才是生成式训练中继参数、架构、MDL、数据之后的"第三预训练轴"。探索式建模（XM）通过每次产生K个输出并仅在最近者上更新，实际上是在优化自由而非简单的生成表达力。作者证明XM损失与候选错过可接受区域的概率有关，并以实验表明更大K提升自由、自由选择器在29/30案例中优于验证集选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成式训练存在局限：每次比较仅产生一个输出，导致模型为追求常见答案而牺牲表达范围的广度。作者指出已有的"探索作为第三预训练轴"的观点不够准确，真正的第三轴是"自由"——即模型行为所隐含约束的强弱。自由的本质是函数属性而非形式属性，且已有形式化证明最弱（约束最少）的模型最可能泛化。

Method: 作者从理论上证明平均XM损失依赖于候选错过可接受区域的概率，探索使漏检概率降至K次幂。随后通过"前向XM"实验测量自由随K的变化，并在跨上下文目标下验证自由提升。最后训练XM候选池，用读无标注父上下文的自由选择器与验证集选择对比，在30个案例中测试29次。

Result: 理论上，K>1时匹配概率随自由上升；实验上，较大K增加或饱和了测量到的自由，在每种测试值下都提升了自由。自由选择器在29/30个案例中胜过验证集选择，表明选择自由能在分布偏移下改进XM性能。

Conclusion: 生成表达力只是自由的一个模式计数代理，它舍弃了赋予自由泛化意义的扩展结构。XM是实现手段，自由才是最终目标。在分布偏移条件下，以自由为目标进行选择能显著改进XM。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance. XM is a means, freedom an end, and selecting for freedom improved XM under distribution shift.

</details>


### [107] [EvoHarness-RL: Learning Self-Evolving Runtime Harness for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05446)
*Xuying Ning,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Hanqing Zeng,Yuanchen Bei,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Li,Qifan Wang,Xiang Shen,Yifan Wu,Jiayi Liu,Hong Li,Yinglong Xia,Xiangjun Fan,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoHarness-RL框架，让长时程LLM智能体离线学习"栈策略"（harness policy），在运行时构造和更新外部状态（Belief、Progress、Experience），通过监督微调与成本感知GRPO训练，在ALFWorld上达到96.9%成功率，并揭示了"栈退火"与"栈演化"两种关键动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体依赖外部执行支持（状态维护、进度追踪、工具调用、结果验证、经验复用），但现有的栈（harness）使用通常依赖提示、启发式或领域特定约定，导致外部工作空间与其使用策略需要人工工程化，缺乏可学习、可自动化的方法。

Method: 提出EvoHarness-RL：（1）将Belief、Progress、Experience（BPE）作为面向策略的栈状态；（2）通过监督栈微调教会基础智能体栈的动作空间及如何构造有用的外部状态；（3）采用成本感知的GRPO探索协调策略，在长时程交互中选择性读取、更新和整合状态。实验在ALFWorld上用Qwen3-8B实现。

Result: 在ALFWorld上达到96.9%成功率，并揭示两个关键动态：栈退火（训练将重复的栈使用模式内化为模型策略，使智能体从频繁调用转向选择性外部状态访问）和栈演化（进度更新与经验整合将栈精炼为紧凑的任务自适应状态基底）。

Conclusion: 长时程智能体受益于可训练的外部栈工作空间构造与协调策略，而非仅仅依赖更强的工具或更大的记忆容量。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents increasingly rely on external execution support to maintain state, track progress, invoke tools, verify outcomes, and reuse experience across interactions. However, effective harness use raises two coupled challenges: state formation from noisy interaction traces and runtime control over external-state access. Existing agents usually handle both through prompts, heuristics, or domain-specific conventions, leaving the external workspace and its usage policy manually engineered. To address this, we study the problem of harness policy learning, where agents learn harness policies offline and deploy them to construct and update external harness state online during runtime task execution. We introduce EvoHarness-RL, which exposes Belief, Progress, and Experience (BPE) as policy-facing harness state. Supervised harness fine-tuning teaches the base agent the harness action space and how to construct useful external state, while cost-aware GRPO explores coordination policies to selectively read, update, and consolidate that state during long-horizon interaction. Instantiated on ALFWorld with a Qwen3-8B LLM, EvoHarness-RL reaches 96.9% success and reveals two key dynamics: harness annealing, where training internalizes recurring harness-use patterns into the model policy and shifts the agent from frequent harness calls toward selective external-state access, and harness evolution, where progress updates and experience consolidation refine the harness into a compact, task-adaptive state substrate. These results suggest that long-horizon agents benefit from trainable policies for constructing and coordinating with external harness workspaces, beyond simply adding stronger tools or larger memories.

</details>


### [108] [Matrix Zonotopic Attention: A Context-Adaptive Value Projection for Set Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05472)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多头注意力中softmax路由（输入相关）与线性值投影（输入无关）的不对称性对置换不变集合目标的影响。作者提出TDOF复杂度度量，证明上下文刚性注意力需要与TDOF成比例的深度，而单层上下文自适应值族即可表示同样的目标。基于此提出Matrix Zonotopic Attention (MZAttn)，用上下文自适应的矩阵-zonotope族替代固定值投影，实验验证了其优势具有选择性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头注意力机制存在内在不对称性：路由是输入相关的（softmax），但值投影是输入无关的（线性矩阵）。这种"上下文刚性"可能限制了注意力对置换不变集合目标的表达能力。作者希望从理论上刻画这种限制的代价，并设计更灵活的结构来克服它。

Method: 1) 提出Transformation Degrees of Freedom (TDOF)复杂度度量，量化目标算子的输入相关方向数；2) 进行深度分离分析，证明上下文刚性注意力需深度与TDOF成正比，而单层上下文自适应值族即可表示；3) 提出Matrix Zonotopic Attention (MZAttn)，用中心矩阵加输入相关门控的生成矩阵和（矩阵-zonotope族）替代固定值投影，初始化时退化为标准注意力，保持置换等变性。

Result: 在多种集合预测任务上，MZAttn的表现与TDOF预测一致：优势具有选择性——对依赖于输入集合高秩、稀疏组合方式的目标优势明显，而对聚合统计类目标（参数匹配的标准注意力已具竞争力）优势很小。

Conclusion: 注意力机制中值投影的上下文刚性是表达能力的重要瓶颈，通过使值族输入自适应可以克服深度需求；但这种架构增益并非普适的，而是高度依赖于目标算子的TDOF特性。

Abstract: Multi-head attention combines an input-dependent softmax routing with an input-independent linear value projection, so the per-sample operator mapping aggregated values to outputs is the same for every input set. We study the consequences of this asymmetry for permutation-invariant set targets. We introduce the Transformation Degrees of Freedom (TDOF) of a target operator, a complexity measure counting the input-dependent directions an exact representation requires, and present a depth-separation analysis showing that context-rigid attention needs depth proportional to the target's TDOF, whereas a single layer with a context-adaptive value family can represent the same target. Building on this analysis, we propose Matrix Zonotopic Attention (MZAttn), which replaces the fixed value projection with a context-adaptive matrix-zonotope family: a centre matrix plus a sum of generator matrices weighted by input-dependent gates. The construction reduces to standard multi-head attention at initialisation, preserves permutation equivariance, and admits a data-driven reachability interpretation. Experiments on a range of set-prediction tasks are consistent with the TDOF prediction that the architectural advantage is selective: it appears on targets that depend on the input set in a high-rank, sparsely combinatorial way, and is small on aggregate-statistic targets where parameter-matched standard attention is already competitive.

</details>


### [109] [KV-Skill: Forging Expertise in the Model's Native Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05475)
*Zhaowei Han,Xiang Zhang,Bing Han,Kai Liu,Danqi Hu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KV-Skill，一种外部因子化算子设计空间，让冻结的语言模型通过轻量接口读取任务知识。它支持两条路径：注册（将文本技能转化为文本派生算子）和奖励学习（直接从任务结果学习紧凑潜在算子）。在十个基准和四个骨干模型上，KV-Skill一致优于文本提示、SoftSkill等方法，且支持独立加载多个技能而不遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务知识通常以提示文本形式存储（模块化但每次使用需重新解释）或以权重更新形式存储（能力难以独立加载、移除或共享）。现有方法难以兼顾模块性和独立可部署性，需要一个既高效又模块化的知识存储方案。

Method: 提出KV-Skill设计空间：1) 注册路径——将作者编写的文本技能转换为文本派生算子，并训练共享的逐骨干接口；2) 奖励学习路径——直接从任务结果学习紧凑的潜在算子，可带或不带作者技能。两条路径都不向提示中增加位置。算子是外部的因子化算子，冻结模型通过轻量接口读取。
