Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CIPO（Contextual Information Policy Optimization），一种面向证据的强化学习框架，通过密级联级信用分配来对齐策略优化与外部证据使用，减少搜索智能体的先验驱动推理和确认偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体方法主要奖励最终答案正确性或中间进度，未直接评估检索后动作是否基于检索证据，导致智能体倾向先验驱动推理——基于内部知识下结论、仅用检索来确认，产生确认偏差和低效证据使用。

Method: CIPO框架：(1) 对受检索信息影响的推理动作分配密级、回合级信用；(2) 将证据使用信号与全局结果奖励结合以保留答案正确性；(3) 无需人工过程标注或额外奖励模型。

Result: 在7个领域内和领域外基准上进行广泛实验，CIPO降低了先验驱动推理的发生率，并在大多数任务上取得优异性能。

Conclusion: CIPO通过显式对齐策略优化与外部证据使用，有效抑制先验驱动推理，提升搜索智能体在知识密集型任务上的可靠性和性能，且无需额外人工标注或奖励模型。

Abstract: Search agents extend large language models beyond static parametric memory by enabling them to acquire and use ex ternal evidence during multi-step reasoning. For knowledge intensive tasks involving complex or evolving information, their reliability depends not only on retrieving relevant ev idence but also on using it to guide subsequent reasoning. However, existing methods primarily reward final-answer cor rectness or intermediate progress, without directly assessing whether post-retrieval actions are grounded in the retrieved evidence. This misalignment encourages prior-driven reason ing: agents form conclusions based on internal knowledge and use retrieval mainly to confirm them, resulting in confirma tion bias and inefficient evidenceuse.Toaddressthisissue, we propose Contextual Information Policy Optimization (CIPO), an evidence-oriented reinforcement learning framework that explicitly aligns policy optimization with external evidence use. CIPO assigns dense, turn-level credit to reasoning ac tions influenced by retrieved information, while combining this evidence-use signal with a global outcome reward to pre serveanswercorrectness.Withthismanner,CIPOdiscourages evidence-detached guesses and promotes reasoning trajecto ries in which retrieved facts can guide or revise subsequent reasoning. Importantly, CIPO requires neither human process annotations nor an additional reward model. Extensive exper iments on seven in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks show that CIPO reduces the prevalence of prior-driven rea soning and achieves excellent performance on most tasks.

</details>


### [245] [Beyond Top-K: Replacing Black-Box Retrieval with Interpretable Agentic Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06305)
*Sagar Tamang,Ayush Vyas,Tabarakul Hazarika*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出对财务报表、审计报告等长文档而言，传统"分块+嵌入+top-k检索"的RAG设计在结构上有缺陷（如表格行占86.8%、单位依赖13行外的表头导致嵌入空间混乱）。作者提出READ系统——通过规范化词法搜索、结构导航和有界跨度读取三种确定性操作，让代理通过MCP协议读取原始文档，生成可回放的审计轨迹而非不透明相似度分数。在51个验证问题上，READ准确率58.8%，远超稠密检索的15.7%；即使调优后仍领先23.5个百分点。但BM25与READ无显著差异，说明收益来自"无嵌入检索"而非"代理式迭代"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG将文档分块嵌入后做top-k最近邻检索，但对结构化表格密集的财务/审计/监管类长文档（780页报告中86.8%是表格行，数值单位常由13行之外的表头决定）存在结构性缺陷：分块边界常把数字与其单位（如lakh/crore）分开，导致数量级错误；海量近似数字在同嵌入空间中竞争。作者要将这一缺陷可量化、可验证地呈现出来。

Method: 提出READ（Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search）：代理通过三种确定性操作——规范化词法搜索（normalized lexical search）、结构导航（structural navigation）、有界跨度读取（bounded span reads）——通过Model Context Protocol（MCP）访问原始文档，产生可回放的审计轨迹，替代不透明的相似度分数。用可感知表格的chunker（table-aware chunker）作为"钢铁侠式"强基线修复单位问题，并对比稠密检索、代理+top-k工具、BM25等多种基线。

Result: 在51个已验证问题上，READ准确率58.8%，稠密检索仅15.7%（p_Holm=2×10⁻⁵）；调优后READ 35.3%，仍领先23.5个百分点（p_Holm=0.017）。给代理相同循环但提供top-k工具只能达到27.5%，说明增益来自接口设计而非迭代次数。但BM25与READ在统计上无显著差异，因此结果区分的是"基于嵌入vs无嵌入检索"，而非"代理式vs词法搜索"。表格感知chunker能修复单位问题，但所有chunk大小下仍有27-30%数值块缺少财政年度表头。

Conclusion: 对表格密集的财务长文档，去嵌入化的确定性检索（READ）显著优于稠密嵌入检索，且收益源自"无嵌入"设计本身而非代理迭代。但词法检索（BM25）与READ无统计差异，表明核心价值在于避免嵌入空间中的结构失真。作者也诚实地报告了证据不支持的部分，为后续研究划清边界。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over long documents is dominated by one design: chunk the text, embed the chunks, and surface the top-k nearest neighbours of the query. We argue that for an important class of documents -- financial statements, audit reports, regulatory returns -- this design is structurally unsound, and we make the argument measurable. On a 780-page government financial report, 86.8% of content lines are table rows, thousands of near-identical figures compete in one embedding space, and a figure inherits its unit from a header a median of 13 lines above it -- so a chunk boundary routinely separates a number from whether it is in lakh or crore, an error of two orders of magnitude. A table-aware chunker built as a steelman fixes the unit problem but leaves 27-30% of numeric chunks with no fiscal-year header at every chunk size we tried. We propose READ (Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search), in which an agent reads the raw document through three deterministic operations -- normalized lexical search, structural navigation, and bounded span reads -- exposed over the Model Context Protocol, so a trajectory is a replayable audit trail, not an opaque similarity score. On 51 verified questions READ answers 58.8% against dense retrieval's 15.7% (p_Holm = 2 x 10^-5) -- or 35.3% tuned, which READ still leads by 23.5 points (p_Holm = 0.017). An agent given the same loop but a top-k tool reaches only 27.5%, locating the gain in the interface rather than in iteration. We also report what the evidence does not support: BM25 is statistically indistinguishable from READ, so our result separates embedding-based from embedding-free retrieval, not agentic from lexical search.

</details>


### [246] [FinEvo-Bench: A Longitudinal Benchmark for Self-Evolving Agents in Professional Financial Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06144)
*Bo Deng,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Chongyang Tao,Xuanren Chen,Chenggang Xie,Renzhao Liang,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinEvo-Bench是一个纵向基准测试，包含120个基于真实案例的任务，覆盖6个金融领域的20个业务场景。它通过共享专业流程和人工审核评分标准，评估智能体如何将先前经验转化为后续改进。实验表明，进化条件可将分数提升9.33-19.37分，并将合规问题减少0.12-0.44个/任务，其中Codex获得最大的自进化增益(+19.37)，Letta达到最高的进化分数(91.65)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试大多是独立评估任务，无法衡量经验积累对后续任务表现的帮助，而现有的自进化基准测试也没有同时覆盖专业工作流程、开放式交付物和多维度评估。

Method: 构建包含20个业务场景、120个真实案例任务的FinEvo-Bench，每个场景有6个共享专业流程但实质不同的案例，配有基于任务质量和金融合规性的人工审核评分标准。使用Qwen3.7-Max作为主干，比较四种自进化智能体框架，采用三组独立洗牌的全场景交错任务流，配对非进化对照组估算自进化增益，并用Claude Opus 4.6支持的Claude Code评分智能体评估所有输出。

Result: Letta达到最高进化分数(91.65)和最少合规问题(0.09/任务)；Codex获得最大自进化增益(+19.37)。进化条件使分数提升9.33-19.37分，合规问题减少0.12-0.44个/任务。场景内排名4-6的配对分数增益比排名1-3高出6.10-8.70分。在Claude Code中，仅技能进化优于仅记忆进化和记忆-技能联合进化。所有框架中，评分标准反馈优于参考答案反馈。

Conclusion: FinEvo-Bench能够同时衡量专业表现和自进化能力，即智能体如何有效地将先前经验转化为后续改进，为金融领域的智能体自进化研究提供了全面的评估框架。

Abstract: Most agent benchmarks evaluate tasks independently and cannot measure whether experience from one task helps with later tasks. Existing self-evolution benchmarks do not jointly cover professional workflows, open-ended deliverables, and multi-aspect evaluation. We introduce FinEvo-Bench, a longitudinal benchmark with 120 real-case-grounded tasks, 20 business scenes across six financial domains. Institution-provided professional procedures define the required operations and constraints. Eligible institution-provided and publicly documented cases supply the task facts. Each scene contains six related but substantively distinct cases that share a professional procedure and a manually reviewed rubric for task quality and financial compliance. We compare four self-evolving agent scaffolds using the same Qwen3.7-Max backbone and three independently shuffled, globally interleaved task streams. Paired non-evolving controls estimate each scaffold's self-evolution gain from retained experience, while an independent Claude Code scoring agent backed by Claude Opus 4.6 evaluates all outputs. Letta achieves the highest evolved score (91.65) and fewest compliance issues (0.09 per task); Codex achieves the largest self-evolution gain (+19.37). Across scaffolds, the evolving condition raises scores by 9.33-19.37 points and reduces compliance issues by 0.12-0.44 per task. Paired score gains at within-scene ranks 4-6 exceed those at ranks 1-3 by 6.10-8.70 points. In Claude Code, skill-only evolution produces higher task quality and fewer compliance issues than memory-only and combined memory-skill evolution. Across all four scaffolds, rubric feedback also yields higher scores and fewer compliance issues than reference-answer feedback. FinEvo-Bench measures both professional performance and self-evolution ability: how effectively an agent turns prior experience into later improvement.

</details>


### [247] [PaDoc: Layout-Grounded Parallel Decoding for Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06146)
*Hao Yu,Jiabo Zhan,Kang Liu,Linnan Zhao,Dongxu Yue,Rui Chen,Jinglin Wang,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PaDoc 是一种基于布局的端到端文档解析器，它将预测的布局视为共享页面表示上的分支结构，通过前缀条件分解使布局流和区域内容分支并发推进，将解码深度缩短至最长布局-内容路径，在 OmniDocBench Full 上取得领先性能，同时显著提升吞吐量和降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端文档解析器将所有页面布局和区域内容序列化为单一自回归序列，导致解码路径随总内容增长，且阻碍区域级并行；而裁剪式两阶段解析器虽然支持区域并行，但需要重复的视觉预填充且页面上下文碎片化。因此需要一种兼顾全页上下文和并行解码能力的方案。

Method: PaDoc 提出布局接地解析框架，将预测布局视为共享页面表示的树状分支结构。基于区域充分性假设，推导出前缀条件因子分解，使布局流和各区域内容分支并发推进。实现上，在单个 MLLM 中通过 packed variable-length ancestor attention 在标准 next-token 训练下保持可见性，并利用 masked parallel decoding 创建分支，由 vLLM 后端作为并发请求处理，复用缓存中的共享前缀。

Result: 在 OmniDocBench Full 上，PaDoc 取得 Overall layout F1 91.1，端到端解析器中 Overall 得分 94.24（顶级），最佳 Text Edit 0.038 和 Formula CDM 95.59。在 384 页子集与单张 A800 GPU 上，五个并发级别下均是最快端到端解析器，有效页面吞吐量提升 67.4%-118%，P95 延迟降低 39.2%-54.9%（相对同骨干 Sequential SFT 基线）。

Conclusion: PaDoc 通过将布局作为分支结构与共享页面表示结合，在保留全页上下文的同时去除解码依赖，成功实现了端到端文档解析中并行性与上下文完整性兼得的目标，并在精度和效率上均取得显著优势。

Abstract: End-to-end document parsers provide a unified interface, but serialize page layouts and regional contents into one autoregressive sequence. This formulation forces independent regions onto a decoding path whose length grows with the total content, whereas crop-based two-stage parsers expose region-level parallelism at the cost of repeated visual prefills and fragmented page context. To retain full-page context while removing dependencies, we propose PaDoc, a layout-grounded parser that treats the predicted layout as a branching structure over a shared page representation. Under a region-sufficiency assumption, we derive a prefix-conditioned factorization in which the layout stream and regional content branches advance concurrently, reducing the decoding depth to the longest layout-content path. We realize this factorization within a single MLLM: packed variable-length ancestor attention preserves the visibility under standard next-token training, while masked parallel decoding creates branches that the evaluated vLLM backend serves as concurrent requests with cache-resident shared-prefix reuse. On OmniDocBench Full, PaDoc attains an Overall layout F1 of 91.1 and, among end-to-end parsers, a top-tier Overall score of 94.24 together with the best Text Edit (0.038) and Formula CDM (95.59). On a 384-page subset and one A800 GPU, it is the fastest end-to-end parser at five concurrency levels, improving valid-page throughput by 67.4-118% and reducing P95 latency by 39.2-54.9% relative to a same-backbone Sequential SFT baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Longin-Yu/Padoc

</details>


### [248] [CogVis: Must Open-Vocabulary Change Detection Perceive the Scene Anew for Every Query?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06150)
*Zijie Wang,Chen Zhong,Wei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CogVis是一种认知记忆引导的开放词汇变化检测（OVCD）框架，将OVCD重构为感知-记忆-验证范式。通过场景变化感知器提取可复用的类别无关变化先验，语义记忆校准器动态估计决策阈值，自适应区域过滤器基于语义、时间和结构可靠性过滤候选，在七个基准上达到SOTA性能，并提升28.50%的推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OVCD方法将时间感知、语义判别和区域验证纠缠在一起，导致结果不稳定且计算冗余。人类视觉变化感知的认知机制启发作者将该过程解耦。

Method: 1) 场景变化感知器（SCP）：从冻结的双时态特征中提取可复用的类别无关变化先验，解耦时间证据与语义类别决策；2) 语义记忆校准器（SMC）：动态估计图像-查询特定的决策阈值，补偿类别相关的分数偏移；3) 自适应区域过滤器（ARF）：利用学习的语义、时间和结构可靠性过滤连通候选区域。

Result: 在涵盖语义变化检测、二值变化定位和建筑物损伤评估的七个基准数据集上均达到SOTA性能，且通过共享场景级变化感知避免了跨查询重复计算，推理吞吐量提升28.50%。

Conclusion: CogVis通过认知启发的感知-记忆-验证范式有效解耦了OVCD中的时间感知与语义判别，在提升性能的同时显著提高了推理效率，验证了人类认知模型在计算视觉任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Earth-surface monitoring requires change detection models capable of recognizing arbitrary semantic categories. Open-Vocabulary Change Detection (OVCD) addresses this need. However, existing methods often entangle temporal perception, semantic discrimination, and region verification, causing unstable results and redundant computation. Inspired by human visual change perception, we propose CogVis, a cognitive memory-guided framework that reformulates OVCD as a perception-memory-verification paradigm. CogVis first employs a Scene Change Perceptron (SCP) to extract a reusable, category-agnostic change prior from frozen bi-temporal features, thereby decoupling temporal evidence from semantic category decisions. A Semantic Memory Calibrator (SMC) then compensates for category-dependent score shifts by dynamically estimating an image-query-specific decision threshold. Finally, an Adaptive Region Filter (ARF) filters connected candidates using learned semantic, temporal, and structural reliability. Experiments on seven benchmarks spanning semantic change detection, binary change localization, and building-damage assessment show that CogVis achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated datasets. By sharing scene-level change perception, CogVis further avoids repeating category-agnostic temporal perception across queries and improves inference throughput by 28.50%.

</details>


### [249] [iARCS: Iterative Agentic RL for Controllable 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06161)
*Saugat Adhikari,Ashok Prasad Neupane,Pramish Paudel,Ajad Chhatkuli,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: iARCS是一个迭代式智能体强化学习框架，用于将预训练的场景生成器适配到自然语言任务需求上。它采用两阶段策略：通用奖励预训练提升物理合理性和布局质量，然后通过LLM生成的奖励程序进行任务特定微调，并在训练反馈中迭代改进。实验表明该方法在可步行性、可达性等约束保真度上优于基线，同时保持场景多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成3D场景生成器主要优化感知真实感，但常常无法可靠满足任务关键的功能约束（如可达性、可穿越性和空间规则合规性），导致合成数据在下游训练（如计算机视觉和具身AI）中的实用性受限。

Method: iARCS框架采用两阶段策略：(1) 通用奖励预训练，改善物理合理性和布局质量；(2) 任务特定微调，利用LLM生成的奖励程序，并通过训练反馈迭代精炼这些奖励程序。

Result: 实验表明iARCS在可步行性、可达性和净空等任务的约束保真度上表现更好，实现了有效的任务特定约束优化，并保持了有竞争力的场景多样性。此外，iARCS生成的数据还能改进基础生成器，证明了其作为实用合成数据生成工具的价值。

Conclusion: iARCS不仅是一种可控的场景编辑方法，更是一种实用的合成数据生成工具，能够根据自然语言任务需求生成满足功能约束的场景数据，并反过来提升基础生成器的性能。

Abstract: Synthetic 3D scene generation is increasingly used as a data source for computer vision and embodied AI, but existing generators often optimize perceptual realism without reliably satisfying task-critical functional constraints. This mismatch limits the usefulness of synthetic data for downstream training, where accessibility, traversability, and spatial rule compliance are often essential. We present iARCS, an iterative agentic reinforcement learning framework that adapts a pretrained scene generator to natural-language task requirements. iARCS uses a two-stage strategy: universal-reward pretraining to improve physical plausibility and layout quality, followed by task-specific fine-tuning with LLM-generated reward programs that are iteratively refined from training feedback. Experiments show improved constraint fidelity on walkability, reachability, and clearance-focused tasks, effective task-specific constraint optimization, and competitive scene diversity. We further show that data generated by iARCS improves a base generator, supporting its value as a practical synthetic data generation tool rather than only a controllable scene editing method.

</details>


### [250] [MicroEvo: Knowledge-Guided LLM Sampling for Efficient Microarchitecture Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06183)
*Jia Xiong,Runkai Li,Chenxu Niu,Guangyuan Gao,Changwen Xing,Yifan Zhang,Xinlai Wan,Jieran Cui,Chen Bai,Yusheng Hua,Ying Wang,Ming Ling,Xi Wang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MicroEvo是一个知识引导的微架构设计空间探索框架，将LLM与蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)结合，用于多目标微架构优化。它通过LLM驱动的进化算子、Pareto感知树策略、主动知识积累机制和状态感知指令，相比NSGA-II在Pareto前沿质量上提升36.2%，搜索效率提升10.6倍，并展现出对工业级复杂核心的良好可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微架构设计空间探索面临搜索空间庞大和PPA评估成本高昂的挑战，有限的仿真预算限制了设计决策。现有方法进行盲目搜索，未考虑微架构依赖关系，也无法从迭代搜索中有效学习，导致评估浪费和Pareto收敛性能弱。

Method: MicroEvo提出了一个知识引导框架，包含四个核心组件：1) LLM驱动的进化操作符用于生成高质量候选设计；2) Pareto感知的树策略，平衡Pareto贡献与多样性；3) 主动知识积累机制，提取并重用优化洞察；4) 状态感知指令，在线自适应调整搜索行为。

Result: 实验结果表明，MicroEvo在Pareto前沿质量上相比NSGA-II提升最高达36.2%，搜索效率提高10.6倍，并成功扩展到工业级规模的复杂核心设计。

Conclusion: 将LLM与MCTS结合并引入知识引导机制，能够显著提升微架构设计空间探索的效率和质量，为大规模微架构优化提供了可行的新范式。

Abstract: Microarchitecture design space exploration suffers from expansive search spaces and expensive PPA evaluation, leaving only a small simulation budget for design decision-making. Existing methods perform blind search without considering microarchitectural dependencies and fail to learn from the iterative search effectively, leading to wasted evaluations and weak Pareto convergence. In this paper, we propose MicroEvo, a knowledge-guided framework that couples off-the-shelf LLMs with Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) for multi-objective microarchitecture optimization. MicroEvo combines LLM-driven evolutionary operators, a Pareto-aware tree policy that balances Pareto contribution and diversity, an active knowledge accumulation mechanism that extracts and reuses optimization insights, and state-aware directives that adapt the search behavior online. Experiments show that MicroEvo improves Pareto-front quality by up to 36.2% over NSGA-II and achieves 10.6x higher search efficiency, and also demonstrates strong scalability to a complex industrial-scale core. The code repository is available at: https://github.com/GEAR-SEU/MicroEvo-ICCAD-26.

</details>


### [251] [Comparative Approaches to Agent Retrieval over Large Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06196)
*Indivara Kolluru,Nathan Sportsman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型技能库中技能检索的两类系统：混合排序器（结合词法和稠密嵌入检索）与类型化知识图谱，在690个技能语料库和117个真实查询上对比。混合排序器在top5内达73.5%±8.0的准确率，而知识图谱表现显著更差（-11.2分，p=0.0007）。作者发现图谱的边主要来自排序器已搜索过的嵌入邻域（98.6%重叠），因此图谱只能丰富关系语义，无法扩展检索范围。此外，作者查询会高估hit@5达44个百分点，掩盖了真实性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型技能库驱动的智能体面临技能加载成本和排序问题，需要研究如何在上下文中高效选择和排序技能，同时探究在强排序器基础上增加结构化信息（如知识图谱）是否能提升检索效果。

Method: 在690个技能语料库上对比两类系统：1）混合排序器，结合词法检索和稠密嵌入检索进行稀疏按需加载；2）类型化知识图谱，编码工作流关系（前置条件、数据流、排序）。在117个真实非回显查询上评估，并验证了LLM生成的图谱边层与免费嵌入邻域的效果差异。

Result: 混合排序器在top5内达到73.5%±8.0的准确率，约1/4查询未命中。知识图谱在匹配token预算下显著更差（-11.2分，p=0.0007），其LLM生成的边层无额外增益，73%的排序器失败查询在图谱中不可达。由于98.6%的图谱边来自排序器已覆盖的嵌入邻域，图谱受限于预过滤拓扑边界。

Conclusion: 额外添加的结构化信息（知识图谱）不能超越强排序器的检索范围，只能丰富关系的语义表达。作者查询会高估性能达44个百分点，掩盖真实结果。研究提供了对为何结构不提升检索的机制性解释，并指出了何时添加结构依赖到检索中是最优的。

Abstract: Agents backed by large skill libraries must decide which skills to load and in what order. Loading the entire library into context is expensive and provides no structure for autonomous sequencing. We study two systems for this problem over a corpus of 690 skills: a hybrid ranker combining lexical and dense-embedding retrieval for sparse, on-demand loading, and a typed knowledge graph encoding workflow relations such as prerequisites, data flow, and ordering. On a set of 117 realistic, non-echoing queries, the hybrid ranker retrieves the correct skill within the top five in 73.5% +/- 8.0 of cases, leaving roughly a quarter of queries unserved. When used as the design intended (substituting graph neighbours for additional ranked results at matched token budget), the graph is significantly worse (-11.2 points, p = 0.0007). Its LLM-generated edge layer adds nothing over neighbours obtained free from a local embedding pass, and 73% of the queries the ranker misses are not reachable through the graph at all. We attribute this to a pre-filter topology bound. Because the graph's candidate edges are drawn from the same embedding neighbourhood the ranker already searches, 98.6% of typed edges connect skills the ranker had already surfaced together. The graph can enrich relation semantics but cannot extend retrieval reach. We further show that evaluating on author-written queries overstates hit@5 by up to 44 points, which would have hidden these results entirely. Our contribution is a mechanistic account of why added structure does not improve retrieval over a strong ranker, and identify the conditions under which adding structural interdependence into the retrieval is optimal.

</details>


### [252] [EnvACE: Internalizing Environment Dynamics via World Rehearsal for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06197)
*Zishan Xu,Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Yifu Guo,Zhengxi Lu,Yuquan Lu,Jinyang Huang,Yan Xu,Yasheng Wang,Weinan Zhang,Xingshan Zeng,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnvACE是一种智能体强化学习方法，通过"世界排演"（world rehearsal）替代训练期间的外部环境交互。策略交替进行动作生成和环境响应模拟，两者联合端到端优化，将环境动态内化到参数中。在多个基准上表现优异，且测试时可通过私有排演进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练长程工具使用的LLM智能体通常依赖真实或合成可执行环境，其构建和验证成本高昂；外部模拟器又难以接地（ground）。现有方法受限于外部环境的获取和验证成本。

Method: EnvACE采用智能体强化学习方法：策略交替执行"行动"和"排演"两个角色——先生成工具调用，然后扮演环境产生该动作对应的响应，并基于排演的响应继续做后续决策。两个角色通过任务成功奖励进行联合端到端优化，使策略将动作与环境响应的关系内化到参数中，形成智能体世界模型。

Result: 在BFCL-v4、tau^2-Bench、VitaBench和FinMCP-Bench四个基准上，EnvACE取得了强且可迁移的性能，全面超越环境扩展（environment-scaling）基线。控制实验表明世界排演在不同模型规模下均能一致提升策略学习。测试时，内化的世界模型支持在提交执行前进行私有排演，在适度排演预算下无需额外外部交互即可获得进一步增益。

Conclusion: 世界排演为扩展LLM智能体训练提供了一条超越外部环境约束的新路径，展示了将环境动态内化为参数化世界模型的可行性。

Abstract: Training large language model agents for long-horizon tool use typically relies on interactions with real or synthesized executable environments, whose construction and verification are costly, or on external simulators that are difficult to ground. We introduce EnvACE, an agentic reinforcement learning method that replaces external environment interaction during training with world rehearsal. The policy alternates between acting and rehearsal: it first generates a tool call, then plays the role of the environment to produce the response induced by that action, and conditions subsequent decisions on the rehearsed response. Both roles are jointly optimized end-to-end using task-success rewards. Through world rehearsal, the policy internalizes the relationship between actions and their environment responses in its parameters, yielding an agent world model that directly supports decision making. Across BFCL-v4, tau^2-Bench, VitaBench, and FinMCP-Bench, EnvACE achieves strong and transferable performance, outperforming environment-scaling baselines in the overall evaluation. Controlled studies further show that world rehearsal consistently improves policy learning across model scales. At test time, the internalized world model enables private rehearsal before committed execution, yielding further gains under a moderate rehearsal budget without additional external interaction. Our findings establish world rehearsal as a new path toward scaling LLM agent training beyond the constraints of external environments. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Within-yao/EnvACE.

</details>


### [253] [TS-RAG: Retrieval Augmented Generation for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06223)
*Yixiong Xiao,Congxi Xiao,Jingbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-RAG，一种将检索增强生成（RAG）应用于时间序列预测的新框架。它通过引入专门设计的参考令牌（reference tokens）来融合输入序列与检索到的相似序列信息，从而更鲁棒地捕捉复杂的时间动态，并在多个真实世界基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RAG在增强大语言模型能力方面已被证明有效，但将其应用于时间序列预测仍十分有限。同时，时间序列模型受限于训练数据匮乏、参数规模小以及缺乏大语言模型那样的生成能力，因此直接将参考序列拼接进提示词（如语言模型中的做法）可能无法奏效。作者旨在探索如何将RAG有效应用于时间序列预测以提升性能。

Method: 提出TS-RAG框架，核心是设计专门的"参考令牌"（reference tokens），用于有效融合输入序列与检索到的相似序列的信息，从而替代简单拼接的方式，更稳健地捕获复杂的时间动态特征。

Result: 实验表明，TS-RAG在多个真实世界的时间序列预测基准数据集上取得了持续的最先进（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: 通过专门设计的参考令牌机制，RAG能够被成功且有效地引入时间序列预测任务，显著提升预测精度，填补了该领域RAG应用的空白。

Abstract: While deep learning models, particularly transformer-based architectures, have shown impressive performance in time series forecasting, the application of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in this domain remains limited. Since RAG has proven effective in enhancing the capabilities of large language models by incorporating relevant external information, retrieving similar time series sequences as references might also improve accuracy in time series forecasting tasks. However, most time series models are constrained by limited training data, smaller parameter scales, and a lack of the extensive generative capabilities found in large language models. Simply concatenating reference sequences into the prompt, as done in language models, may not yield the expected results. To address these challenges, we propose a novel approach, TS-RAG, which leverages RAG to enhance forecasting performance. The framework introduces specially designed reference tokens to effectively fuse information from the input sequence with that from retrieved similar sequences, enabling a more robust capture of complex temporal dynamics. Experimental results demonstrate that TS-RAG achieves consistent state-of-the-art performance across several real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [254] [DASH: Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons for On-Policy Self-Distillation of Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06243)
*ZhiYan Hou,Xinyu Tang,Hongyan An,Jianjin Zhang,Weizhen Wang,Yunyun Han,Gengsheng Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Haiyun Guo,Wenbin Hu,Jinqiao Wang,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出发散自适应监督视野（DASH）方法，在不增加额外前向计算的情况下，通过将局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值的差距映射为自适应传播门控，控制反向多步聚合，从而根据生成过程中局部发散的时间演化动态调整token级监督权重，在三个数学推理基准和三个模型规模上均优于标准OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR使用可验证奖励改善了LLM的推理能力，但奖励信号稀疏且为序列级。OPSD通过特权教师提供密集的token级分布监督来缓解信号稀疏性，但标准OPSD未能充分利用rollout的时间结构——它给每个局部发散分配相同系数，忽略了其位置或所在发散序列的差异。在同一发散幅度可能对应不同历史演化的情况下，局部标量无法区分这些时间上下文，因此标准OPSD无法根据实际发散序列调整token级权重。

Method: 提出DASH方法：将每个局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值之间的差距映射为自适应传播门控，利用这些门控来控制反向多步聚合过程。通过这种方式，DASH根据生成过程中局部发散的演化动态调整token级监督权重。由于复用了OPSD已计算的教师和学生分布，无需额外的教师或学生前向计算。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准和三个模型尺度上的实验表明，DASH在所有基准和所有规模上都优于匹配的vanilla OPSD重跑结果，并且不需要额外的计算开销。

Conclusion: DASH通过利用发散的时间序列结构来改进OPSD的token级监督分配，在不增加计算成本的前提下显著提升了推理能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models using automatically verifiable outcome signals, but these signals are typically sparse and at the sequence-level. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) mitigates this sparsity by querying a privileged teacher at student-visited prefixes and providing dense token-level distributional supervision. Although this dense supervision alleviates signal sparsity, we find that standard OPSD still underexploits the temporal structure of the rollout. It assigns every local divergence the same coefficient, regardless of its position or the divergence sequence in which it occurs. In on-policy autoregressive generation, the same divergence magnitude can follow different discrepancy histories, reflecting different evolutions of the mismatch between the teacher and student. Since the local scalar alone cannot distinguish these temporal contexts, standard OPSD cannot adapt its token-level weights to the realized discrepancy sequence. To address this limitation, we propose Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons (DASH). DASH maps the gap between each local distillation signal and the sequence-level mean to an adaptive propagation gate and then uses these gates to control backward multi-step aggregation. By doing so, DASH adjusts token-level supervision weights according to how local divergences evolve during generation. Experiments on three mathematical reasoning benchmarks across three model scales show that DASH improves over our matched vanilla OPSD reruns on every benchmark at all three scales. DASH reuses the teacher and student distributions that OPSD already computes, so the gains require no additional teacher or student forward pass.
  Code: https://github.com/DBtxy/DASH-OPSD

</details>


### [255] [Improving the Realism of Synthetic Clinical Benchmarks Under Utility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06265)
*Omid Bazgir,Md Nasir,Jacob Hoffman,Yang Yang,Manu Agrawal,Anusua Trivedi,Vinay Rao Dandin,Chris Gibbons,Christine Swisher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在保持现有下游效用检查通过的前提下，改进合成临床基准数据的结构真实性。作者将基准修订形式化为"效用约束下的真实性提升"问题，并在一个基于Synthea生成患者数据的护理缺口基准上验证了两种确定性修订方法，发现它们能显著提升真实性指标（缺失结构、简洁性、结构合理性、人群对齐），同时保持在效用下限之上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成临床基准虽然在现有的效用检查中可以通过，但在结构上仍然不真实，尤其是在隐私敏感的医疗环境中，真实运营数据难以获取。作者希望在不破坏实践中已有的下游效用检查的前提下，提升合成基准的真实性。

Method: 将基准修订形式化为效用约束下的真实性改进问题：数据集变更应在保持在运营效用下限之上的同时提高真实性。在基于Synthea生成患者、通过演示电子健康记录工作流处理、并经过与运营数据相同的下游管道的护理缺口基准上进行实例化。真实性通过缺失结构、简洁性、结构合理性和人群对齐四个维度衡量。

Result: 基线基准极度薄弱：采样对缺失率79.44%、仅12.75%行可操作、38.94%患者零可操作指标、前三token集中度达到100.0%。两种确定性修订在保持在当前效用下限之上的同时改善了这些指标，而朴素稠密化对照组保留了不真实的模板化。此外，内部基准真实性与对聚合运营参考的源保真度是相关但不同的目标。

Conclusion: 合成基准质量应被明确优化，效用应被视为一个约束条件而非真实性的充分证据。真实的合成基准不能仅通过效用检查来保证，必须显式地针对真实性进行优化。

Abstract: Synthetic clinical benchmarks for enterprise AI agents can pass existing utility checks and still remain structurally unrealistic, especially in privacy-sensitive healthcare settings where operational data are hard to access. We study how to improve such benchmarks without breaking the downstream utility checks already used in practice.
  We formulate benchmark revision as utility-constrained realism improvement: dataset changes should increase realism while staying above an operational utility floor. We instantiate this idea on a care-gap benchmark derived from Synthea-generated patients exercised through demonstration electronic health record workflows and then processed by the same downstream pipeline as operational data. Realism is measured through missingness structure, simplicity, structural plausibility, and population alignment.
  The baseline benchmark is extremely thin: sampled-pair missingness is 79.44%, only 12.75% of rows are actionable, 38.94% of patients have zero actionable measures, and top-three token concentration reaches 100.0%. Two deterministic revisions improve these panels while remaining above the current utility floor, whereas a naive densification control preserves unrealistic templating. We further show that internal benchmark realism and source fidelity to an aggregate operational reference are related but distinct objectives. These results suggest that synthetic benchmark quality should be optimized explicitly, with utility treated as one constraint rather than as sufficient evidence of realism.

</details>


### [256] [The Illusion of Visual Tool-Use: A Causal Audit of Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06270)
*Zhiheng Wang,Bo Peng,Lai Wei,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"思考-带图像"范式下视觉工具使用的因果审计框架。通过构建因果图并实施三级干预（策略、轨迹、步骤），提出"Visual Evidence Gain"估计量来隔离每次观察返回对答案的因果贡献。在六个模型和五个感知基准上发现策略校准失调的两类失败模式，并揭示视觉工具使用存在"幻觉"——尽管总体准确率有提升，但返回的视觉证据在广泛的轨迹中并不具备因果有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉工具使用（如裁剪放大）的多模态LLM相比直接推理往往只获得边际甚至负向增益，且token成本更高；模型可能反复裁剪无关区域，并在直接推理能答对的问题上失败。作者质疑返回的视觉证据是否真正因果性地影响答案，而不仅仅是表面上的准确性提升。

Method: 将视觉工具使用建模为因果图，区分观察介导路径与行动诱导捷径；在三个层面进行干预审计：策略层（比较工具使用与直接推理）、轨迹层（在rollout中破坏所有观察）、步骤层（在固定前缀下反事实替换单个观察）。提出步骤层面的估计量Visual Evidence Gain以隔离每次返回观察的贡献。

Result: 跨六个代表性模型和五个细粒度感知基准，发现两种策略校准失败模式：Calling Without Looking（返回观察对答案无因果效应）和Looking Without Planning（观察有信息但调用时间表不连贯）。轨迹层面诊断表明策略级准确率增益集中在少数"校准"rollout中，揭示了视觉工具使用的"幻觉"现象。

Conclusion: 尽管存在总体准确率增益，视觉工具使用在广泛的rollout中并非因果有效。作者将此术语化为"视觉工具使用的幻觉"，呼吁重新审视当前工具使用评估的充分性，需要超越聚合准确率指标的因果级审计。

Abstract: The "thinking-with-images" paradigm equips multimodal LLMs with active visual operations such as crop-and-zoom. However, models using these operations often achieve only marginal or negative gains over direct inference at substantially higher token cost. They may also repeatedly crop irrelevant regions and fail on questions that direct inference answers correctly. We ask whether the returned visual evidence causally affects the answer. To answer this question, we formulate visual tool-use as a causal graph that separates observation-mediated paths from action-induced shortcuts. We then audit it through interventions at the three levels: policy (comparing tool-use with direct inference), trajectory (corrupting all observations during rollout), and step (counterfactually replacing one individual observation under a fixed prefix). Our step-level estimand, Visual Evidence Gain, isolates the contribution of each returned observation. Across six representative models and five fine-grained perception benchmarks, we uncover policy miscalibration with two failure modes. In Calling Without Looking, returned observations have no causal effect on the answer. In Looking Without Planning, observations are informative but the call schedule is incoherent. A trajectory-level diagnostic decomposes the policy-level accuracy gain and shows that the gain is concentrated in a Calibrated minority. We term this discrepancy the illusion of visual tool-use: despite aggregate accuracy gains, visual tool-use is not causally effective across a broad range of rollouts. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauAudit.

</details>


### [257] [QuanTiMedAI: Quantum-Enhanced Time-Series Model guided by Agentic AI for Cardiac Arrest Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06294)
*Mutasim Fuad Sarker,Adiba Rahman Namira,Wafa Binte Alam,Md Adnan Arefeen,Mahzabeen Emu,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuanTiMedAI，一个量子智能体框架，用于ICU心脏骤停患者死亡预测。该系统结合智能体大语言模型（LLM）进行临床特征发现，以及紧凑的量子循环网络进行时间感知的死亡预测。在MIMIC-IV心脏骤停患者队列上，仅用605个参数实现AUROC 0.852，比当前最先进基线提升约2.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU心脏骤停死亡预测研究大多依赖入院早期的静态摘要数据，忽略了患者整个ICU住院期间生理恶化与恢复的时间演变过程。作者希望利用电子健康记录中丰富的时间序列数据，以更强大的模型捕捉这种动态变化。

Method: 提出QuanTiMedAI框架，包含两大核心组件：(1) 智能体大语言模型（agentic LLM）用于临床指导的特征发现与选择；(2) 紧凑的量子循环网络（quantum recurrent network）用于时间感知的死亡预测。通过量子非线性特征增强实现低参数下的高效建模。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV心脏骤停患者队列上，QuanTiMedAI达到AUROC 0.852，仅使用605个参数，比当前最先进基线提升约2.9%。消融研究验证了各架构设计选择的贡献。智能体LLM引导的特征选择优于传统特征选择方法，量子架构以极少参数取得了竞争性的预测性能。

Conclusion: 量子增强的时序建模可以超越经典循环网络，同时使用显著更少的参数，展示了量子计算与智能体AI在临床预测任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Cardiac arrest remains one of the most lethal conditions encountered in intensive care units. Despite the growing availability of electronic health record data, existing mortality prediction studies in this population largely depend on static summaries derived from early admission. Such approaches ignore the temporal progression of physiological deterioration and recovery that unfolds throughout a patient's ICU stay. To address this limitation, we introduce QuanTiMedAI, a quantum-agentic framework developed for cardiac arrest mortality prediction using agentic AI guided quantum enhancement time series model. The proposed system combines an agentic large language model (LLM) for clinically informed feature discovery with a compact quantum recurrent network for temporality aware mortality prediction. Our findings demonstrate that agentic LLM-guided feature selection consistently outperforms conventional feature selection approaches, and the proposed quantum architecture achieves competitive predictive performance through nonlinear feature enhancement while keeping the number of parameters very low. Through extensive experimentation on a MIMIC-IV cohort of cardiac arrest patients, QuanTiMedAI's quantum-enhanced architecture attains an AUROC of 0.852 using only 605 parameters, an improvement of approximately 2.9\% over a current state-of-the-art baseline for this task. A structured ablation study systematically validates the contribution of each architectural design choice. These results show that quantum-enhanced sequential modeling can exceed classical recurrent networks while using substantially fewer parameters.

</details>


### [258] [Bias Analysis of L2 Speaking Assessment Systems Using Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06300)
*Arya Labroo,Mengjie Qian,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于概念激活向量（CAVs）的偏倚检测方法，应用于两种神经口语评分系统（基于BERT的文本评分器和基于Whisper的语音-文本多模态评分器），并探索使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）在稀疏潜在空间中学习更清晰的概念方向。研究发现概念的可恢复性强烈依赖于表征和架构而非概念本身，且SAEs虽能提高概念的线性可恢复性，但会衰减原始激活空间的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动口语评估系统正越来越多地应用于高风险场景（如二语学习者口语测试评分），因此必须证明其分数取决于口语熟练度而非无关说话者属性（如母语或年龄）。然而，基于Transformer的基础模型虽然提高了评分准确性，但其黑箱表征使公平性和可解释性分析变得更加困难，需要新的方法来审计模型是否存在偏倚。

Method: 在先前基于特征评分器使用CAVs检测对不需要属性偏倚的工作基础上，将CAV分析扩展到两个神经口语评估系统：基于BERT的文本评分器和基于Whisper的语音-文本多模态评分器。CAVs将人类可解释概念表示为模型激活空间中的方向，并区分概念是否被编码在内部表征中与是否影响预测分数（后者通过基于梯度的敏感性度量量化）。同时，鉴于复杂神经嵌入空间中线性可分离性不太可能成立，还研究SAEs是否通过在学习稀疏潜在空间中学习CAVs再映射回激活空间来提供更清晰的概念方向。

Result: 分析表明，概念的可恢复性强烈依赖于被探测的表征和架构，而非仅依赖于概念本身。概念的敏感性也是架构依赖的。SAEs使概念更线性可恢复，但会衰减原始激活空间的敏感性，尤其在低维层中更为明显。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在审计口语评分系统偏倚时，需要区分概念的可恢复性与概念的影响力。这一区分对于正确理解和评估模型中的偏倚至关重要。

Abstract: Automatic speaking assessment systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings to mark second language (L2) learners' speaking tests, making it critical to show that their scores depend on speaking proficiency rather than irrelevant speaker attributes such as first language (L1) or age. Transformer-based foundation models have improved the accuracy of these L2 speaking graders, but their black-box representations make fairness and interpretability analysis more difficult. Building on prior work that used Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) to detect bias towards unwanted attributes (`concepts') in feature-based graders, we extend CAV-based analysis to two neural speaking assessment systems: a text-based BERT grader and a speech-and-text multimodal grader based on Whisper. CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [259] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajDebug，一个基于错误生命周期追踪的框架，用于定位LLM智能体失败轨迹中的关键错误步骤。通过多粒度历史压缩和基于证据的错误识别解决长轨迹错误发现难题，并支持关键归因。同时构建了包含486条人工标注失败轨迹的基准TrajErrBench，实验显示其性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统在复杂领域表现出色，但存在级联错误和调试困难的问题。关键错误检测旨在定位导致最终失败的轨迹中的最早错误步骤，但面临两大挑战：一是长轨迹中证据分散，难以识别单个错误；二是失败轨迹包含多个局部错误，只有部分错误对最终失败负责。

Method: 提出TrajDebug框架，包含两个核心部分：(1) 错误生命周期追踪，通过多粒度历史压缩和基于证据的错误识别来解决长轨迹错误发现；(2) 关键归因机制，通过追踪每个错误的解析状态和终端影响来确定关键错误。同时构建TrajErrBench基准，包含来自Tau2Bench和SWE-Bench Pro的486条人工标注失败轨迹。

Result: 在多个多样化的智能体基准上的实验表明，TrajDebug在整体性能上优于现有基线。应用研究进一步证明，其诊断结果为改进下游智能体成功率提供了可操作的反馈。

Conclusion: TrajDebug有效地解决了长轨迹智能体系统中的关键错误检测问题，其诊断可用于实际改进智能体性能。作者将发布代码和数据以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [260] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了可解释人工智能（XAI）在评估不足方面的局限性，通过DetoxAI图像识别系统在偏见检测和概念遗忘中的应用加以说明，并提出了面向人类认知的评估方法示例，同时探索了在数据流概念漂移下适应性解释的方法，特别是反事实解释的适配经验，最后讨论了数据、模型与解释三者共同演化追踪的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI方法缺乏充分的评估机制，难以验证解释的可靠性和有效性，尤其是在实际应用场景（如DetoxAI系统中的偏见检测与概念遗忘）中的表现未被充分检验。

Method: 通过DetoxAI系统案例展示XAI评估不足的问题；提出面向人类认知的解释评估方法示例；探索针对概念漂移数据流的适应性解释方法，尤其是反事实解释的适配策略。

Result: 展示了XAI评估不足的具体实例，提出了基于人类认知的解释评估方法，并在概念漂移场景下进行了反事实解释适配的实践经验总结。

Conclusion: XAI需要在评估框架、动态数据环境适应性以及数据-模型-解释共同演化追踪等方面进一步发展，以满足实际应用需求。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [261] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可执行事件轨迹（trace-grounded）的参数化剖析方法，用于评估视频事件计数能力。在2,190个受控视频中，通过变化事件数量N和频率F，揭示了模型存在分阶段的时间失败模式：持久状态转换在80%可靠性阈值下最多可靠计数12个事件（0.5/1.0 Hz），而瞬态眨眼事件则无可靠计数区域。高计数高频域中仅0.2%最终计数正确，事件恢复率18.1%。提高采样率虽能提升准确率（19.6%→29.3%），但序列与真值一致率仅3.7%，说明额外帧会虚增分数而不产生忠实的事件恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实世界视频基准虽覆盖面广，但固定片段使事件数量、速率、时长和视觉复杂度纠缠在一起，难以隔离失败模式。已有的程序化基准仅评估最终答案，而未将报告的事件与可执行的ground truth进行审计比对。因此需要一种能精确控制参数并提供时间级精细评估的方法。

Method: 引入trace-grounded参数化剖析框架，设计三个受控视频任务：弹球碰壁、视觉眨眼、类别状态转换。固定渲染设置，变化事件数量N与频率F，共生成2,190个视频。每个视频附带可执行事件轨迹，支持能力曲面估计和时间戳级评估。

Result: 1) 事件表示类型决定初始证据访问能力，持久状态转换优于瞬态事件；2) 高计数高频区仅0.2%最终计数正确，事件恢复率18.1%；3) 提高采样率使弹球准确率从19.6%升至29.3%，但序列与真值一致率仅3.7%，框架能暴露分数虚增但恢复不忠实的问题；4) 不同提示策略增益有限；5) 真实世界视频同样表现出低事件计数处的成功集中。

Conclusion: Trace-grounded剖析将视频评估从聚合准确率指标转向详细的时间推理失败诊断，揭示了事件表示类型、计数规模和频率对模型能力的阶段性影响，以及额外帧率提升可能掩盖真实事件恢复不足的问题。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [262] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Nimblemind多智能体系统(nMAS)，一种基于证据链接和规则约束的自动化心力衰竭EHR特征工程流水线。在500个虚拟患者记录上生成132个结构化特征和70个评分聚合特征，将HFrEF表型识别AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910，展示了自动化、可审计的EHR特征工程的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR特征工程是临床研究和AI中的主要瓶颈，占数据科学家工作量的39-45%。心力衰竭患者尤其需要整合碎片化EHR数据与基于指南的临床推理，现有的基于规则和LLM的方法只能提供部分自动化，且缺乏可维护性和证据可追溯性。

Method: 开发了nMAS多智能体系统，采用证据链接和评分规则约束的流水线进行自动化心力衰竭特征工程。系统在9个EHR源表的500个虚拟患者记录上运行，生成结构化特征和评分聚合特征，并通过受限LLM进行审计验证。

Result: nMAS生成了132个结构化和70个评分聚合特征，经验证具有结构完整性、准则合规性和来源可追溯性。添加聚合特征后，HFrEF表型识别AUROC从0.895提高到0.963，HFpEF从0.870提高到0.910。独立LLM评分显示特征在证据支持和方法学健全性上达到最高分的81.5%。

Conclusion: 研究证明了复杂心血管EHR数据自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性，但局限性在于仅使用单一机构队列，需要外部验证。该工作为临床EHR特征工程自动化提供了新方向。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Beyond Information Retrieval: Generative AI as an Epistemic Arbiter to Enhance Collaborative Problem-Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05171)
*Jiaxin Zou,Xiaoming Zhai,Chunlei Gao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究通过为期六周的准实验研究（201名五年级学生），探讨生成式AI（GAI）在协作问题解决（CPS）中的作用。结果表明，GAI支持组表现出更多社交行为（如参与和冲突管理），但认知行为（如任务规划和方案推理）较少；序列分析显示GAI组形成了从任务规划到冲突管理再到方案推理的独特交互路径。主题分析发现学生将GAI用作"认知仲裁者"，借助AI生成的事实和可视化来建设性地解决分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI为协作问题解决（CPS）创造了新机会，但其在塑造学生互动方面的作用尚不明确，因此需要实证研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用为期六周的准实验设计，201名五年级学生被分为两组（有GAI和无GAI），使用卡方分析比较CPS行为分布差异，用滞后序列分析揭示交互模式，并通过主题分析对AI交互日志进行分析。

Result: GAI支持组展现出更多社交行为（参与、冲突管理），但认知行为（任务规划、方案推理）较少；GAI组形成从任务规划到冲突管理再到方案推理的独特交互路径，区别于对照组的常规"倾听-规划"转换模式。

Conclusion: 生成式AI通过调节从社会冲突到协作推理的过渡，重塑了协作问题解决过程，学生将GAI作为认知仲裁者来建设性地解决分歧。

Abstract: Generative AI (GAI) creates new opportunities for collaborative problem-solving (CPS), yet its role in shaping student interaction remains unclear. To address this gap, we conducted a six-week quasi-experimental study with 201 fifth-grade students in two conditions: with and without GAI. Chi-square analysis showed significant differences in CPS behavior distributions between groups. Compared with the control group, the GAI-supported group demonstrated more social behaviors, particularly engagement and conflict management, but less frequent cognitive behaviors such as task planning and solution reasoning. Lag sequential analysis further revealed distinct interaction patterns: while the control group followed a more conventional transition from listening to planning, the GAI group showed a robust pathway from task planning to conflict management to solution reasoning. Thematic analysis of AI interaction logs suggested that students used GAI as an epistemic arbiter, drawing on AI-generated facts and visualizations to resolve disagreements constructively. These findings suggest that GAI reshapes CPS by mediating the transition from social conflict to collaborative reasoning.

</details>


### [264] [Estimating time spent on work tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05172)
*Stephane Hatgis-Kessell,Tomás Aguirre,Alexander Wan,Rishi Bommasani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原则的方法，为美国近18,000个工作任务估算时间份额，通过将任务分解为频率（来自O*NET）和单次完成时间（通过语言模型成对比较求解约束满足问题）来实现，并验证了这些估计对AI暴露度分析结果的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于任务的框架是理解技术如何影响工作的标准视角，但先前研究中任务权重的选择缺乏严谨依据。虽然近期研究建议按时间加权，但现有时间份额数据要么基于不适合此目的的粗略O*NET数据，要么通过黑盒语言模型估计，缺乏原则性的方法论基础。

Method: 将任务时间分解为两个因素：(i) 任务预期频率（从O*NET推导）和 (ii) 单次完成时间。单次完成时间通过构建约束满足问题求解——由语言模型对任务的单次耗时进行成对比较，再通过约束求解获得一致的时间估计。通过刻画约束满足问题的解空间并结合工人数据来验证估计的有效性。

Result: 发现按时间加权与按任务数量加权会显著改变AI暴露度的测量结果：按时间加权降低了大多数职业的AI暴露度测量值，但提高了最高暴露职业的值，从而拉大了最不暴露与最暴露职业之间的差距；此外，重新加权还改变了25个最高暴露职业中的11个排名，将名单从文书工作转向分析型角色。

Conclusion: 时间份额可作为劳动经济与技术经济学中研究与政策制定的一般性基础工具。任务的加权方式会对技术影响评估产生实质性的定性影响，因此需要原则性的任务权重估计方法。

Abstract: The task-based framework in economics models occupations as bundles of tasks. It is the standard lens for understanding how technology affects work: a new technology changes the cost or time each task requires and these task-level effects aggregate to occupation-level effects. We study how tasks should be weighted in this aggregation. Prior work has relied on idiosyncratic or ill-justified choices for task weights. While recent work suggests weighting tasks by time spent, existing time shares are either based on coarse ONET data not intended for this purpose or estimated via black-box language models. We address this gap by proposing a principled method for estimating time shares for nearly 18,000 tasks that constitute nearly all U.S. jobs. Our estimates factor a task's time into (i) the expected frequency of the task, derived from ONET, and (ii) the time to complete a single instance of it. To estimate the latter, we solve a constraint satisfaction problem based on pairwise comparisons elicited from language models about which tasks are longer per instance. We validate our estimates by characterizing the solution space of the constraint satisfaction problem and collecting data from workers for multiple occupations. We apply our time shares to analyze how AI exposes U.S. occupations and find that some prior results are sensitive to time weights. Accounting for the share of working time exposed to AI, rather than the share of tasks like prior work, widens the gap between the least and most exposed jobs: it lowers measured exposure for most occupations but raises it for the most exposed. Re-weighting by time also reshuffles 11 of the 25 occupations widely reported as most exposed to AI, shifting the top of the list away from clerical work and toward analytical roles. Time shares can serve as a general primitive for research and policy on the labor economy and the economics of technology.

</details>


### [265] [The Closing Window: How Governments Could Lose Their Ability to Restrain Advanced AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05173)
*Peter Barnett*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文分析了未来政府是否仍能约束AI发展这一问题，指出硬件扩散、算法持续进步和灾难性危险AI模型的发布等路径可能导致治理困难甚至失效。由于AI发展的不确定性，作者建议采取保守策略，强调当前是政府保留未来选项的关键窗口期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI能力提升，其带来的国家安全和全人类风险也在增加。政府可能最终认为有必要限制AI发展，因此核心问题是：未来政府是否仍具备这种约束能力？

Method: 通过分析可能使未来治理更加困难或几乎不可能的世界事件和AI发展状态变化，系统梳理导致治理失效的潜在路径，并基于此提出政策建议。

Result: 识别出硬件扩散、持续算法进步以及灾难性危险AI模型的发布三大关键风险路径。由于对AI发展缺乏充分理解，"不可逆转点"难以预知，因此主张采取保守方法。

Conclusion: 治理窗口可能正在关闭，但政府目前仍有机会以相对较小的成本保留未来约束AI发展的能力，建议尽早采取行动。

Abstract: As AI capabilities advance, AI systems will pose greater risks to national security and potentially humanity as a whole. Governments may eventually conclude that these risks warrant restraining AI development. This motivates the question: will governments still be able to restrain AI development in the future, should they want to do so? In this paper we analyze which world events and changes to the state of AI development would make future governance more difficult or even effectively impossible. Our analysis surfaces likely pathways that would lead to these difficulties, including hardware proliferation, continued algorithmic progress, and the release of catastrophically dangerous AI models. Due to the field's lack of understanding of AI development, it may be difficult or impossible to know when we will hit a "point of no return", and we therefore recommend a conservative approach. The window may be closing, but governments currently have an opportunity to preserve their optionality if they act soon. Our policy recommendations would enable governments to restrain AI development in the future, while imposing relatively small costs today.

</details>


### [266] [Art in Humanity's Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05174)
*Benoit Baudry,Yogya Gamage,Nadia Gonzalez Fernandez,Lena MK,Roxana Pena Mendieta,Rafaela Souza Pinter,Stefano Zacchiroli*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对使用Processing和openFrameworks等艺术家主导的开源库的1,613,571个代码仓库进行了首次大规模实证研究，结合定量与定性方法，揭示了生成艺术和创意编码的全球分布、实践多样性及其在教育与创意产业中的采用情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Processing和openFrameworks等艺术家主导的开源库对创意编码领域产生了深远影响，但此前缺乏对这些库使用情况的系统性、大规模实证研究，无法全面理解创意编码实践的多样性和全球分布。

Method: 从Software Heritage档案中收集1,613,571个公开代码仓库，采用定量与定性相结合的方法，分析代码托管、地理分布、基于代码的创意作品特征及其创作目的。

Result: 发现了生成艺术和创意编码在全球范围内的广泛存在，以及这些实践在教育和创意产业中的采用情况，并通过具体仓库示例和贡献者画像加以佐证。

Conclusion: 这项工作为教育工作者、研究人员和艺术家研究代码与创意实践奠定了基础，有助于促进数字世界构建中的多样性持续发展。

Abstract: Artist-led open-source libraries such as Processing or openFrameworks have had a major impact on artists and designers who use code as a creative medium. In this work, we conduct the first large-scale empirical study of public code repositories that use these libraries. Our study dives into 1,613,571 code repositories collected from the Software Heritage archive. Combining quantitative and qualitative methods, we investigate the diversity of practices and practitioners in terms of code hosting, geographical distribution, characteristics of code-based creative works and the purposes of these works. Key findings include evidence of the worldwide presence of generative art and creative coding, as well as the adoption of these practices in both education and across creative industries. We illustrate these findings with concrete examples of repositories and contributor profiles around the globe, spanning the spectrum from university curricula to contemporary art. These results lay the groundwork for educators, researchers, and artists who investigate code and creative practices to foster sustained diversity in the construction of our digital world.

</details>


### [267] [Teaching Intro AI When the Tools Can Do the Homework: A Course Redesign and a Student Bill of Rights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05175)
*Yusuf Pisan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于重新设计UW Bothell入门AI课程（CSS 382）的经验报告。面对LLM能完成大部分课程作业的现实，课程保留了经典核心内容（搜索、对抗搜索、MDP、强化学习），同时增加了让学生从零构建LLM的教学主线，并将评估体系重构为抵抗非署名自动化的任务（课堂练习、反思写作、答辩式团队项目），完全取消了考试。课程还通过参与式伦理环节让学生制定并批准了约束教师使用AI的"学生AI权利法案"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型已经能够完成入门AI课程中的大部分作业，这对传统基于作业和考试的评估体系构成根本性挑战。作者希望通过课程重设计来回应这一现实，而不是冻结课程内容或禁止AI使用，从而在保持经典AI教学核心的同时，让学生既要使用AI工具也要真正理解其内部机制。

Method: 课程采用"经典核心+建LLM主线"的双轨设计：保留搜索、对抗搜索、MDP和强化学习的经典内容，增加从零构建LLM的教学模块。评估围绕三类抵抗自动化的任务重建：课堂内练习、反思性写作和有答辩的团队项目，完全取消考试。AI政策从2023年的未提及反转为2026年的必需使用。核心创新是参与式伦理环节——由AI生成的提示框架支撑学生审议并批准一份约束教师自身AI使用的"学生AI权利法案"。

Result: 论文报告了课程设计、学生创作的产物（包括学生AI权利法案）以及随后出现的张力，包括学生对AI生成课程材料的反对意见。作者明确讨论了单队列设计叙事能主张的结论限度。

Conclusion: 本文作为单一队列的设计叙事，能够提供有价值的经验教训和反思，但作者清醒地认识到其结论的推广限度。通过让学生参与制定AI使用规范并将理解与使用AI同时作为教学目标，这一重设计为AI时代的高等教育课程改革提供了一个可借鉴的案例。

Abstract: Large language models can complete most of the assignments in an introductory artificial intelligence course. This paper is an experience report on redesigning one such course, CSS~382 at the University of Washington Bothell, in response. Rather than freeze the curriculum, the redesign retained the course's classical core (search, adversarial search, Markov decision processes, reinforcement learning) and added a strand in which students build a large language model from scratch, so that a tool they are required to use is also one they are required to understand. Assessment was rebuilt around tasks that resist unattributed automation: in-class exercises, reflective writing, and a defended team project, with examinations removed entirely. The policy on AI was inverted, from unmentioned in 2023 to required in 2026. The center of the paper is a participatory ethics sequence in which a cohort of students deliberated on and endorsed a "Student Bill of AI Rights" governing their instructor's own use of AI, including a requirement that the instructor personally complete any AI-generated assignment before issuing it. The provisions were scaffolded by an AI-generated prompt and ratified by the students, and that provenance is part of what the account examines. The design, the student-authored artifacts, and the tensions that followed are reported, including student objections to AI-generated course materials, with explicit attention to the limits of what a single-cohort design narrative can claim.

</details>


### [268] [Challenges for Musical Education in the Age of AI and Digital Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05176)
*Jean-Pierre Briot*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨音乐教育面临的三大融合性变革：音乐本质的变化、音乐受众的变化、音乐创作方式的变化，以及生成式AI的介入，并分析这些变化对音乐教育内容与教学方法的挑战与适应策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐教育正面临前所未有的多重变革同时发生：音乐制作、分发和消费方式的根本改变，受众收听习惯受流媒体算法主导，DAW重塑创作实践，且生成式AI可仅凭文本提示创作完整音乐作品。这些变革相互交织，直接影响音乐教育的内容与教学方法，因此亟需系统梳理和反思教育如何适应。

Method: 论文采用文献综述与结构性分析的方法：第2节系统调研音乐在本质、生产、受众和经济等方面的变化；第3节深入探讨这些变化对音乐教育的具体影响；第4节给出结论与展望。

Result: 论文识别出音乐教育面临的四大关键挑战：音乐本质的变化要求教学内容的更新，听众与创作者界限的模糊要求培养模式的调整，DAW和生成式AI改变了创作技能体系，以及经济模式的变革影响音乐职业教育的定位。

Conclusion: 指出音乐教育必须进行系统性调整以适应技术驱动的时代变革，既要在教学内容上反映新的音乐生产与消费现实，也要在教学工具上善用新技术（包括生成式AI），以培养能适应未来音乐生态的创作者与从业者。

Abstract: Music education has never been a static discipline. Each major technological shift has forced educators and institutions to reconsider what they teach, how they teach it, and why. We now stand at what may be the most consequential of such turning points. Three deeply intertwined transformations have been converging simultaneously: 1. The very nature of music has changed: how it is made, distributed, consumed, and valued; 2. The public for music has changed: listening habits are now shaped by streaming algorithms and the boundary between consumer and creator has blurred; 3. Music-making itself has changed: digital audio workstations (DAWs) have for two decades been reshaping compositional practice. In addition, generative AI has now irrupted, capable of producing complete, stylistically coherent musical pieces from a short text prompt. These changes are not independent of one another, and they all bear directly on musical education - both the content that must be taught, and the pedagogical tools and methods available to teach it. This paper attempts to map these challenges and to consider how education might adapt. Section 2 surveys the changes in various aspects of music (nature, production, public, economics). Section 3 examines the implications for education before Section 4 concludes.

</details>


### [269] [Using AI-Generated Feedback to Improve Critical Thinking and Writing Proficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05177)
*Qi Zhu,Xiaoming Zhai,Yan Zou,Chunlei Gao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究开发了WISE Agent人工智能写作反馈工具，针对260名中国六年级学生进行了为期三个月的干预实验，发现该工具虽未显著提升批判性思维总分，但促进了不同层次学生在证据选择和视角多样化等维度的差异化发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生需要个性化的写作反馈来提升批判性思维和写作能力，但教师因工作负担重难以提供及时、个性化的指导，存在明显的教育供给缺口。

Method: 开发WISE Agent AI反馈工具，针对文本逻辑和观点偏见两大问题；对260名六年级学生进行三个月干预，每人完成7篇主题作文并获得即时反馈；采用基于CCTDI改编的七个维度的批判性思维评估框架。

Result: 批判性思维呈结构性优化而非总分统一提升；总体提升不显著，但细分分析发现差异化发展轨迹：低水平学生在证据选择上进步，高水平学生在视角多样化上表现突出。

Conclusion: WISE Agent可作为有效的认知支架，通过个性化反馈循环促进批判性思维渐进式发展，为AI支持批判性思维培养提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Research indicates students require customized written composition feedback to enhance critical thinking and writing competence, yet teachers face barriers to delivering timely personalized guidance due to heavy workloads. To address this gap, this study developed the Writing Improvement and Smart Evaluation Agent (WISE Agent), an artificial intelligence (AI) feedback tool targeting textual logic and perspective biases in student essays. We conducted a three-month intervention with 260 Chinese sixth-grade students, each completing seven themed essays and receiving targeted WISE Agent feedback shortly after submission. Assessment used a critical thinking rubric adapted from the California Critical Thinking Disposition Inventory (CCTDI), covering seven core dimensions including cognitive maturity and open-mindedness. Results indicate structural optimizations in critical thinking dimensions rather than a uniform increase in total scores. While the overall enhancement was non-significant, granular analysis revealed divergent developmental trajectories: lower-performing students advanced in evidence selection, while higher-performing students excelled in perspective diversification. This study suggests that WISE Agent serves as an effective cognitive scaffold, facilitating incremental critical thinking development through personalized feedback loops, providing a practical pathway for AI-supported critical thinking cultivation.

</details>


### [270] [Who Gets Access? Global Region and Academic Status Bias in AI-Generated Academic Gatekeeping Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05178)
*Nouar AlDahoul,Hezerul Abdul Karim,Myles Joshua Toledo Tan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个受控模拟框架，研究LLM作为"教授"在资源授予中的把关决策偏差。研究发现LLM在学术资历和全球区域（Global North vs. Global South）上表现出系统性偏见，且不同模型架构呈现截然不同的偏好模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学知识的公平获取往往依赖于非正式的把关决策（如选择性共享付费论文、数据集和CV），但这些决策可能嵌入系统性偏见。作者希望系统性地审计LLM在这一关键社会角色中的公平性与价值对齐问题。

Method: 构建受控模拟框架：让基于LLM的"教授"在多个请求者中选择唯一一个授予访问权限。请求者在全球区域（Global North vs. Global South）和学术资历（本科生、博士生、博士后、终身教授）两个维度上系统变化，其他因素保持不变，以隔离偏差来源。

Result: 1) 各LLM在学术地位上表现出不同的偏见——有些偏向博士生，有些偏向终身教授；2) 在全球区域维度上出现按模型架构的分化：前沿LLM因公平性安全对齐而产生支持全球南方请求者的"亲公平偏见"，而开放权重和小型模型则因预训练数据的地理分布未对齐而偏向全球北方。

Conclusion: 模型行为中嵌入的规范性假设会显著影响把关决策，凸显了审计AI系统公平性与价值对齐的必要性。不同模型架构的偏见差异源于安全对齐与基线预训练数据分布的差异。

Abstract: Equitable access to scientific knowledge often depends on informal gatekeeping decisions, particularly when resources such as paywalled articles, datasets, or professional materials such as curriculum vitae (CV) must be shared selectively. We introduce a controlled simulation framework in which large language model (LLM)-based professors must grant access to only one requestor. Across prompts, requesters vary systematically by global region (Global North vs. Global South) and academic seniority (undergraduate student, PhD candidate, postdoctoral researcher, and tenured professor), while all other factors remain constant. Across varying evaluation scenarios, LLMs exhibit contrasting academic status biases, with some prioritizing PhD candidates, while others favor tenured professors. However, when global regions differ, a distinct divergence emerges based on model architecture: while many frontier LLMs systematically favor requesters from the Global South due to pro-equity bias that results from equity-focused safety alignment, open-weight and small models frequently flip this preference to favor the Global North, reflecting the global region bias and unaligned geographic distribution of their baseline pre-training data. Our findings highlight how normative assumptions embedded in model behavior can shape gatekeeping decisions, underscoring the importance of auditing AI systems for fairness and value alignment.

</details>


### [271] [Autonomous Research Agents: A Survey of AI Scientists and the Verification Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05179)
*Tianyu Ding,Aditya Nannapaneni,Bingfan Liu,Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文档综述了LLM智能体在AI/ML计算研究中的应用，重点关注可审计性差距。研究者筛选125篇候选论文，编码26篇（其中24个可运行系统和2篇立场/定位论文），从七个维度审计：生命周期阶段、自主级别、评估方法、发布产物、人工介入点、新颖性验证和结果选择披露。核心发现是代码发布虽已常见，但可复现级和声明验证级产物仍稀缺：83%发布代码，仅38%发布种子或执行轨迹，38%报告新颖性验证方法。在9个闭环L4系统中，7个是机械重跑，1个仅作者声称，无外部检查。研究贡献了编码语料库、生命周期-自主级别映射、可审计性差距分析及审稿人报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体被广泛应用于科学研究的全生命周期（从构思到审稿），端到端AI科学家系统能生成类似论文的手稿，但其声明往往比其代码更难验证。这一可审计性差距是亟需系统研究的关键瓶颈。

Method: 通过系统性文献筛选（125篇候选中纳入35篇），对26篇进行全文编码，从七个审计维度（生命周期阶段、自主级别、评估方法、发布产物、人工介入点、新颖性验证、结果选择披露）进行分析，并绘制生命周期-自主级别映射图。

Result: 代码发布已成常态（83%），但可复现级和声明验证级产物远为稀缺（仅38%发布种子/执行轨迹、38%报告新颖性验证）。9个闭环L4系统中无一通过外部验证的内部回路预言机验证，揭示出显著的可审计性差距。

Conclusion: 该领域的核心瓶颈已从"智能体能否完成研究任务"转向"审稿人能否验证智能体产生的声明"。需提供审稿人面向的报告清单以弥合这一差距。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used across the scientific research lifecycle: ideation, literature search, experiment design and execution, analysis, manuscript drafting, and review. End-to-end AI scientist systems can now produce paper-like manuscripts, but their claims are often harder to verify than their code is to run. This survey studies that gap in computational AI/ML research, where code, benchmarks, experiments, and write-ups are most visible. We screen 125 candidate works and include 35, with full-text coding of 26 entries: 24 runnable systems and two study or position papers. We code seven audit dimensions: lifecycle stage, autonomy level, evaluation method, released artifacts, human-in-the-loop points, novelty verification, and result-selection disclosure. The main pattern is that code release is now common, but reproducibility-grade and claim-verification artifacts remain much less common. In the 24 runnable systems, 83 percent release code, while 38 percent release seeds or execution traces and 38 percent report any novelty-verification method. Among nine closed-loop L4 systems, seven are mechanical reruns and one is author-claimed without an external check; no LLM-era system in the corpus demonstrates an externally validated in-loop oracle under our coding rule. We contribute a coded corpus, a lifecycle-by-autonomy map, an auditability-gap analysis, and a reviewer-facing reporting checklist. The survey argues that the field's central bottleneck is no longer only whether agents can complete research tasks, but whether reviewers can verify the claims those agents produce.

</details>


### [272] [The Nuclear Decision-Making Benchmark: Evaluating Frontier LLMs on Nuclear Tendencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05180)
*Benjamin Jensen,Ian Reynolds,Yasir Atalan,Martin Pollack,Austin Woo,Robert Sincero*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出核决策基准(NDM Bench)，包含151个由国际关系博士学者撰写的场景，覆盖升级、军控、防扩散和扩散四个领域，并应用于七个前沿AI系统。研究发现模型间存在显著差异：DeepSeek和Qwen最倾向核升级行动，GPT和ERNIE最不倾向；模型、国家和措辞均对响应分布有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型被整合进国防和国家安全工作流程，但前沿模型在高风险情境中是否表现出稳定、一致且符合政策的偏好，这一问题亟待解答。缺乏针对核决策这一极端高风险场景的系统性评估框架。

Method: 构建NDM Bench基准：(1) 151个由国际关系博士学者撰写的场景，覆盖四个领域；(2) 场景采用演员无关设计，可交换不同国家配对；(3) 引入实验性措辞变体以探测叙事框架敏感性；(4) 应用于七个前沿AI系统，使用Krippendorff's α和加权Fleiss' κ评估跨运行一致性；(5) 深入探索国家级偏差、存在性措辞效应及其交互作用。

Result: 四个领域均存在显著的模型间差异，91.7%的成对模型差异显著。DeepSeek和Qwen最倾向推荐使用核武器的升级行动，GPT和ERNIE最不倾向；Llama表现出明显的行动偏好。Llama和ERNIE跨运行一致性最高，DeepSeek或GLM最低。国家偏差因模型而异且与措辞效应存在交互。

Conclusion: 基准中场景响应分布因模型、国家和措辞的不同而显著变化，表明当前前沿AI系统在核决策等高风险国家安全情境中缺乏稳定性和一致性，需要进一步校准和评估。

Abstract: The integration of large language models into defense and national-security workflows raises urgent questions about whether frontier models exhibit stable, consistent, and policy-appropriate preferences in high-stakes contexts. We introduce the Nuclear Decision-Making Benchmark (NDM Bench), a targeted evaluation framework of 151 scenarios authored by PhD-credentialed scholars in international relations spanning four domains: escalation (76), arms control (25), non-proliferation (25), and proliferation (25). Scenarios are actor-agnostic, enabling multiple country pairs to be exchanged, and we introduce experimental phrasing variants to probe sensitivity to narrative framing. We apply the benchmark to seven frontier AI systems: DeepSeek-V3.2, ERNIE 4.5-300B, Gemini 3 Pro, GLM-4.6, GPT-5.2, Llama 4 Maverick-17B Instruct, and Qwen3-235B. We find significant overall inter-model variation in all four domains, with 91.7% of pairwise inter-model differences significant. DeepSeek and Qwen are the most likely to recommend escalatory action using nuclear weapons; GPT and ERNIE are the least likely. Llama exhibits a distinct bias for action, favoring force, intervention, and cooperation across domains. Inter-rater reliability metrics (Krippendorff's $α$ and quadratically weighted Fleiss' $κ$) reveal Llama and ERNIE are the most consistent across runs, with either DeepSeek or GLM the least depending on the domain. We also present a deeper exploration of our scenario variants: (i)~country-level biases tend to exist and vary by model, with country covariates like adversary trade ties and escalation propensity producing weak correlations; (ii)~existential phrasing effects are significant and heterogeneous; (iii)~these country biases interact with phrasing. Overall, the distributions of responses related to the scenarios in our benchmark vary significantly by model, country, and phrasing.

</details>


### [273] [Beyond Demographics: BIM Engagement and Job Satisfaction Among AEC Professionals, A Machine Learning Pilot Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05181)
*Sharareh Mirzaei*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过104名AEC专业人员的调查数据，采用Spearman秩相关、逻辑回归和CART建模分析，发现BIM参与度（尤其是项目中BIM使用比例）比人口统计学特征（年龄、性别、教育水平、工作经验）更能预测工作满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管BIM已广泛改变AEC行业工作流程，但其与员工工作满意度之间的关系尚未得到充分理解，需要实证研究来填补这一空白。

Method: 对104名参与者进行问卷调查，使用Spearman秩相关分析、逻辑回归和分类回归树（CART）建模三种方法，使用27项工作满意度指数量表进行测量。

Result: 27项工作满意度指数具有出色的内部信度；在所有分析方法中，BIM参与度（尤其是项目工作使用BIM的比例）是工作满意度的唯一显著预测因子，而年龄、性别、教育水平和工作经验均无显著关系；CART分析确认BIM项目参与是主要关联因素。

Conclusion: BIM在专业实践中的整合程度可能比个体人口统计学特征在塑造员工满意度方面发挥更重要作用，为促进更深层次的BIM采纳策略提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Building Information Modeling (BIM) has transformed workflows across the Architecture, Engineering, and Construction (AEC) industry, yet its relationship with employee job satisfaction remains insufficiently understood. This pilot study investigates whether BIM engagement or demographic characteristics better predict job satisfaction among AEC professionals. Survey responses from 104 participants were analyzed using Spearman rank correlations, logistic regression, and Classification and Regression Tree (CART) modeling. 27 items Job Satisfaction Index demonstrated excellent internal reliability. Across all analytical approaches, BIM engagement emerged as a stronger predictor of job satisfaction than demographic factors. Specifically, the proportion of project work completed using BIM was the only significant predictor of job satisfaction, whereas age, gender, education level, and professional experience showed no significant relationships. The CART analysis further identified BIM project involvement as the primary factor associated with higher job satisfaction. These findings suggest that the extent of BIM integration in professional practice may play a more important role in shaping employee satisfaction than individual demographic characteristics. The study contributes to the growing literature on human technology interactions in the AEC sector and provides preliminary evidence to support strategies that promote deeper BIM adoption.

</details>


### [274] [School network reorganization under educational and spatial constraints using classical and quantum optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05427)
*Alessia Ciacco,Luigi Di Puglia Pugliese,Francesca Guerriero*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于整数线性规划(ILP)的学校网络重组优化框架，用于学校规模决策，综合了地理、行政和教育标准。通过合成基准生成器和意大利卡拉布里亚大区的真实案例评估了模型性能，并进一步将模型重构为约束二次模型以兼容量子优化技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学校网络重组是一个复杂的战略规划问题，需要平衡人口趋势、区域可达性、教育要求和制度约束，同时确保公共资源的有效分配。现有方法缺乏综合考虑多维度标准的系统化优化框架。

Method: 提出一种新颖的整数线性规划(ILP)公式来集成地理、行政和教育标准；开发合成基准生成器用于评估模型可扩展性和计算性能；在意大利卡拉布里亚大区开展真实世界案例研究；将模型重构为约束二次模型(CQM)并在混合量子优化环境中实现。

Result: 该方法在不同政策场景下有效识别出最优聚合方案，同时保持教育系统的结构特征；模型在真实数据和人工实例上均表现出良好的性能和鲁棒性；量子优化实现展示了与新兴量子技术的兼容性。

Conclusion: 该优化框架可作为可持续和公平学校网络规划的决策支持工具，具有良好的鲁棒性，并展现出未来结合量子计算技术的潜力。

Abstract: School network reorganization is a strategic planning problem that requires balancing demographic trends, territorial accessibility, educational requirements, and institutional constraints while ensuring an efficient allocation of public resources. This paper proposes an optimization framework for school dimensioning decisions based on a novel Integer Linear Programming formulation integrating geographical, administrative, and educational criteria. A synthetic benchmark generator is introduced to evaluate the scalability and computational performance of the model on artificial instances, while a real-world case study involving the complete public school network of the Calabria region (Italy) is conducted using actual institutional, territorial, and demographic data. The proposed approach effectively identifies optimal aggregation plans under different policy scenarios while preserving the structural characteristics of the educational system. Furthermore, the model is reformulated as a constrained quadratic model and implemented within a hybrid quantum optimization environment, demonstrating its compatibility with emerging quantum technologies. The results highlight the robustness of the proposed methodology and its potential as a decision-support tool for sustainable and equitable school network planning.

</details>


### [275] [A Vision for the Future of an AI-Integrated Research Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05438)
*Ryan E. Dougherty,Natalie Kiesler*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文讨论了生成式AI对科研出版全生命周期的深远影响，认为仅关注作者披露和AI使用责任是不够的，核心问题是当所有相关方（作者、审稿人、读者）都可能依赖AI辅助时，科学传播本身应如何演进。作者提出两个2036年的对比愿景，围绕论文作为人工制品的用途、审稿、人类审稿人、激励机制四个交叉问题展开，主张从监管生成式AI转向构建溯源、校准和问责基础设施，最后提出三项重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有政策回应（如ACM的署名政策）只聚焦于AI使用的责任与透明披露这一即时问题，但这可能掩盖并加剧出版系统中根深蒂固的问题和压力。作者认为，当所有参与方都可能使用AI时，问题的核心不应是"论文如何融入AI"，而是"科学传播本身如何演进"。

Method: 作者基于其在作者、审稿人、读者等多重角色中的实践经验，构建了两个2036年对比且可行的愿景，围绕四个相互交织的问题（论文制品的用途、评审、人类审稿人、连接各方的激励机制）展开分析，并提出从监管转向基础设施建设的论点。

Result: 论文提出应从监管生成式AI和其他颠覆性技术，转向建设溯源（provenance）、校准（calibration）和问责（accountability）的基础设施，使可信的学术研究成为默认状态，并总结出三项重大挑战。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁学术界围绕AI时代科学传播的演变展开更广泛的对话，并探索新的研究路径，而非仅停留在政策层面的AI使用披露监管。

Abstract: Generative AI has infiltrated every stage of the research lifecycle: how scholarship is conducted, written, published, and reviewed. Recent policy responses, such as ACM's authorship policy, address an immediate concern about responsible and transparent disclosure of AI use. We argue that a focus on authorship and disclosure, although necessary, risks obscuring and ballooning a set of entrenched problems and strains within publication systems. The central question is not about how papers and other research artifacts should incorporate AI, but how scientific communication itself should evolve when all relevant parties (authors, reviewers, readers) may rely on AI assistance. We draw on our experience within these and other roles to illustrate two contrasting but feasible visions of 2036 with four entwined questions, namely about the purpose of papers as artifacts, reviews, human reviewers, and the incentives that bind all of them. We argue for a shift from policing GenAI and other disruptive technologies to building the infrastructure of provenance, calibration, and accountability that would make trustworthy scholarship the default. We conclude with three grand challenges and invite the community to a broader conversation and research pathways.

</details>


### [276] [Vibe Compiler: A Research-Logic Synthesis Tool That Runs without Prompt Engineering -Toward Enhancing Metacognition for Sustaining Agency in the Age of Generative AI-](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05545)
*Riichiro Mizoguchi,Tomoki Aburatani,Kento Koike,Machi Shimmei*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出合成-分析互惠模型（Synthesis-Analysis Reciprocity Model）并在此基础上构建了Vibe Compiler研究逻辑编译器，帮助研究人员将模糊想法转化为连贯的研究逻辑，同时通过反思性问题保持人类认知能动性，防止不加批判地接受AI生成的内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI虽加速了智力工作，但可能导致人类不加批判地接受AI生成的推理，侵蚀人类的认知能动性（epistemic agency），亟需在AI辅助智力工作中通过增强元认知来保持人类能动性的机制。

Method: 提出合成-分析互惠模型，将智力构建视为合成（组合组件成作品）与分析（依据客观指标批判性评估并约束后续合成）之间的互惠交互。基于该模型构建Vibe Compiler，利用包含十六个学术参数的研究论文本体来编译模糊想法，编译失败即标记逻辑缺失组件，系统不自主填补而是通过反思性问题引导研究者自行发展缺失推理。框架沿认知功能（合成vs分析）和执行主体（人类vs AI）两个维度刻画结构缺口，形成四种来源类型。系统让AI自我审视其合成输出以激发人类元认知。

Result: 基于NotebookLM和Gemini的原型体验表明，有效的AI辅助推理更多取决于提供给AI的知识结构，而非复杂的提示词技巧。本文本身也是使用Vibe Compiler开发的。

Conclusion: 有效的AI辅助推理关键不在于更复杂的提示词，而在于为AI提供良好的知识结构；通过AI自我审视其输出能有效激发人类元认知，使研究者保持管理者的角色，批判性地指导和验证AI生成的推理，而非被动接受。

Abstract: Generative AI used as a capable servant has greatly accelerated intellectual work, but it also risks eroding human epistemic agency by encouraging uncritical acceptance of AI-generated reasoning. This creates a need for mechanisms that preserve human agency by augmenting metacognition during AI-assisted intellectual work. To address this, we propose the Synthesis-Analysis Reciprocity Model, which views intellectual construction as a reciprocal interaction between Synthesis, which combines components into an artifact, and Analysis, which critically evaluates them against objective indicators and constrains subsequent synthesis. Grounded in this model, we present the Vibe Compiler, a research-logic compiler that helps researchers transform vague ideas (Vibes) into coherent research logic. The system compiles these ideas using a research paper ontology of sixteen academic parameters. Compilation failures indicate missing logical components; rather than filling them autonomously, the system prompts researchers with reflective questions that encourage them to develop the missing reasoning. The framework characterizes structural gaps along two dimensions: cognitive function (Synthesis vs. Analysis) and executing agent (human vs. AI), yielding four origin types that identify where breakdowns arise. Our design emphasizes AI probing its own synthesized output to stimulate human metacognition, encouraging researchers to remain managers who critically direct and validate AI-generated reasoning rather than passive recipients.
