Experience with a prototype built on NotebookLM and Gemini suggests that effective AI-assisted reasoning depends less on sophisticated prompting than on the knowledge structure provided to the AI. This paper was developed using the proposed Vibe Compiler.

</details>


### [277] [The Judgment-Consequence Gap: LLM Moral Reasoning in Healthcare Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05583)
*Hadi Hosseini,Samarth Khanna,Leona Pierce*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究探讨大语言模型（LLMs）在医疗资源稀缺情境下的道德推理能力，重点考察模型如何推理责任判断及其后果。研究发现LLMs存在"判断-后果差距"：模型认同患者对有害健康行为负有责任，但拒绝让该判断影响资源分配决策（倾向随机分配），而人类则一致地偏向责任较轻的患者。此外，LLMs更重视信息可及性对责任判断的影响，且随着推理能力提升，与人类的规范分歧反而被放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs进入医疗保健等高风险领域，其在稀缺医疗资源分配中涉及责任判断的道德推理变得至关重要。患者自身行为导致的疾病往往涉及责任归因，理解LLMs如何在这种情境下推理责任及其后果，对确保模型在临床决策中的安全应用具有重要意义。

Method: 研究评估了多个模型家族和不同能力层级的大量LLMs，使用改编自先前研究的多样化临床情景（vignettes），系统地追踪模型在三个连续层面的判断：行为层面、由此导致的疾病层面、以及拒绝治疗的结果层面，并与人类判断进行比较。

Result: 发现了"判断-后果差距"：LLMs与人类在患者对健康危害行为负有责任的判断上基本一致，但在资源分配上LLMs普遍拒绝让责任判断影响决策（默认随机分配），而人类一致偏向可指责性较低的患者。此外，LLMs比人类更看重信息可及性，当健康风险知识不可获取时会降低责任判断。随着模型推理能力提升，与人类的规范分歧反而被放大。

Conclusion: LLMs在责任与资源稀缺交会时采用了与人类系统不同的道德框架，且更强的推理能力不一定带来更接近人类的价值判断，反而可能加剧规范性分歧。这提示在部署LLMs于高 stakes 医疗决策前，需深入审视其潜在的道德推理机制。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) enter high-stakes domains such as healthcare, understanding their moral reasoning becomes essential. Decisions about scarce medical resources often hinge on judgments of responsibility, particularly when patients' own actions contribute to illness. We investigate how LLMs reason about responsibility and its consequences, tracing their judgments across successive levels, from the behavior, to the resulting illness, to the denial of care. We evaluate a wide range of LLMs, spanning different model families and capability levels, on various clinical vignettes adapted from prior studies.
  Our results identify a judgment-consequence gap: LLMs largely agree with humans that patients bear responsibility for health-harming behaviors, yet overwhelmingly refuse to let that judgment influence how they allocate scarce resources. Specifically, LLMs default to random allocation, whereas humans consistently favor the less-culpable patient. Compared to humans, LLMs also place greater emphasis on access to information, reducing responsibility judgments when health-risk knowledge is unavailable. These findings reveal that LLMs apply a systematically different moral framework than humans when responsibility and resource scarcity intersect, surprisingly often amplifying normative disagreement with humans as reasoning capability increases.

</details>


### [278] [Studying People to Study AI: Expert Perspectives on the Epistemic Fit and Barriers of Human Research in AI Safety & Ethics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05656)
*Jessica Y. Bo,Paula Akemi Aoyagui,Shalaleh Rismani,Dipto Das,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过专家调查（n=93）和专家访谈（n=17），探讨AI安全与伦理（AISE）领域中人类实证研究被边缘化的原因，发现其受制于效度质疑、资源壁垒、方法偏好及基础设施约束，并提出提升人类研究认知契合度的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全风险评估目前主要依赖技术方法（如模型基准测试和LLM模拟），而基于人类受试者的实证研究被边缘化。作者希望探究这一差距背后的原因，理解为何人类研究在AISE领域接受度较低。

Method: 采用混合研究方法：(1) 对93位来自技术、社会技术、治理和规范四个背景的AI安全与伦理研究者进行专家调查；(2) 对其中17位进行深度专家访谈，以揭示人类研究在AISE领域采用与接受度受限的原因。

Result: 研究发现虽然普遍认可人类研究对AISE证据生成的价值，但其采用受限于感知效度问题、有形资源障碍、认识论与方法论上的个人偏好，以及更广泛研究社群的基础设施约束。尤其值得注意的是，技术背景的研究者更不重视人类研究且跨学科协作更少，反映出对人类方法存在认识论张力。

Conclusion: 作者提出建议以确立人类研究在AISE中的认识论契合度，并弥合研究者面临的限制性障碍，同时避免流于形式的"人类洗涤"（human-washing）现象。

Abstract: Safety risks of AI are becoming increasingly evident in human interactions with AI technologies. The prominent approaches to evaluating these risks favor technical methods, such as model benchmarks and LLM simulations, often sidelining empirical research with human subjects. To examine this apparent gap in the acceptance of human research, we conduct an expert survey (n=93) and expert interviews (n=17) with AI Safety & Ethics (AISE) researchers from Technical, Sociotechnical, Governance, and Normative backgrounds. Our findings suggest that although there is a consensus that human research is valuable for generating evidence for AISE, its adoption and acceptance are constrained by perceived validity issues, tangible resource barriers, epistemic and personal preferences in methods, and infrastructural constraints from the broader research community. In particular, Technical researchers tend to value human research less and collaborate across disciplines less, suggesting an epistemic tension towards human methods. We propose recommendations for establishing the epistemic fit of human research within AISE and bridging the prohibitive limitations that researchers face, while avoiding performative 'human-washing'.

</details>


### [279] [Validity, Reliability, and Transparency in Artificial Intelligence Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05800)
*A. Mukundan,Debayan Gupta,Subhashis Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文主张现代AI系统在数据收集和处理合法的情况下，仍可能产生不可靠或不合理的推断，构成现有数据保护框架未能充分涵盖的根本性风险。作者从批判性数据研究和测量理论出发，提炼出可操作化的监管框架，主张应将推断有效性作为比例性评估和部署审批的前提条件，并以印度最高法院Puttaswamy判决中的信息自决宪法原则为依据，将其从数据收集扩展到使用合法性的层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据保护框架主要针对隐私相关危害（如数据泄露、重新识别、画像），但未能充分捕捉AI系统产生的更根本性风险——即使数据收集和处理合法，AI的推断本身可能不可靠或不合理。AI带来了构建效度、混杂、代表性、分布漂移和公平性权衡等独特问题，需要专门的监管关注。

Method: 作者首先诊断了AI在认识论层面的局限性（借鉴批判性数据研究和测量理论），然后从中推导出一个结构化且可操作化的监管框架。该框架将推断有效性作为比例性评估和部署审批的前提条件，区别于欧盟AI法案基于领域划分的风险分级方法，并以印度宪法信息自决原则作为理论根基。

Result: 提出了一套包含AI特定有效性评估、部署后监控、以及基于认识论风险和潜在收益结构化阐述的比例性评估的综合治理框架。

Conclusion: 有效治理必须纳入AI特定有效性评估、部署后监控，以及将认识论风险与潜在收益进行结构化阐述的比例性评估。现有框架（包括欧盟AI法案）未做此关键安排，本文填补了这一监管空白。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems increasingly mediate decisions affecting individuals and societies. Existing data protection frameworks address certain privacy-related harms, particularly those arising from data leakage, re-identification, and profiling. However, they inadequately capture a more fundamental risk: unreliable or unjustified inference produced by AI systems even when data collection and processing are legitimate. This article argues that modern AI raises distinct concerns of construct validity, confounding, representativeness, distribution shift, and fairness trade-offs that require specialised regulatory attention. In the context of AI, transparency and explainability acquire distinct and significantly more challenging meanings than in conventional software. A substantial body of work in critical data studies and the measurement-theoretic literature has diagnosed these epistemological limitations. This article's contribution is to derive from that diagnosis a structured and operationalizable regulatory framework. We argue that validity of inference should function as a precondition for proportionality assessment and deployment approval --- a move that existing frameworks, including the EU AI Act's domain-based risk tiers, do not make. We ground this argument in the constitutional principle of informational self-determination articulated in the Indian Supreme Court's \emph{Puttaswamy} judgement, extending its reach from data collection to the legitimacy of use of data. Effective governance must therefore incorporate AI-specific validity assessment, post-deployment monitoring, and proportionality assessments grounded in structured articulation of both epistemic risk and potential benefit.

</details>


### [280] [The Algorithmic Flattening of Sound: Computational Evidence and Justice Implications of AI Music Homogenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06106)
*Zoe Slendebroek,Danaé Metaxa*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对大型生成式音乐系统（Suno 和 Lyria 3）进行了审计，通过 72 个 MIR 特征和多种诊断指标，在四个流派中对比 AI 生成音乐与人类音乐的声学差异。研究发现两种不同的均质化倾向：Lyria 压缩流派内声学多样性，Suno 则坍缩流派间区分度。文章从正义视角论证了这种均质化的伦理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式音乐系统正大规模部署，但其是否导致音乐均质化（homogenization）尚缺乏系统性审计。作者希望在技术上量化这种均质化，并从正义（justice）视角解释其社会影响——即哪些音乐风格会变得可读、被重视并获得经济回报。

Method: 审计两个商业系统（Suno 和 Lyria 3），覆盖四个流派（Afrobeats、K-pop、Dance Pop、Heavy Metal）。每个系统每流派生成 100 首曲目，与等量人类语料对比。使用 72 个 MIR 特征及多种诊断（离散度、冗余度、可分性），仅以流派名称作为提示词以揭示系统的默认音乐倾向。

Result: Lyria 压缩流派内声学多样性；Suno 坍缩流派间声学区分但不压缩流派内离散度。两系统均不能忠实遵循用户提示，表明观测模式反映学习先验而非提示约束。两系统未能收敛到共同声学轮廓，彼此声学距离大于随机人类子样本。但标准分类器可近乎完美地基于 MIR 特征区分 AI 与人类曲目。

Conclusion: 生成式音乐系统的均质化不仅是美学上的好奇心，而是一种与正义相关的条件，会塑造哪些音乐风格在规模化生成输出中被识别、被重视并获得经济回报。这种均质化模式具有结构性、系统依赖性和可检测性。

Abstract: This paper audits whether large-scale generative music systems exhibit measurable musical homogenization relative to human-produced music, and develops a justice-centered account of why this matters. We audit two commercially deployed systems (Suno and Lyria 3) across four genres (Afrobeats, K-pop, Dance Pop, and Heavy Metal). For each system and genre, we generate 100 tracks and compare them against human corpora of equal size, using 72 music information retrieval (MIR) features and multiple diagnostics of dispersion, redundancy, and separability. We define homogenization as reduced acoustic variation in standard computational audio features including rhythm and timing, timbre/spectral shape, and dynamics, both within genres and across genre boundaries. We also generate tracks using only a genre name as the prompt, with no additional instructions, to reveal each system's default musical tendencies. The results show two structurally distinct homogenizing tendencies. Lyria reduces within-genre acoustic diversity, while Suno collapses the acoustic distinctions between genres without compressing within-genre spread. Neither system follows user prompts faithfully, indicating that the observed patterns reflect learned priors rather than prompt constraints. The two systems do not converge on a common acoustic profile and are more acoustically distant from each other than two random human subsamples would typically be. Nevertheless, a standard classifier distinguishes AI from human tracks near-perfectly on MIR features alone. We argue that these patterns matter not as an aesthetic curiosity but as a justice-relevant condition, shaping which musical styles become legible, valued, and economically rewarded as generated outputs increasingly circulate at scale.

</details>


### [281] [What out-of-the-box LLMs can(t) do in law? A Turing test in Italian exams for lawyers, judges and notaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06166)
*Germana Bertoli,Ilaria Amelia Caggiano,Francesca Lagioia,Riccardo Rovatti,Giovanni Sartor,Emiliano Troisi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过盲测图灵实验，评估了主流LLM在意大利三项法律职业考试（律师、法官、公证员）中的表现，发现模型在对抗性法律论证和法理分析上可媲美甚至超越人类顶尖水平，但所有模型均未通过要求目标导向法律规划和严格形式/实质约束的公证员考试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注LLM在法律领域的通用能力，但缺乏对LLM在不同法律专业任务（如论证、分析、规划）上的系统性盲测评估。本文旨在通过真实考试场景的盲测设计，揭示LLM法律能力的边界和任务特异性差异。

Method: 采用盲测图灵实验设计：让主流LLM生成完整的书面考试答卷，将答卷匿名化处理使其与人类考生提交无法区分，由资深考官使用与真实考试相同的评分标准进行评估。实验覆盖三项意大利法律职业考试（律师、法官、公证员），评估多个主流LLM模型。

Result: 结果显示不同模型和任务间表现差异显著。某些LLM在对抗性法律论证和法理分析方面达到或超过人类顶尖水平，但所有模型在公证员考试中均告失败——该考试要求目标导向的法律规划以及严格的正式和实质性约束。研究还识别出任务特异的优势和局限性以及重复出现的法律失败模式。

Conclusion: 尽管仅限开箱即用的系统，研究结果提供了LLM在不同法律专业任务中法律能力的当前范围和边界的定性证据。LLM在论证型任务上表现优异，但在需要约束性规划的高要求任务上存在系统性短板。

Abstract: The article reports on a blind Turing Test experiment, assessing the performance of out-of-the-box leading LLMs on three Italian legal professional exams: the Bar, Judges and Notary exams. Leading LLMs were asked to generate full written exam papers, which were made indistinguishable from human submissions and anonymously evaluated by expert examiners, using the same criteria applied in real examinations. Results reveal marked differences across both models and tasks. While some LLMs match or exceed top human performance in adversarial legal argumentation and doctrinal analysis, all models fail in the notary exam, which requires goal-directed legal planning under strict formal and substantive constraints. Beyond ranking models, the study identifies task-specific strengths, limitations and recurring legal failure patterns. Although limited to out-of-the-box systems, the findings provide qualitative evidence on the current scope and boundaries of the legal competence of LLMs across distinct professional tasks.

</details>


### [282] [From Precision Medicine to Precision Education: A Vision for AI-Powered Student Digital Twins, Preventive Student Success, and Career-Aligned Academic Pathways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06322)
*Kaushik Dutta*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"精准教育"（Precision Education）范式，借鉴医疗领域从被动治疗转向预防性护理的经验，倡导将AI、学习分析、数字孪生等技术应用于高等教育，实现对学生学术、行为、财务和职业数据的持续分析，以识别风险、个性化干预并优化教育路径，核心概念是"学生数字孪生"（Student Digital Twin），并强调从预测走向因果性、可操作干预是核心方法论挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育在学生成功方面仍处于被动反应状态，往往在学生挂科、落后学业进度、累积过重债务或辍学之后才识别问题。作者类比医疗领域数十年前从被动治疗转向预防性护理的成功经验，认为高等教育正处于类似的转折点，亟需变革。

Method: 提出"精准教育"框架：AI持续分析学术、行为、财务和职业数据以识别新兴风险、推荐个性化干预、优化教育路径并协调学业决策与长期职业成功。核心是"学生数字孪生"——一个持续更新的学习者表征，可模拟多种教育未来和干预场景。文章还对比了普渡大学Course Signals和佐治亚州立大学GPS Advising等早期部署案例，并分析了预测到因果干预的方法论挑战、使能技术、伦理治理及研究议程。

Result: 论文提供了概念框架，审视了使能技术，回顾了实证研究记录及其局限，分析了伦理与治理问题，并勾勒出未来十年AI赋能高等教育的研究议程。早期部署案例（如Course Signals和GPS Advising）为该愿景提供了经验基础。

Conclusion: 仅靠预测是不够的，核心方法论挑战在于从预测转向因果性、可操作性的干预。作者主张高等教育应借鉴医学的预防性范式，通过精准教育实现从被动"治疗"到主动"预防"的转变，使AI真正推动学生成功的可衡量改善。

Abstract: Higher education remains largely reactive in its approach to student success. Institutions frequently identify academic problems only after students have failed courses, fallen behind in degree progression, accumulated excessive debt, or departed without a credential. Healthcare faced a similar challenge decades ago. It responded by shifting from reactive treatment to preventive care powered by predictive models, risk stratification, electronic health records, and artificial intelligence (AI). This paper argues that higher education stands at an analogous inflection point. Drawing on advances in learning analytics, educational data mining, machine learning, workforce analytics, and digital twin technologies, we propose a paradigm we call Precision Education. Under this framework, AI continuously analyzes academic, behavioral, financial, and career data to identify emerging risks, recommend personalized interventions, optimize educational pathways, and align academic decisions with long-term career success. Central to the model is the Student Digital Twin, a continuously updated representation of a learner that can simulate multiple educational futures and intervention scenarios. We ground the vision in evidence from early deployments such as Course Signals at Purdue and GPS Advising at Georgia State University. We also argue that prediction alone is insufficient. The central methodological challenge is the move from prediction to causal, actionable intervention. The paper presents a conceptual framework, examines enabling technologies, reviews the empirical record and its limits, analyzes ethical and governance implications, and outlines a research agenda for the next decade of AI-enabled higher education.

</details>


### [283] [Investigating Artificial Intelligence Digital Sovereignty in Mobile Shopping Apps: A Case Study of Nigeria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06364)
*George Grispos,Sajda Qureshi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究通过法医分析与上下文文档分析相结合的方法，考察尼日利亚移动应用中的AI技术如何影响数字主权，以平台透明度作为关键指标。研究发现AI被广泛应用但透明度不足，同时社会经济分析显示尼日利亚对数字平台依赖日益加深、AI认知度中等、交互模式不均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼日利亚电商移动应用迅速扩张带来欺诈和用户控制力下降等问题，引发对数字主权的担忧，但缺乏关于AI如何在移动应用中影响个人数字主权的经验证据。

Method: 采用诠释性研究取向，结合选择性Android应用的法医分析（forensic analysis）与上下文文档分析，识别AI功能并评估披露实践，辅以尼日利亚社会经济分析。

Result: AI在所选应用中广泛实施，但对AI使用的透明度仍然有限；尼日利亚对消费数字平台的依赖日益增加，AI认知水平中等，交互模式不均衡。

Conclusion: 该研究通过提供AI透明度和平台实践的实证证据，增进了对个体数字主权的理解，并揭示了在AI驱动的数字环境中保护用户控制所面临的挑战。

Abstract: The use of e-commerce mobile applications is expanding in Nigeria, creating both opportunities and risks, including fraud and reduced user control over digital technologies, raising concerns about digital sovereignty. This research examines how Artificial Intelligence (AI) in Nigerian mobile applications affects digital sovereignty, examined through platform transparency as a key indicator of user awareness and control. Using an interpretive approach, the research combines the forensic analysis of selected Android applications with contextual document analysis to identify AI features and evaluate disclosure practices. The findings show that AI is widely implemented in the applications, yet transparency about its use remains limited. A socio-economic analysis of Nigeria further shows an increasing dependence on consumer digital platforms, moderate AI awareness, and uneven patterns of interaction. By providing empirical evidence on AI transparency and platform practices, this study advances understanding of individual digital sovereignty and highlights challenges for protecting user control in AI-driven digital environments.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [284] [Contour integral methods and model order reduction for parametric linear control systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05363)
*Serkan Gugercin,Mattia Manucci*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轮廓积分方法（CIM）用于高效计算控制形式参数化线性系统在指定时间区间内的输出，并能达到用户指定的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低参数化线性系统在整个参数域上评估输入-输出关系的计算成本。

Method: CIM通过沿修正积分轮廓应用求积规则来近似逆拉普拉斯变换；针对参数化系统，将CIM与基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）有效结合，利用贪心算法根据推导的误差估计构建投影空间，并证明该投影框架自然满足Hermite插值条件。

Result: 在非参数化和参数化基准控制系统上验证了方法的准确性和高效性，与最先进的基于投影的MOR方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该方法能够大幅降低参数域内评估输入-输出关系的成本，适用于广泛的输入函数以及能被低维子空间良好捕捉的初始条件。

Abstract: This paper introduces a contour integral method (CIM) for efficiently computing outputs of parametric linear systems in control form over specified time intervals and to a user-prescribed accuracy. The CIM approximates the inverse Laplace transform via a quadrature rule applied along a modified integration contour. For parametric systems, we show how CIM integrates effectively with projection-based model order reduction (MOR) where a greedy algorithm builds the projection spaces following an error estimate we derive for this setting. We additionally demonstrate that the developed projection framework naturally enforces Hermite interpolation conditions. This combination substantially lowers the cost of evaluating the input-output relations across the parameter domain, for a wide range of input functions, and for initial conditions well captured by a low-dimensional subspace.. We demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the approach on benchmark non-parametric and parametric control systems, comparing against state-of-the-art projection-based MOR methods.

</details>


### [285] [An Exact Solution of the Two-Ball Multi-Look Search Game with Three Boxes and Heterogeneous Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05406)
*Igor Kleiner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个异构多球搜索成本博弈问题：藏匿者将两个相同的球分配到三个搜索成本不同的盒子中，搜索者自适应地打开盒子直到找到两个球。论文给出了该零和博弈的精确解，博弈值由三个显式有理函数的最大值给出，并刻画了三种参数区域下的最优藏匿策略（乘积形式分布）和最优搜索策略（通过72棵基础决策树归纳）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典搜索博弈通常假设单目标或同质成本，而现实中搜索目标可能多个且各位置成本不同。本文旨在解决一个具体但非平凡的异构多目标搜索成本博弈，为该类问题提供精确的求解方法，填补该方向的理论空白。

Method: 作者将所有非浪费的确定性搜索策略归约为72棵基础决策树，进一步归纳为42种不同的开箱次数分布。通过解析方法解决第一种参数区域；利用两组精确的搜索者混合策略解决第二种区域，四组解决第三种区域。混合策略在全参数区域的可行性通过精确有理Bernstein基证书验证，并辅以独立实现交叉验证。

Result: 博弈值等于三个显式有理函数的最大值。在三种参数区域下，最优藏匿策略分别为限制在三种、五种或全部六种放置方式上的乘积形式分布。所有结果通过零和线性规划值和154个二进节点的Bernstein验证器独立复核。

Conclusion: 该论文完整解决了两个相同球、三个异构成本盒子的搜索成本博弈，提供了精确的博弈值表达式和最优策略刻画，其方法论（决策树归约+混合策略构造+Bernstein证书）可为更广泛的多目标搜索博弈提供可推广的分析框架。

Abstract: A Hider distributes two identical balls among three boxes whose search costs satisfy $a\ge b\ge c>0$. A Searcher opens boxes adaptively until both balls are found; every opening incurs the corresponding box cost and recovers at most one ball. We give an exact solution of this heterogeneous multi-look search-cost game. The value is the maximum of three explicit rational functions. In the three parameter regimes, an optimal Hider strategy is the product-form distribution restricted respectively to three, five, or all six placements. Every non-wasteful deterministic Searcher policy is payoff-equivalent to one of $72$ elementary decision trees, which induce only $42$ distinct opening-count profiles. An analytic argument settles the first regime. Two exact Searcher mixtures settle the second, and four settle the third. Feasibility of the parameterized mixtures over the full cost region is established by exact rational Bernstein-basis certificates. Independent implementations reproduce the policy set, the zero-sum linear-program values, and all $154$ dyadic nodes examined by the Bernstein verifier.

</details>


### [286] [A Relaxation-Based Decomposition Approach for Solving a Supported-Evacuation Problem in Wildfires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05413)
*Shahryar Moradi,Antoine Sauré,Jonathan Patrick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对野火情境下弱势群体（如医院病人和长期护理居民）的疏散规划问题，构建了一个两阶段随机优化模型，同时优化设施选址、车队规模和车辆路径，并受到严格时间窗约束。为解决NP难问题，作者提出基于Logic Based Benders分解的创新求解方法，结合组合Benders割和基于逻辑的不等式。通过在科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区野火演练的真实数据实验，证明了该方法能生成高质量解，显著优于其他策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火疏散中弱势群体的支援性疏散问题（supported evacuation）研究不足，这类群体对时间窗和资源安排有严格要求，需要同时优化设施选址、车队规模和路径规划，但该问题具有NP难复杂性，亟需高效的求解方法。

Method: 构建两阶段随机优化模型以应对不确定性；采用Logic Based Benders Decomposition框架，引入Combinatorial Benders Cuts和基于逻辑的不等式来加速收敛并处理NP难问题。

Result: 基于Roxborough Park社区野火演练的真实数据开展大量数值实验，结果显示该方法能获得高质量解，在避难场所选址、车辆利用率和整体疏散效率方面均显著优于替代策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Logic Based Benders分解的求解方法能够有效解决弱势群体野火疏散的复杂优化问题，为应急管理部门提供切实可行的决策支持工具。

Abstract: This study addresses the critical yet under researched area of supported evacuation for vulnerable populations during wildfires, such as hospital patients and long term care residents, by developing a two stage stochastic optimization model that optimizes facility location, fleet sizing, and vehicle routing under strict time windows. To overcome the problem NP hard complexity, the authors propose an innovative solution methodology leveraging Logic Based Benders Decomposition, featuring Combinatorial Benders Cuts and logic based inequalities. Extensive numerical experiments and real world data from a community wildfire drill in Roxborough Park, Colorado, demonstrate that the proposed approach yields high quality solutions, significantly improving shelter placement, vehicle utilization, and overall evacuation efficiency compared to alternative policies.

</details>


### [287] [A proximal subgradient method for nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05460)
*Felipe Atenas,Alejandro Jofré,Pedro Pérez-Aros,David Torregrosa-Belén*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种近端随机次梯度方法，用于最小化期望代价（其被积函数可能非光滑且非凸）与下半连续、近端有界函数的和。该方法在每次迭代中用逐步细化的样本均值近似期望代价，并通过Armijo型线搜索选择步长。分析给出了几乎必然收敛性、轨迹收敛性以及KL性质下的多项式收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机优化方法在处理非光滑、非凸问题时存在诸多限制，例如要求正则项（弱）凸、要求随机oracle的方差一致有界等。本文旨在放宽这些假设，构建一个更通用的框架，涵盖平滑损失与凸函数之差等更广泛的问题类别。

Method: 提出的算法结合了近端次梯度更新（近端项处理正则函数）与递增样本均值近似（处理期望代价）。步长通过Armijo型线搜索确定，确保满足充分下降性（允许随机误差）。被积函数满足非光滑、局部化的下降引理变体，该结构假设同时涵盖具有Lipschitz梯度的光滑损失以及此类损失与凸函数之差。

Result: 在样本规模序列仅需非递减且无界（无需指定增长率）的宽松条件下，建立了函数值序列的几乎必然收敛性以及每个聚点的平稳性。借助KL性质，将子序列收敛升级为整个轨迹收敛到单一平稳点。对于指数型KL解奇异函数和多项式增长样本规模，导出了函数值和迭代点序列的显式多项式收敛速率（含对数因子）。

Conclusion: 该框架相比现有方法支持更一般的问题表述：无需正则项的（弱）凸性，也无需随机oracle方差的统一界。即使在光滑情形下，其收敛性保证也是新的。这是对随机非凸非光滑优化领域的重要理论贡献。

Abstract: This work introduces a proximal stochastic subgradient method for minimizing the sum of an expected cost, whose integrand is potentially nonsmooth and nonconvex, and a lower semicontinuous, prox-bounded function. We target a broad class of integrands obeying a nonsmooth, localized variant of the descent lemma in the decision variable, a structural assumption that simultaneously covers smooth losses with Lipschitz gradient and differences of such losses with convex functions. At each iteration the expected cost is replaced by a sample average that is progressively refined, and the proximal-subgradient stepsize is selected by an Armijo-type line search enforcing a sufficient-decrease property up to stochastic errors induced by the sample-based approximation. This framework accommodates substantially more general problem formulations than existing methods, in particular, it requires neither (weak) convexity of the regularizer nor a uniform bound on the variance of the stochastic oracle, and our analysis yields convergence guarantees that are new even in the smooth setting. Specifically, we establish almost sure convergence of the sequence of function values and stationarity of every accumulation point of the trajectories under the relaxed requirement that the sample-size sequence be merely nondecreasing and unbounded, with no prescribed growth rate. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we further upgrade this subsequential guarantee to convergence of the whole trajectory to a single stationary point. Finally, for exponential-type KL desingularizing functions and polynomially growing sample sizes, we derive explicit polynomial convergence rates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the function values and the iterates.

</details>


### [288] [An Inertial Block Proximal Linearized Method with Adaptive Momentum for Nonconvex and Nonsmooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05502)
*Weifeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带两阶段自适应动量的惯性块近端线性化方法（IBPL⁺-TP），用于求解多块非凸非光滑优化问题，该方法在收敛性和数值性能上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多块非凸非光滑优化问题广泛存在于地震前兆异常分析和机器学习等应用中，但现有方法的收敛速度较慢且外推参数设置受限，亟需更高效的求解方法。

Method: 提出IBPL⁺-TP方法，包含三大创新：(1)引入两阶段自适应动量策略来有效更新外推参数；(2)允许使用两个不同的外推点以加速收敛；(3)两个外推点的外推参数相互独立且不受其他参数约束。

Result: 证明该方法保证目标函数单调收敛，序列全局收敛到临界点，并建立收敛速率。在稀疏非负矩阵分解（ℓ₀约束）和稀疏非负CP分解（ℓ₀约束）两个非凸非光滑机器学习问题上，数值实验表明该方法优于多种最先进方法。

Conclusion: IBPL⁺-TP方法通过两阶段自适应动量和双外推点策略，在保持理论收敛性保证的同时显著提升了求解非凸非光滑多块优化问题的效率和性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a class of multiblock nonconvex nonsmooth optimization problems, which covers many applications such as the analysis of pre-earthquake anomalies and machine learning. To solve this class of problems, we propose the inertial block proximal linearized method with two-phase adaptive momentum (IBPL$^+$-TP). Compared to the current methods, our method possesses three main advantages: (1) it introduces a two-phase adaptive momentum strategy to effectively update the extrapolation parameters, (2) it allows using two different extrapolation points to accelerate the convergence, (3) it allows the extrapolation parameters of these two extrapolation points to be independent of and unconstrained by all other parameters. While maintaining the above advantages, we prove that our method ensures the monotonic convergence of the objective function of this class of problems, and we also prove that the sequence generated by our method globally converges to a critical point, as well as establish the convergence rate of our method. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, we apply it to solve two nonconvex and nonsmooth machine learning problems, namely sparse nonnegative matrix factorization with $\ell_0$-constraints and sparse nonnegative CP decomposition with $\ell_0$-constraints. The numerical experimental results on solving these problems show that our method outperforms several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [289] [A Unified Framework for Iterate Convergence of Bregman Proximal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05536)
*He Chen,Jiaming Fan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为Bregman近端方法（BPMs）建立了统一的迭代收敛框架，适用于广泛的核函数和复合目标函数。通过扩展缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）性质并引入依赖核的参数化函数，作者证明了扩展SKŁ性质对所有连续子解析函数成立，并在温和正则条件下验证了标准BPM满足框架假设。此外，基于参数化函数，作者还证明了连续时间BPM（镜像流）在o-minimal可定义目标函数下收敛到驻点，这是首个无需凸性或孤立性假设的镜像流轨迹收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bregman近端方法的迭代收敛问题长期以来悬而未决，尤其对于非凸目标函数。虽然chen2026skl通过缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）性质取得了进展，但仅适用于Shannon熵核和线性约束问题，缺乏统一的收敛理论框架。

Method: 本文通过扩展SKŁ性质并引入核依赖的参数化函数，建立了统一的迭代收敛分析框架。作者证明扩展SKŁ性质对所有连续子解析函数成立（特别是核具有封闭区域时），并验证标准BPM在温和正则条件下满足框架假设。同时利用参数化函数分析连续时间BPM（镜像流）的轨迹收敛性。

Result: 建立了适用于广泛核函数和复合目标函数的BPM统一迭代收敛框架；证明扩展SKŁ性质对所有连续子解析函数成立；证实标准BPM在温和正则条件下满足框架假设；实现了首个对o-minimal可定义目标函数无需凸性或孤立性假设的镜像流轨迹收敛结果。

Conclusion: 离散时间和连续时间的收敛结果共同为BPM提供了统一的轨迹收敛理论，显著拓展了此前仅适用于Shannon熵核和线性约束问题的结果，解决了BPM迭代收敛这一长期开放问题。

Abstract: Iterate convergence of Bregman proximal methods (BPMs) has long remained open, especially for nonconvex objectives. Recently, \citet{chen2026skl} made progress by establishing iterate convergence for a BPM via the so-called scaled Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (SKŁ) property, but only for the Shannon entropy kernel and linearly constrained problems. In this paper, we develop a unified iterate convergence framework that applies to a broad group of kernels and composite objective functions. Our approach extends the analytical tools in \cite{chen2026skl}, in particular the SKŁ property, which plays a central role in ensuring convergence of the generated sequences. By introducing kernel-dependent parameterization functions, we show that the extended SKŁ property holds for all continuous subanalytic functions, particularly when the kernel has a closed domain. We then verify that the assumptions of the framework are satisfied by standard BPMs under mild regularity conditions, thereby establishing their iterate convergence for a wide range of objective functions. Furthermore, based on the parameterization functions, we show that the continuous-time BPM (mirror flow) converges to a stationary point for o-minimal definable objective functions, yielding the first trajectory convergence result for mirror flow without imposing convexity assumptions on the objective function or isolation assumptions on stationary points. Taken together, these discrete- and continuous-time convergence results provide a unified trajectory convergence theory for BPMs.

</details>


### [290] [A Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter for Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05559)
*Lei Ma,Yuzhong Hu,Xiaoming Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出重定域Yau-Yau滤波器（RD-YYF），将固定大小局部窗口中心对准最新状态估计，利用物理信息神经网络离线生成FKE解快照，并通过主成分分析和轻量残差代理实现在线高效密度演化预测，在几何约束目标跟踪任务中优于EKF和PF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau-Yau滤波器将非线性状态估计转化为由前向Kolmogorov方程（FKE）控制的概率密度传播，但应用于目标跟踪时需要在有限计算域上高效近似FKE，这是其实际应用的主要瓶颈。

Method: 离线阶段：利用PINNs生成FKE解快照，通过PCA构建密度演化的低维表示，并训练轻量残差代理网络将初始条件系数和域中心映射到终端解系数。在线阶段：在重定域窗口中用预训练代理预测密度演化，随后进行观测更新和状态估计。

Result: 在两个几何约束目标跟踪实验中，RD-YYF的跟踪误差低于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）和粒子滤波（PF），同时保持高效的每时间步推断。消融实验表明域重定域改善了高概率区域的密度近似并加速了离线PINN收敛。

Conclusion: RD-YYF展现了在非线性目标跟踪中实现高效且准确状态估计的潜力，通过域重定域策略有效平衡了计算效率与密度近似精度。

Abstract: The Yau-Yau filter reformulates nonlinear state estimation as probability-density propagation governed by the Forward Kolmogorov equation (FKE). Applying it to target tracking, however, requires efficient FKE approximation on a finite computational domain. This paper proposes a Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter (RD-YYF), which solves the FKE within a fixed-size local window centered at the latest state estimate. This design concentrates numerical resolution near the dominant posterior density. Offline, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) generate FKE solution snapshots, while principal component analysis constructs a low-dimensional representation of density evolution. A lightweight residual surrogate maps the initial-condition coefficients and domain center to the terminal-solution coefficients. Online, the pretrained surrogate predicts density evolution within the recentered window, followed by observation update and state estimation. Experiments on two geometrically constrained target-tracking examples show that RD-YYF achieves lower tracking errors than the extended Kalman filter (EKF) and particle filter (PF), while retaining efficient per-timestep inference. Ablation results indicate that domain recentering improves density approximation in high-probability regions and accelerates offline PINN convergence. These results demonstrate the potential of RD-YYF for efficient nonlinear target tracking

</details>


### [291] [SDDmiP.jl: A Software Package with a Provably Convergent Benders Algorithm for Multi-Stage Stochastic Mixed-Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05567)
*Akul Bansal,Simge Küçükyavuz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源软件包，实现了多阶段随机整数规划的Benders型分解算法，其具有收敛性保证。该算法集成了多种割族，包括Benders割、强化Benders割、Lagrangian割以及ReLU割（后者为一般混合整数状态变量提供收敛保证）。由于生成这些割的对偶问题常有多重最优解，尽管每个解都能产生有效割，但割的质量差异很大。为此，软件包实现了两种基于对偶问题归一化和正则化的割选择策略来强化割的质量，并引入交替割准则，在有效时使用廉价Benders割，仅在必要时调用昂贵紧割。在四类多阶段随机整数规划基准问题上的计算实验评估了这些方法，并揭示了问题结构对实际性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段随机整数规划的计算求解具有挑战性，虽然Benders型分解算法是常用方法，但一般混合整数状态变量的收敛性缺乏保证。ReLU割虽能提供收敛保证，但其对偶问题存在多重最优解，导致生成的割在逼近子问题成本的质量上差异显著，需要有效的割选择策略来强化割质量并提升算法整体效率。

Method: 开发了一个开源软件包，实现收敛的Benders型分解算法。方法包括：(1) 集成多种割族（Benders割、强化Benders割、Lagrangian割、ReLU割）；(2) 提出两种基于对偶问题归一化和正则化的割选择策略，以从多重最优解中挑选质量更优的割；(3) 引入交替割准则，根据情况在廉价Benders割和昂贵紧割之间智能切换；(4) 在四类多阶段随机整数规划基准问题上进行系统实验评估。

Result: 通过四类多阶段随机整数规划基准问题的计算实验，验证了所提出的割选择策略和交替割准则的性能，并分析了不同问题结构对算法实际性能的影响，为实践中的算法选择提供了指导。

Conclusion: 该开源软件包为多阶段随机整数规划提供了一套实用且具有理论保证的求解工具。通过归一化和正则化的割选择策略以及交替割准则，有效平衡了割质量与计算成本，其性能受问题结构影响，为后续研究和实际应用提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: We present an open-source software package that implements a provably convergent Benders-type decomposition algorithm for multistage stochastic integer programs. In addition to standard cut families, such as Benders, strengthened Benders, and Lagrangian cuts, the algorithm incorporates rectified linear unit (ReLU) cuts, which provide convergence guarantees for general mixed-integer state variables. However, the dual problems used to generate these cuts often admit multiple optimal solutions. Although each solution yields a valid cut that separates the incumbent, the resulting cuts can differ in how well they approximate the subproblem cost. To strengthen these cuts, our package implements and evaluates two cut-selection strategies based on normalization and regularization of the dual problem. We also incorporate an alternating-cut criterion that uses cheaper Benders cuts when they are effective and invokes more expensive tight cuts only when necessary. Computational experiments on four classes of multistage stochastic integer programs benchmark these methods and provide insights on how problem structure affects their practical performance.

</details>


### [292] [The Benefits of an Integrated Approach for Stability-Constrained Power System Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06349)
*Gereon Recht,Benedikt Jahn,Oussama Alaya,Karl-Kiên Cao,Hendrik Lens*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究电力系统规划中综合考虑稳定措施（惯量与电压稳定约束）与发输电及储能扩张的集成方法，发现该集成方法可降低系统成本，且电网构网型电池储能系统的双重用途优于STATCOM等其他稳定措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着逆变器资源（IBR）在电力系统中的渗透率不断提高，需要替代同步发电机对动态电压稳定和惯量响应的贡献。然而传统电力系统规划方法是顺序式的，稳定措施仅在规划后期才被考虑。这种时序分离可能导致次优的规划结果。

Method: 提出一种集成式电力系统规划方法，在发输电和储能系统扩张的同时，通过简化的惯量和电压稳定性约束来同步考虑稳定措施。

Result: 集成式方法能够降低系统成本，且电网构网型电池储能系统（具有双重用途：既提供稳定支撑又供能）相较于静止同步补偿器（STATCOM）等其他稳定措施更具优势。

Conclusion: 集成式规划方法优于顺序式方法，构网型电池储能系统因其双重用途特性是更具经济性的稳定措施选择。

Abstract: Increasing penetration of inverter-based resources in today's power systems requires substitution of the contribution from synchronous generators to dynamic voltage stability and inertial response. However, established approaches for power system planning are sequential, as stabilising measures are only considered at a later stage of the planning process. We investigate the advantages of an integrated approach for power system planning, where stabilising measures are considered simultaneously with the expansion of generation, transmission, and storage systems via simplified stability constraints on inertia and voltage stability. We find that system costs are reduced with the integrated approach and that the dual-use option of grid-forming battery energy storage systems is favoured over other stabilising measures like static synchronous compensators.

</details>


### [293] [Convergence Rates for Variational Inequality Projection Neural Networks with a State-Dependent Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05574)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间投影神经网络求解闭凸集上变分不等式问题，提出了一种既非Hessian生成也非标量的状态相关矩阵度量（其逆定义为投影度量），证明了投影关于自变量和度量的联合正则性，并在谱界条件下将欧氏Lipschitz估计从平方界改进为线性界。对于Lipschitz强单调算子与Lipschitz度量，证明局部指数收敛并给出显式速率和半径；在紧可行集上，通过显式度量变分界获得全局指数收敛。对于二次连续可微的一致正定度量配合强单调线性算子，系统存在周期轨道环带，且逆度量在环带内违反Hessian可积性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间投影神经网络在预条件变分不等式方面的收敛性分析仅覆盖两类度量：Hessian生成的逆度量（可用Bregman距离消除度量导数项）和状态依赖的标量度量。本文希望填补矩阵度量（其逆既非Hessian生成也非标量）这一空白，建立更一般的收敛性理论。

Method: 作者研究正定矩阵依赖于状态的可变度量投影动力系统，其逆矩阵定义投影度量。主要技术贡献包括：(1) 证明投影在其参数和度量上的联合正则性；(2) 在常见谱界条件下，将欧氏Lipschitz估计从平方界改进为界本身；(3) 利用显式度量变分界证明紧集上的全局指数收敛；(4) 针对Lipschitz强单调算子和Lipschitz度量建立局部指数收敛（含显式速率与半径）；(5) 对强单调线性算子配合二次连续可微的一致正定度量，构造周期轨道环带并证明逆度量在环带内违反Hessian可积性。

Result: 数值实验验证了解析公式的正确性，并量化了两种收敛证明中的松弛量（slack）。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了连续时间投影神经网络对变分不等式的收敛性理论，覆盖了此前未被处理的矩阵度量情形，证明了局部与全局指数收敛，并揭示了此类系统可能出现周期轨道环带的动力学行为，表明非Hessian可积度量可产生丰富而可解析刻画的现象。

Abstract: We study continuous-time projection neural networks for variational inequalities on closed convex sets. A positive definite matrix that depends on the state preconditions the operator, and its inverse defines the projection metric. Existing convergence analyses of this flow cover Hessian-generated inverse metrics and state-dependent scalar metrics. In the first case, a Bregman distance eliminates metric-derivative terms. We treat a matrix metric whose inverse is of neither kind. We prove joint regularity of the projection in its argument and metric. Under common spectral bounds, the Euclidean Lipschitz estimate improves from the squared bound to the bound itself. For Lipschitz strongly monotone operators and Lipschitz metrics, we prove local exponential convergence with explicit rate and radius. On compact feasible sets, an explicit metric-variation bound yields global exponential convergence. A twice continuously differentiable, uniformly positive definite metric and a strongly monotone linear operator produce an annulus of periodic orbits. The inverse metric violates Hessian integrability throughout the annulus. Deterministic computations confirm the analytic formulas and quantify slack in both convergence certificates.

</details>


### [294] [An inverse mixed-integer optimization framework for learning interpretable models of expert decision making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05585)
*Anurag Holani,Rishabh Gupta,John Wassick,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种逆优化方法，用于从专家的历史决策中联合学习其偏好（感知成本）和决策规则，从而构建可解释的模型来复现专家决策过程。通过三个案例研究（排班问题、生产规划问题、实际路径规划问题）验证了该方法的通用性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家的决策策略经过多年经验积累，往往包含难以显式表达的隐式偏好和决策规则，这阻碍了知识转移。学习可解释的模型来捕捉专家决策过程对于培训新手、改善人机系统性能以及实现全自主系统至关重要。

Method: 开发了一种逆优化（inverse optimization）框架，能够同时学习决策者的偏好（感知成本）以及支配其决策选择的决策规则，从而构建可解释的决策模型。

Result: 在三个不同领域的案例研究（排班问题、生产规划问题、真实路径规划问题）中，同时建模感知成本和决策规则比仅建模单一因素获得了更好的预测性能，证明了方法的通用性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 同时学习感知成本和决策规则的逆优化框架能够更准确地捕捉和复现专家决策行为，相比传统方法具有更强的灵活性和更优的预测能力，在多个工程应用领域具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Understanding how experts make decisions and being able to transfer that knowledge is important, especially in complex engineering applications. It is highly valuable for training novices, improving the performance of human-machine systems, and potentially enabling fully autonomous systems that perform as well as human experts. However, an expert's decision-making strategy, developed through years of experience, is often not directly accessible, since the implicit preferences and decision rules involved can be difficult to specify explicitly. This has motivated the use of observed decisions made by the expert to learn an interpretable model that captures the expert's decision-making process. In this work, we develop an inverse optimization approach to jointly learn the decision-maker's preferences (or perceived costs) and the decision rules governing their choices. We demonstrate the general applicability of our approach using three case studies that consider a shift assignment problem, a production planning problem, and a real-world routing problem, respectively. Across these case studies, modeling both perceived costs and decision rules leads to better predictions, highlighting the value of the proposed framework and its greater flexibility in capturing and replicating expert decision making.

</details>


### [295] [Fast Gradient Algorithm with Dry-like Friction and Nonmonotone Line Search for Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05653)
*Lien T. Nguyen,Andrew Eberhard,Xinghuo Yu,Chaojie Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于Hilbert空间中可微（可能非凸）函数最小化的快速梯度算法。该算法将凸函数的干摩擦性质扩展为非凸情形下的"类干摩擦"性质，并结合线搜索技术自适应更新参数。算法可退化为多种现有带Hessian阻尼和干摩擦的惯性梯度算法，并通过Moreau包络的自变分性质扩展到弱凸非光滑优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有惯性梯度算法在非凸优化中缺乏统一的收敛性分析框架，且干摩擦性质原本仅适用于凸函数，限制了其更广泛的应用场景。

Method: 1) 将干摩擦性质从凸函数扩展到非凸设置，提出"类干摩擦"性质；2) 采用线搜索技术自适应更新迭代参数；3) 利用Moreau包络的变分性质处理弱凸非光滑优化问题；4) 研究了更广泛KL函数的Moreau包络KL指数。

Result: 根据参数选择，算法可实现子序列收敛到临界点或全序列收敛到目标函数的"近似"临界点；在Merit函数的KL性质下建立全序列收敛到临界点的结论。仿真结果验证了算法的效率和类干摩擦与外推、线搜索技术结合的潜在优势。

Conclusion: 该算法提供了一个统一框架，涵盖多种现有惯性梯度算法，并通过参数灵活性实现更强的收敛保证；同时将收敛性分析推广到弱凸非光滑优化问题，扩展了KL指数理论的应用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a fast gradient algorithm for the problem of minimizing a differentiable (possibly nonconvex) function in Hilbert spaces. We first extend the dry friction property for convex functions to what we call the dry-like friction property in a nonconvex setting, and then employ a line search technique to adaptively update parameters at each iteration. Depending on the choice of parameters, the proposed algorithm exhibits subsequential convergence to a critical point or full sequential convergence to an ``approximate'' critical point of the objective function. We also establish the full sequential convergence to a critical point under the Kurdyka--Lojasiewicz (KL) property of a merit function. Thanks to the parameters' flexibility, our algorithm can reduce to a number of existing inertial gradient algorithms with Hessian damping and dry friction. By exploiting variational properties of the Moreau envelope, the proposed algorithm is adapted to address weakly convex nonsmooth optimization problems. In particular, we extend the result on KL exponent for the Moreau envelope of a convex KL function to a broad class of KL functions that are not necessarily convex nor continuous. Simulation results illustrate the efficiency of our algorithm and demonstrate the potential advantages of combining dry-like friction with extrapolation and line search techniques.

</details>


### [296] [Curvature Residual Geometry in Bregman Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05680)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过最小化Bregman损失拟合线性回归模型时的凸性几何问题。尽管生成势函数φ是强凸的，回归目标关于参数θ可能非凸。作者将Hessian表示为加权Gram矩阵，推导了局部强凸性、光滑性和梯度下降条件线性收敛的简单条件，并对二次-四次势函数给出了精确的标量凸性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的凸分析框架下，生成势函数的凸性并不能保证由Bregman损失诱导的回归目标在参数空间中也是凸的，这造成了一个理论与优化实践之间的鸿沟。理解这种残差依赖曲率与势函数、设计矩阵之间的交互作用，对于保证优化算法的收敛性至关重要。

Method: 作者将Hessian矩阵重写为一个加权Gram矩阵，其权重依赖于势函数的导数与当前残差。基于这一表示，推导出局部强凸性、光滑性及梯度下降条件线性收敛的充分条件。随后针对二次-四次势函数进行了详细分析，导出精确的标量凸性条件，识别负曲率区间，并给出局部和全局的正曲率充分条件。

Result: 数值实验表明，标量凸性条件在某些点上可能失败，但完整Hessian在这些点上仍保持正定。此外，随着四次参数增大，能使梯度下降收敛的步长范围逐渐缩小。

Conclusion: 该研究刻画了反向Bregman回归中残差依赖曲率如何与生成势函数及设计矩阵相互作用，为理解此类非凸优化问题的几何结构提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider linear regression models fitted by minimizing Bregman losses of the form \[ \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^n \left[ φ(y_i)-φ(x_i^\topθ) -φ'(x_i^\topθ) \bigl(y_i-x_i^\topθ\bigr) \right], \] where \(y_i\) is the observed response and \(x_i^\topθ\) is the linear prediction. Even when the generating potential \(φ\) is strongly convex, the resulting regression objective may be nonconvex in \(θ\). This creates a gap between the convexity of the generating potential and the optimization geometry of the fitted model.
  The Hessian can be written as a weighted Gram matrix whose weights depend on the derivatives of the potential and the current residuals. This representation gives simple conditions for local strong convexity, smoothness, and conditional linear convergence of gradient descent.
  For the quadratic--quartic potential, we derive an exact scalar convexity condition, identify the interval of negative curvature, and obtain local and global sufficient conditions for positive curvature. Numerical experiments indicate that these scalar conditions may fail while the full Hessian remains positive definite at the evaluated points. They also indicate that the range of tested gradient-descent step sizes leading to convergence decreases as the quartic parameter grows.
  These results characterize how residual-dependent curvature interacts with the generating potential and the design matrix in reverse Bregman regression.

</details>


### [297] [A Damped Subspace Splitting Algorithm for Constrained Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05682)
*Yuanming Su,Yukuan Hu,Xin Liu,Guanghui Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DASSP的单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法，用于解决约束密度泛函理论（CDFT）中的离散优化问题，这是首个具有严格收敛保证的CDFT算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDFT是描述电子激发态和电荷局域态的重要框架，但其离散优化问题同时包含Stiefel流形约束和非凸二次约束，现有算法要么无法高精度满足二次约束，要么因双循环迭代结构面临收敛问题。

Method: 首先利用旋转不变性并引入非线性子空间对齐约束，推导出子空间分裂重构，将两组约束解耦；基于该重构提出单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法DASSP，每次迭代包含谱最小化步骤、投影梯度步骤和阻尼对偶上升步骤。

Result: 在合成和真实CDFT问题上的数值结果表明，DASSP达到了较高的可行性精度，在保持鲁棒性的同时展现出良好的效率。

Conclusion: 该工作有望为可靠且高效的大规模CDFT应用铺平道路，是首个具有严格收敛保证的CDFT算法。

Abstract: Constrained density functional theory (CDFT) provides a powerful framework for describing electronically excited and charge-localized states, which underlie a broad range of physical and chemical phenomena. However, the discretized optimization problems arising from CDFT calculations remain challenging, owing to the presence of both the Stiefel manifold constraint and additional nonconvex quadratic constraints. Existing algorithms either fail to enforce the quadratic constraints with high accuracy or face convergence issues due to double-loop iterative structures. In this paper, we first derive a subspace-splitting reformulation that decouples the two groups of constraints, by exploiting the inherent rotation invariance and introducing a nonlinear subspace alignment constraint. Based on this reformulation, we propose a single-loop damped alternating direction method of multipliers, called DASSP. To the best of our knowledge, DASSP is the first algorithm for CDFT calculations with rigorous convergence guarantees. Each iteration of DASSP comprises a spectral minimization step, a projected gradient step, and a damped dual ascent step, all of which admit efficient implementations. Numerical results on synthetic and realistic CDFT problems demonstrate that DASSP attains high feasibility accuracy and exhibits favorable efficiency without compromising robustness. We expect that this work will pave the way toward reliable and efficient large-scale CDFT applications.

</details>


### [298] [Quadratic Degree Sequence Optimization and the Critical Roots of a Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05827)
*Frédéric Meunier,Shmuel Onn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了图的度序列优化问题在二次函数情形下的复杂度，引入了图的"临界根"概念，并证明这些临界根定义了区间，在这些区间上，当二次根变化时问题的最优值是凸的分段仿射函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度序列优化问题是一个基础且重要的组合优化问题，它要求在图的所有子图中找到使给定函数（关于顶点度数的函数）和最大化的子图。研究其在二次函数情形下的复杂性，可以深化对该问题结构性质的理解，并为更一般情形提供理论工具。

Method: 论文通过引入"临界根"(critical roots)这一新概念来刻画二次函数度序列优化问题的结构。临界根将参数空间划分为若干区间，使得问题的最优值在这些区间上呈现凸的分段仿射性质，从而可以通过分析临界根来理解问题的复杂度。

Result: 证明了对二次函数情形，随着二次根的变化，最优值函数是凸的分段仿射函数，其分段点由图的临界根决定。这一结果揭示了问题最优值的精细结构特征。

Conclusion: 通过临界根的概念，论文为理解二次度序列优化问题提供了新的视角，建立了最优值函数的结构性质，为后续研究一般情形的复杂度提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The degree sequence optimization problem is to find a subgraph of a given graph which maximizes the sum over all vertices of a given function evaluated at the subgraph degree of that vertex. Here we study this problem and its complexity for quadratic functions. In particular, we introduce the critical roots of a graph, and show they define intervals over which the optimal value of the problem, as the quadratic root varies, is convex piecewise affine.

</details>


### [299] [Robust priority-aware coverage optimization for aerial sensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05873)
*Vanshika Datta,C. Nahak,J. C. Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向空中传感器网络的优先级感知鲁棒覆盖优化框架，在传感器位置不确定条件下最大化加权覆盖率，并设计PAROO算法确定传感器朝向，实验验证其在机场监控场景中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中传感器网络在实际应用中面临传感器位置不确定性的挑战，同时不同监控区域的重要性不同，现有方法未同时兼顾优先级权重和位置扰动的鲁棒性，难以在安全敏感场景中有效分配感知资源。

Method: 构建结合监控约束和RRF（鲁棒性）公式的数学优化模型，提出优先级感知鲁棒朝向优化（PAROO）算法，通过优化传感器朝向最大化加权覆盖目标。

Result: 在机场启发式监控场景中，所提框架能有效将感知资源导向高优先级区域，加权覆盖率优于多种代表性基线方法，展示了在安全敏感环境中的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 该框架在传感器位置不确定条件下实现了优先级感知的鲁棒覆盖优化，具有良好的实际适用性，尤其适用于安检等安全敏感环境。

Abstract: This article presents a priority-aware robust coverage optimization framework for an aerial sensor network under sensor location uncertainty. Each region is assigned a priority weight, and the objective is to maximize the weighted coverage while maintaining robustness against positional perturbations. A mathematical optimization model is developed by incorporating surveillance constraints and an RRF-based robustness formulation into the proposed framework. An efficient priority-aware robust orientation optimization (PAROO) algorithm is then proposed to determine the sensor orientations that maximize the weighted coverage objective. Experimental results on an airport-inspired surveillance scenario demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively directs sensing resources toward high-priority regions and achieves higher weighted coverage than representative baseline approaches, highlighting its practical applicability in security-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [300] [Exact Anchoring and a Dualization-Based Matheuristic for Bi-Level Dual-Defense Network Interdiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05922)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为双层防御-攻击者模型(BDAM)提供了精确解。通过将攻击者-路径项进行下层对偶化，得到等价的单层混合整数规划，可在18个基准配置中全部证明最优性（其中17个在10秒内完成）。同时指出供应规则在平局情况下不适定，并提出平局不变的威胁走廊公式。新提出的MILP-DA算法在大多数配置上优于已发表的混合元启发式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层拦截模型常用元启发式求解，但其解的质量无法评估，因为在大规模测试下无法获得精确最优解。作者之前用混合元启发式求解BDAM，但无法验证解的最优性，需要提供精确解作为基准。

Method: 1) 利用BDAM中攻击者-路径项的主导地位，通过下层对偶化实现精确单层重构（当弧长是防御者二元决策的线性函数时可用）；2) 分析供应规则在最短路径平局情况下的不适定性，提出平局不变的威胁走廊公式；3) 将精确锚点与走廊解码及可行性修复结合，形成MILP-DA算法。

Result: 精确MIP在全部18个三行和五行基准配置上证明最优性（17个在10秒内），并给出15*30网格的强非认证上界。已发表的均值比该前沿低3.55%，且差距随规模扩大。MILP-DA在18个认证配置中赢得13个，在所有18个更大配置上全部获胜，失败均发生在三行网格上。

Conclusion: 平局处理偏差会使目标偏移低于10^(-3)但供应成本偏差达0.83单位，说明该缺陷被目标权重压制而非真正消除。MILP-DA优于已发表的混合元启发式，但三行网格上的失败表明该方法在特定结构下仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Bi-level interdiction models are frequently solved by metaheuristics whose solution quality cannot be assessed, because exact optima are unavailable at the scales tested. We supply them for the bi-level dual-defense attacker model (BDAM), which couples node interdiction, edge destruction and capacitated supply support, and which we previously solved by a hybrid metaheuristic. First, BDAM's dominant attacker-path term admits an exact single-level reformulation by lower-level dualization, a reduction available whenever arc lengths are linear in the defender's binary decisions; the resulting mixed-integer program certifies optimality on all eighteen three-row and five-row configurations of our earlier benchmark, seventeen in under ten seconds, with strong uncertified incumbents out to 15*30 grids. Our published averages sit 3.55% below that frontier, and the gap widens with scale. Second, the supply rule, like any rule priced on a single attacker shortest path, is ill-posed under ties, and our threat-corridor formulation is tie-invariant by construction. The tie-break moves the objective by under 10^(-3) but the realized supply cost by up to 0.83 units, so the defect is suppressed by the objective weight rather than absent. Third, MILP-DA pairs the exact anchor with the corridor decode and a feasibility repair; compared against our published figures with no re-implementation on either side, it wins on thirteen of eighteen certified configurations and on all eighteen larger ones, every loss falling on a three-row grid.

</details>


### [301] [Stabilizer Design for Policy Iteration in Stochastic Linear Quadratic Control: A Spectrum-Assignment Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05953)
*Xinyu Cao,Bing-Chang Wang,Ying Cao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间不定随机线性二次控制问题，提出了一种新颖的谱分配方法来获得初始稳定控制器，从而解决了模型无关策略迭代（PI）的初始化难题。通过利用李雅普诺夫型算子的谱，逐步从稳定辅助系统逼近原始系统，并设计了不依赖初始稳定策略的模型无关算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略迭代（PI）是解决最优控制问题的重要强化学习工具，但其初始化阶段通常需要完整的模型信息来寻找初始稳定控制器，这严重限制了模型无关PI的应用。对于状态和控制依赖乘性噪声的随机系统，稳定性不能由确定性情况下的Hurwitz条件保证，而需要满足包含漂移项和扩散项的李雅普诺夫型不等式，因此相应的模型无关PI初始化问题更具挑战性。

Method: 提出了谱分配方法：利用李雅普诺夫型算子的谱，通过调整累积因子逐步从稳定辅助系统逼近原始系统，从而获得稳定控制增益。进一步结合系统数据与累积因子的调整，设计了一个不依赖初始稳定策略的模型无关算法来实现最优控制。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提出方法的有效性，表明该方法能够在无模型条件下获得初始稳定控制器并实现最优控制。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了连续时间不定随机线性二次控制中模型无关PI的初始化难题，为随机系统中无需初始稳定策略的强化学习控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Policy iteration (PI) is an important reinforcement learning tool for solving optimal control problems which includes an initialization stage, i.e., the search for an initial stabilizing controller. However, the initialization stage typically relies on complete model information, thereby imposing substantial constraints on the initialization of model-free PI. For stochastic systems with multiplicative noise dependent on state and control, the stability is not ensured by Hurwitz conditions as in the deterministic case, but rather by a Lyapunov-type inequality that incorporates both drift and diffusion terms. Therefore, the corresponding model-free PI initialization problem is more challenging. To this end, a novel spectrum assignment method is proposed to obtain an initial stabilizer for PI in continuous-time indefinite stochastic linear quadratic control. With the help of the Lyapunov-type operator's spectrum, the original system is gradually approximated from the stable auxiliary system by adjusting a cumulative factor, thereby obtaining a stabilizing control gain. Furthermore, by leveraging system data and adjusting the cumulative factor, we design a model-free algorithm that does not rely on an initial stabilizing policy and can achieve optimal control. Finally, simulation results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [302] [Distributed Fault Diagnosis in Discrete Event Systems with Transmission Delay Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06035)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分可观测离散事件系统中的分布式故障诊断问题，提出了一种考虑传输延迟影响的分布式可诊断性条件，并通过延迟记录器和新的诊断函数来验证该条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中的分布式/分散式可诊断性条件未考虑代理间通信传输延迟的影响，而在实际系统中传输延迟是不可避免的，因此需要新的方法来验证存在传输延迟时故障是否仍能在有限步内被诊断。

Method: 提出一种扩展的分布式可诊断性条件，该条件将文献中的分散式可诊断性条件推广到存在传输延迟的场景。通过构造一个名为"延迟记录器"(delay recorder)的新颖结构和一个新的诊断函数来验证该可诊断性条件。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的验证方法能够成功地确定故障在该场景下是否可被诊断。

Conclusion: 本文为存在传输延迟的部分可观测离散事件系统提供了一种分布式故障可诊断性的验证方法，填补了该领域在传输延迟影响下诊断能力分析的空白。

Abstract: This note studies the distributed fault diagnosis problem in partially-observed discrete event systems, where the system is monitored by a group of agents to cooperatively diagnose faults within a finite number of steps. The novelty of this work is the creation of a methodology to verify when the faults can be diagnosed even in the presence of transmission delay impairments. To address this scenario, a new distributed diagnosability condition is proposed, which extends decentralized diagnosability conditions proposed in the literature. Such distributed diagnosability condition is then verified via a novel structure named delay recorder and a new diagnosis function. Theoretical analysis shows that the verification method can successfully determine whether the faults can be diagnosed.

</details>


### [303] [New Lower Bounds for Weak Limited Augmented Zarankiewicz Numbers in the $m\times 3$ Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06050)
*Liqun Qi,Yannan Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文确定了弱受限增广Zarankiewicz数 $z_{wL}(m,3)$ 在 $m=5,6,7,8,9$ 时的精确值，均等于 $2m$，并通过具体构造和精确有限搜索分别给出下界与上界。这些结果强有力地支持了猜想 $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$（对所有 $m\ge 5$），并由此推出 BSR$(m,3)\ge 2m$ 的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定弱受限增广Zarankiewicz数的精确值，验证并支持一般猜想 $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$，同时为BSR（二分超图斜Ramsey数）提供下界。

Method: 下界通过显式的弱可容许构造给出；上界（对 $m=7,8,9$）通过对无 $C_4$ 基准图进行精确有限搜索获得。结构分析利用已知小情形结果作为支撑。

Result: 求得 $z_{wL}(5,3)=10,\ z_{wL}(6,3)=12,\ z_{wL}(7,3)=14,\ z_{wL}(8,3)=16,\ z_{wL}(9,3)=18$，即 $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$ 对所有 $5\le m\le 9$ 成立；并推出 BSR$(m,3)\ge 2m$。

Conclusion: 这些新结果与已知小情形共同强烈支持猜想 $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$ 对所有 $m\ge 5$ 成立，并为二分超图斜Ramsey数提供了新的下界。

Abstract: We determine the exact weak limited augmented Zarankiewicz numbers $z_{wL}(m,3)$ for $m=5,6,7,8,9$. We establish \[ z_{wL}(5,3)=10,\quad z_{wL}(6,3)=12,\quad z_{wL}(7,3)=14,\quad z_{wL}(8,3)=16,\quad z_{wL}(9,3)=18. \] Thus $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$ for all $5\le m\le 9$. The lower bounds are given by explicit weak admissible constructions, while the matching upper bounds for $m=7,8,9$ are obtained by exact finite searches over canonical $C_4$-free base graphs. Together with the known small cases, this strongly supports the conjecture that \[ z_{wL}(m,3)=2m \quad \text{for all } m\ge 5. \] Since BSR$(m,n)\ge z_{wL}(m,n)$, these constructions imply \[ {\rm BSR}(m,3)\ge 2m \quad \text{for } m=5,6,7,8,9. \]

</details>


### [304] [On Same-Sample and Independent-Sample Stochastic Extragradient for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06182)
*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了随机外梯度（SEG）方法在单调变分不等式问题（VIPs）上的收敛性，重点关注同样本SEG（S-SEG）与独立样本SEG（I-SEG）的差异。研究发现S-SEG对样本Lipschitz参数敏感，单纯均值Lipschitz和有界方差不足以保证收敛；同时在可能无界的域上建立了两种变体的高概率受限缺口收敛，并证明某些改进不可能实现；最后揭示了S-SEG在非对称双步长下仍可能几乎必然发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外梯度算法是求解VIP的基础算法，其确定性收敛理论已很完善，但随机版本的理解仍不充分。现有分析多聚焦于I-SEG，且假设域紧致或随机算子方差有界；而对性质差异显著的S-SEG研究甚少。本文旨在弥补这些文献空白。

Method: 作者首先通过反例证明S-SEG对样本wise Lipschitz参数敏感——仅靠均值Lipschitz性和有界方差即使在紧集上也无法保证收敛。然后在放宽的假设条件下（允许可能无界域），为每种SEG变体建立了高概率受限缺口收敛理论，并通过构造反例证明某些改进在一般情况下不可能达到。最后，分析了非对称双步长选择对S-SEG的影响，展示其会失效。

Result: (1) 证明了S-SEG收敛依赖于更强的样本wise Lipschitz条件而非仅均值Lipschitz；(2) 在无界域上建立了S-SEG与I-SEG的高概率受限缺口收敛结果，并给出不可改进性下界；(3) 构造了一个随机单调VIP实例，证明S-SEG即使在修正的非对称双步长下仍几乎必然发散。

Conclusion: S-SEG与I-SEG在理论上存在本质差异，S-SEG的收敛条件更为苛刻，某些对I-SEG有效的改进策略（如非对称双步长）无法推广到S-SEG。这为随机外梯度方法的理论边界提供了重要刻画。

Abstract: We study stochastic extragradient (SEG) methods for solving monotone variational inequality problems (VIPs) over a feasible set. Although extragradient is a foundational algorithm for VIPs and its deterministic convergence theory is well developed, its stochastic counterpart remains less understood. Most existing analyses focus on independent-sample SEG (I-SEG) and assume either that the domain is compact or that the variance of the stochastic operator is uniformly bounded. The behavior of same-sample SEG (S-SEG), a natural variant with materially different properties, has received far less attention. In this work, we address these gaps in the literature. We first show that S-SEG is sensitive to samplewise Lipschitz parameters: mean Lipschitzness and bounded variance alone do not ensure convergence, even on a compact set. Then, for possibly unbounded domains, we establish a high-probability restricted-gap convergence for each SEG variant under a relaxed set of assumptions, and show that certain fundamental improvements to these results are impossible in general. Finally, we show that a known asymmetric double step-size selection that guarantees almost sure last-iterate convergence for I-SEG can fail for S-SEG: there exists a stochastic monotone VIP for which S-SEG diverges almost surely even under the modified step-sizes.

</details>


### [305] [Characteristic Sensitivity Ensembles for Inference of Hidden Dynamics from Marginal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06190)
*Qi Wang,Gustaaf Jacobs*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从观测到的边际概率密度函数推断广义常微分方程系统动力学的方法，通过将不可逆或随机过程重构为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流，利用特征线表示和随机梯度下降实现参数推断，无需求解高维Liouville方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际系统中的扩散和其他不可逆过程在低维观测中呈现出随机性和记忆性，传统的推断方法难以处理这类随机或不可逆动力学。作者希望通过将随机过程重构为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流，将不可逆或随机动力学的推断问题转化为从边际观测恢复确定性ODE的问题，从而简化推断过程。

Method: 作者提出了一种随机梯度方法：1）将低维观测的不可逆过程增广到高维状态空间，使其满足双曲Liouville方程；2）利用特征线表示算法，将粒子从初始分布沿特征线传输，避免直接求解高维Liouville方程；3）通过沿特征线的敏感性传播获得欧拉敏感性，并使用交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计量；4）利用该梯度估计量执行随机梯度下降来推断参数。

Result: 四个实验验证了该方法：(1) 恢复通过单模态边际观测的三模态线性系统；(2) 含隐藏模态的非线性Gompertz增长模型；(3) 隐藏模态使单峰边际变为双峰的双稳态系统；(4) 恢复细胞流中粒子的Stokes-Oseen阻力定律。文章还分析了这些设置下的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将不可逆或随机动力学的推断问题转化为确定性ODE的恢复问题，通过特征线表示避免了高维Liouville方程的计算瓶颈，交叉U统计量保证了梯度估计的无偏性，多种实验验证了方法的有效性和收敛性。

Abstract: A framework is developed for the inference of dynamics described by a generalized system of ordinary differential equations. A stochastic gradient method is coined that infers dynamics from observed marginal probability density functions using the joint probability density function of the observable and latent variables. Diffusion and other irreversible processes observed in a low-dimensional state can be recast as deterministic, reversible flows in a sufficiently augmented state space, where the joint density satisfies the hyperbolic Liouville equation. The marginal distribution observed is the projection of these hyperbolic dynamics onto the observed coordinates, with the latent components carrying the randomness and memory. This reframing allows inference for irreversible or stochastic dynamics into the recovery of a deterministic Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) from marginal observations. Instead of solving the high-dimensional Liouville equation for the joint density, the algorithm exploits its characteristic representation. Particles sampled from the initial distribution are transported along characteristic lines. The Eulerian sensitivity with respect to parameters is obtained by sensitivity propagation along the characteristic lines, with a crossed U-statistic producing an unbiased gradient estimator, which enables stochastic gradient descent. Four experiments validate the method: recovery of a three-mode linear system observed through the marginal of a single mode; a nonlinear Gompertz growth model with a hidden mode; a bistable system whose hidden mode turns a unimodal marginal bimodal; and Stokes--Oseen drag law recovery for particles in a cellular flow. Convergence behavior is analyzed across these settings.

</details>


### [306] [Muon on the Stiefel Manifold Admits an Exact Closed-Form Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06218)
*Mikhail Solonko,Molozhavenko Alexander,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化方法在Stiefel流形（正交列矩阵流形）上的应用，发现其更新存在精确闭式解，据此提出Skewon算法，并给出了非凸光滑设定下的一阶收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon作为矩阵感知优化方法在Stiefel流形上的现有扩展依赖于启发式、近似或迭代更新，计算效率各异且缺乏精确解，需要更高效且理论上可靠的方案。

Method: 证明Stiefel流形上的Muon更新具有精确闭式解，基于此推导并实现Skewon算法，用于正交约束优化。

Result: 提出了Skewon算法，具备高效实现，并在光滑非凸设定下建立了其一阶收敛保证。

Conclusion: Skewon为Stiefel流形上的正交约束优化提供了既有精确闭式解又能保证收敛的实用算法，弥补了现有Muon扩展方法的不足。

Abstract: We study Muon, a recently proposed matrix-aware optimization method, in the context of the Stiefel manifold. This manifold consists of matrices with orthonormal columns and is ubiquitous in machine learning and scientific computing. Existing extensions of Muon to this manifold rely on heuristic, approximate, or iterative updates with varying computational efficiency. We show that the corresponding Stiefel Muon update admits an exact closed-form solution and use this result to develop Skewon, a practical algorithm for orthogonality-constrained optimization with an efficient implementation. We further establish first-order convergence guarantees for Skewon in the smooth non-convex setting.

</details>


### [307] [Localized Stabilization of Transport PDEs by Interior Flux Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06249)
*Constantinos Kitsos,Ian Manchester*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有界域上带源项的多维连续性方程的镇定问题，通过局部内部通量反馈实现。反馈通过通量的散度施加，使得误差满足带有局部阻尼的输运方程。在有限时间特征阻尼不等式等几何条件下，证明了误差的L2指数稳定性，并给出了ISS估计及非线性速度场的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维连续性方程在物理和工程中广泛出现（如质量守恒、交通流、多智能体系统等），但其镇定问题因通量反馈的结构约束和几何复杂性而具有挑战性。作者希望设计局部内部通量反馈，在保证物理可实现性（通过通量散度施加）的前提下，实现误差的指数稳定，并给出可验证的几何条件。

Method: 1）将反馈选取为使误差满足带局部阻尼的输运方程；2）提出有限时间特征阻尼不等式（要求特征线在时间窗口内积累均匀阻尼量）作为主要几何条件；3）配合增益条件（局部阻尼与压缩放大的关系）证明L2指数稳定性；4）引入Lyapunov型入口条件处理支撑受限轨迹族的特征阻尼；5）构造加权Lyapunov泛函给出微分Lyapunov判据和ISS估计；6）讨论通量反馈的椭圆右逆实现，并推广到非线性速度场。

Result: 在增益条件下证明了误差的L2指数稳定性；获得加权Lyapunov泛函带来的微分判据及对加性扰动的输入-状态稳定性（ISS）估计；将特征阻尼论证推广到状态依赖的非线性速度场；通过二维例子验证了几何、增益与可实现性条件。

Conclusion: 本文为多维连续性方程提供了基于局部内部通量反馈的镇定框架，核心创新在于将镇定问题转化为几何上的特征阻尼条件与增益条件，并结合Lyapunov方法给出定量稳定性保证和ISS性质，同时考虑了反馈通量的椭圆右逆可实现性及非线性推广。

Abstract: We study stabilization of multidimensional continu- ity equations with source terms on bounded domains by means of localized interior flux feedback. The feedback is prescribed through the divergence of the flux and is chosen so that the error with respect to a reference profile satisfies a transport equation with localized damping. The main geometric condition is a finite-time characteristic damping inequality, requiring relevant characteristics to accumulate a uniform amount of damping over a time horizon. This condition is shown to yield exponential stability in L2 of the error, under a gain condition relating localized damping to compressive amplification of the transport field. Lyapunov-type entrance conditions ensure characteris- tic damping on support-restricted families of trajectories. A weighted Lyapunov functional provides a differential Lyapunov criterion and an input-to-state (ISS) estimate with respect to additive perturbations. We also discuss elliptic right-inverse realizations of the feedback flux and extend the characteristic damping argument to velocity fields depending nonlinearly on the state. A two-dimensional example finally illustrates the geometric, gain, and realization conditions.

</details>


### [308] [Fault Diagnosis and Prognosis in Partially-Observed Discrete Event Systems with Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06295)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散事件系统中观测延迟场景下的故障诊断与预测问题，提出了针对延迟观测的诊断性和可预测性条件，并通过引入延迟观测器和新的验证函数实现对这两个条件的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际系统中，观测数据可能因通信或传感器限制而延迟到达，现有诊断与预测方法未充分考虑这一场景；同时已有的可预测性（prognosability）概念在该场景下过于保守，需要修正。

Method: 提出两个针对延迟观测的诊断与预测条件；修订了现有的可预测性概念以避免保守性；引入延迟观测器（delay observer）和新的验证函数来验证诊断性和可预测性条件。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的验证方法在系统故障诊断与预测中是有效的。

Conclusion: 本文为延迟观测场景下的离散事件系统故障诊断与预测提供了新的条件和有效的验证方法，修正了原有可预测性概念的保守性缺陷。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis and prognosis in discrete event systems are studied in the scenario where the observations are possibly received with delay. To address this scenario, two conditions for diagnosis and prognosis with delayed observations are proposed, where we show that the state-of-the-art notion of prognosability must be revised to avoid conservativeness. Diagnosability and prognosability conditions are then verified by introducing a delay observer and a new verification function. Theoretical analysis indicates the effectiveness of the verification method for fault diagnosis and prognosis in the system.

</details>
